# Effects Library — Stage E3 (Generators, Light Leak, Transitions as Recipes) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Two render generators (gradient, soft noise) and five transition recipes — Растворение, Через цвет, Сдвиг, Световая волна, Шторка — plus Засветка (Light Leak), with transitions anchored to their clip pair, kept consistent by every clip edit, placed by dragging a card onto a cut or by a click, selected and edited on the timeline and in the inspector, reachable from MCP and the AI assistant — while every project saved before E3 opens and looks exactly as it did.

**Architecture:** `kadr-scene` gains `LayerContent::Generator` (a `Gradient` or a value `Noise`, plain data with normative `at()` helpers) and `TransitionOp::Over`; both renderers implement them and stay within ±1 LSB. `kadr-effects` gains a `Compile` enum (clip effect or cut), `Compiled.overlays`, `TransitionCtx` / `TransitionBuild`, the Light Leak and the five cut recipes. `kadr-project`'s `Transition` becomes `{id, recipe, version, params, from, to, duration, extra}` — loaded leniently, old `kind` transitions migrated by content on every load — and `Sequence::reconcile_transitions` keeps transitions on their cuts after every command and at load. The evaluator returns several layers per track (a clip and its overlays, or a transition and its overlay) and builds transitions through the registry. The editor draws, selects and edits transitions (`transitions_ui.rs`), applies transition cards to cuts, and shows a transition block in the inspector; MCP and the AI speak `add_transition` / `set_transition` / `remove_transition`.

**Tech Stack:** Rust 2024 workspace, `serde` / `serde_json`, `rayon` (CPU renderer), Slint 1.18.1 (`apps/editor/ui/*.slint`), `kadr-i18n` JSON catalogs, `png` (golden images), `cargo test` / `cargo clippy` on Windows (Git Bash).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-11-effects-library-design.md` — this plan is stage **E3** only (§0 row E3, §1 transition row, §2 transition fields and migration, §3 `Compiled` overlays/transition, §4 generators and transitions, §5 «Засветка» and «Переходы», §6.1 transitions bullet, §6.2 selected transition, §6.3, §6.4 names, §7 `SetTransition` and MCP, §9, §10 item 3). Draft where cited: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-30-effects-system-design.md` (generators :75-77, transition build :193-194, Light Sweep :304, Light Leak :326). The controller's decisions D1–D11 (`.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-11-effects-e3/decisions.md`) are binding; read them with the spec. Rulings in force: `.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-11-effects-e1/progress.md` (R2: format stays 3; R4/R8: read-only tracks; R10: AI no-ops dropped from plans; R11; R13–R17) and `.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-11-effects-e2/progress.md` (E2-R1 neutral primitives skipped anywhere; E2-R2 the reference calls the normative helpers; E2-R3 `a = 0` → transparent under a running chain; E2-R4 grain cell on the short side; E2-R6 `put_effect` keeps progress). Out of scope (D11): audio (E4), heavy effects (Whip Pan, Zoom Through, blur, glow — GPU), keyframed transition parameters, transitions on audio tracks, a hover preview of transitions on the main preview. Style: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-effects-e2.md`. Line numbers below are as of `6be57cb`; earlier tasks move them — find code by symbol.

## Global Constraints

- **Generators** (D6, refinements 1–3, exact): a `LayerContent::Generator` layer's sample at an output pixel centre is the generator's premultiplied value at that centre's **local** position `l = placement.to_canvas()⁻¹(canvas position)` (local pixels = canvas pixels at the layer's scale 1, origin at the content rectangle's top left; a `Placement::fill` layer's local position is its canvas position). No sampling clamp; the crop limits coverage only; effects, opacity, coverage and blend apply as for `Solid`. **Gradient** — `t` = `(x·cos a + y·sin a − offset) / max(length, 1e-3)` (linear) or `|l − centre| / max(radius, 1e-3)` (radial), NaN → 0, clamped into [0, 1]; usable stops = finite positions clamped into [0, 1], stably sorted by position, straight colours clamped into [0, 1] and premultiplied; `t ≤ first` → first, `t ≥ last` → last, else linear between the two around `t` in premultiplied values (equal positions: the later); no stops → transparent. **Noise** — cell `s` = `cell` (at least 1; non-finite → 1); slices `n = ⌊time⌋` (non-finite time → 0) with seeds `seed ^ fmix64(n)` and `seed ^ fmix64(n + 1)` and weight `w = smoothstep(0, 1, time − n)`; lattice `L(i, j) = lerp(h₀, h₁, w)`, `h = pcg32_hash(slice seed, (j as u32) << 32 | (i as u32)) / (2³² − 1)`; `v(l)` = `smooth_bilerp` of the four corners of cell `(⌊x/s⌋, ⌊y/s⌋)` with smoothstep weights; `m = clamp((v − ½)·contrast + ½, 0, 1) · clamp(amount, 0, 1)`; colour `(r·a·m, g·a·m, b·a·m, a·m)` of the straight colour clamped into [0, 1]. The helpers in `crates/scene/src/primitives.rs` are normative: the reference calls them (E2-R2), the CPU matches within ±1 LSB.
- **Transitions** (D1, D5, spec §1): stored per sequence, anchored to `from` (ends at the cut) and `to` (starts there) on one video track; valid while they meet (`from.timeline_out == to.timeline_in`) and both are enabled. Window `[cut − ⌊d/2⌋, cut − ⌊d/2⌋ + d)` with `d = min(duration, from, to)`; under two frames there is none; the earlier cut wins; window progress is linear `(t − start) / d` as f64, clamped, then the recipe's. A transition the registry cannot run (unknown recipe or version) is a hard cut. One transition per cut. Recipes: `clean_fade {style: clean|film}`, `fade_through_color {color: black|white}`, `smooth_push {direction}`, `light_sweep {direction, color: warm|white|cold}`, `wipe {direction, softness 0..1}`; `left` = the pictures (or the edge) move leftwards, the incoming from the right.
- **Saved semantics never change**: `FORMAT_VERSION` stays 3 (R2); `kadr_core::hash` and `Curve` keep their pinned values; an old `kind` transition renders exactly as before (`cross_dissolve` → `clean_fade {style: clean}` = `Dissolve`, `dip_to_black` → `fade_through_color {color: black}` = `DipToColor(Rgba::BLACK)`, `wipe` → `wipe {direction: left, softness: 0}` = `Wipe { angle: π, softness: 0 }`, linear progress, the same window) — pinned by golden pixels blessed before the model changes; the old fields `kind`, `track`, `at` are never written again. Migration never panics on malformed input (`get_mut(..).and_then(as_*_mut)`, never `IndexMut`); a malformed transition is dropped with a log line; an unknown recipe, version, parameter or field survives load → save verbatim.
- **Consistency** (D2): every command that changes clips keeps transitions on their cuts **in the same undo entry** — `EditCommand::apply` runs `Sequence::reconcile_transitions` once per top-level command (a `Batch` included) after it succeeded; loading reconciles too.
- **Light Leak** (D7): two `Screen` generator overlays (noise, then the radial glow) directly above the clip layer, on the clip's placement and crop, opacity × the clip layer's opacity, ids `LayerId::derived(effect id, n)` (never the clip's); strength 0, switched off, unknown recipe → no overlay at all; «До» (bypass) shows none.
- CPU / reference parity: every byte within ±1 LSB (one more at a pixel where the reference stored a value within 1e-3 LSB of a rounding tie). A neutral primitive is skipped wherever it stands (E2-R1); under a running chain a sample with `a = 0` becomes transparent (E2-R3). Golden PNGs within ±1 LSB, (re)written only with `KADR_UPDATE_GOLDEN=1 cargo test -p <crate> --test <file>`, and every rewritten PNG is looked at (Read tool) before it is committed.
- `kadr-render` depends on `kadr-scene` and `kadr-core` only; `kadr-effects` and `kadr-project` are its **dev**-dependencies (golden images), never normal ones.
- All time is integer flicks (`kadr_core::Time`); f64 only for a fraction (progress, positions); durations a user sets are whole frames (`FrameRate::snap`), at least two.
- `EditEngine` records an undo entry for **every** successful command: commands return `EditError::NoOp` when nothing changes and callers check "nothing would change" before sending; one gesture = one entry (a slider drag commits once on release through `LiveEdit`); history labels in the catalogs' noun style («Переход: Шторка», «Изменение перехода: Шторка», «Удаление перехода»).
- Slint: interactive repeaters keep stable models (`util::sync_rows_by_key`, `util::reuse_rows`, `inspector::same_or_new`; never a `for` over an array literal that depends on changing state); icons are `Icon` paths (viewbox 24), never ◀ ▶-style glyphs; in a `PopupWindow` handler call the callback **before** `popup.close()`; a fixed-height row in a layout gets `vertical-stretch: 0`; a struct reaches Rust only if exported from `app.slint`.
- Every user-facing string is in both `crates/i18n/locales/ru.json` and `en.json` (RU + EN); Slint `Tr.t("key")`, Rust `kadr_i18n::{t, tf}`; seconds via `util::fmt_secs`; new entries go before the closing `}` of each file (comma after the previous last line). `crates/i18n/tests/sources.rs` and `effects_ui::tests::every_library_string_exists_in_both_catalogs` enforce it.
- MCP house style: unknown fields rejected per op, millisecond arguments integers within ±10 000 000 000, "no change" rejected one op at a time (`validate_strict`) and dropped inside an AI plan (R10), every rejection names its reason; `EDIT_OPS` / `REFUSED_OPS` stay in step with kadr-mcp's description (`edit_ops_match_the_kadr_mcp_description`, `every_ai_command_is_an_edit_op_or_refused`).
- Tests: `cargo test --workspace --exclude kadr-mcp` and `cargo test -p kadr-mcp`; `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets` without new warnings in touched code. A headless Kadr may lock `target\debug\kadr.exe`: a build that fails only at replacing it is not a code failure. Use your own `CARGO_TARGET_DIR` if another build holds the default one.
- Work on branch `feature/effects`. Commits end with the line `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`. Never print or commit API keys. The owner's media in `C:\Users\Boris\Desktop\000-Brazil` is read-only (never modified, never hard-coded in committed tests). Live acceptance is the controller's (Task 10) through `kadr-mcp`; implementers do not run the app or kadr MCP tools.

## Spec refinements (decided here; report them to the owner)

1. **Generators draw in local layer pixels** (D6 says "canvas pixels of the output pixel centre"): a generator is evaluated at the output pixel centre's position in its layer's local pixels — canvas pixels at the layer's scale 1. For a full-canvas generator (the Light Sweep band) that *is* the canvas position; for Light Leak it makes the leak lie on the clip's picture and move with a picture-in-picture (D7), which canvas positions would not.
2. **Gradient shape**: `Linear { angle, offset, length }` (the spec's "angle + offset" needs a length to scale `t`) and `Radial { centre, radius }`, both in local pixels; stops interpolate in premultiplied values (a transparent stop never tints its neighbour).
3. **Noise** is a 2D value noise on a lattice of `cell` local pixels with smoothstep-bilinear weights, animated by blending two `time` slices (D6); the CPU reads lattice values from a per-layer table (the "low resolution" lattice) and hashes only points outside it — the same numbers.
4. **`TransitionOp::Over`** (`out = to + from·(1 − to.a)`): Сдвиг needs both pictures fully, edge to edge, not a mix; the push's seam is moved to a whole output pixel (`TransitionCtx.output`) so no line of the track below shows through it.
5. **Side shifts are canvas fractions, not `PlacementDelta`s** (D5 named `from/to: PlacementDelta`): a delta's offset is in the picture's own fractions under its scale and rotation, so a PiP or turned side would slide by the wrong length or diagonally; a canvas shift moves the whole side (with its overlays) by one canvas length.
6. **Recipe shape**: `RecipeDef.compile` is an enum `Compile::{Clip(fn), Cut(fn)}`; `RecipeDef::compile_clip` / `compile_cut`. `Compiled` gains `overlays: Vec<Overlay>` (`Overlay { generator, blend, opacity }`, geometry in the picture's local pixels via `CompileCtx::picture()`); the spec's `Compiled.transition` is the separate `TransitionBuild`. Clip recipes' functions are untouched.
7. **Overlay opacity** = the recipe's opacity × the clip layer's opacity (its opacity and fades), so a fading clip takes its leak with it.
8. **Light Leak**: options `color: warm|pink|cold`, `variant: "1"|"2"|"3"` (top-left, top-right, bottom-left corner); at `k = 1` a radial glow (radius 0.85 × the picture diagonal, opacity 0.55) and noise (cell 0.45 × the picture's short side, contrast 1.6, opacity 0.4), the glow drifting 20 % of the picture over the clip; values are tuned with the owner at acceptance (constants in `crates/effects/src/leak.rs` only).
9. **Transition numbers**: «Плёнка» lifts exposure by `0.35 · sin(π·p)` EV on both sides; Шторка's softness 1 = a band of 25 % of the canvas diagonal (default 0.25); Световая волна dissolves on `EaseInOut` under a band 35 % of the canvas extent long at opacity 0.85, entirely off the canvas at both ends; Сдвиг's motion is `Cinematic`; directions default to `left`; a new transition is 0.5 s on whole frames.
10. **Consistency rule** (D2): a transition stays while `to` is on a video track and the clip now ending where `to` starts is `from` or a piece `from` was split into (each piece continuing the one before: same media, next timeline and source time) — then it is re-anchored to that piece; otherwise it is removed. A disabled clip keeps its transition (it draws nothing while either clip is off). An insert at the cut breaks the pair.
11. **Card click target** (D8): of the inspected video clip's two edges, those where a neighbour meets it, the one nearest the playhead — not any cut on the track (which could be far from the selected clip). Applying a card over a cut that has a transition keeps its duration (a card changes the kind, not the timing); the same transition again sends nothing.
12. **Timeline**: a transition is a pill over its clamped window (at least 16 px) in the clip row's middle band — below the fade-handle strip, above the keyframe strip — named when 56 px or wider; hit-testing order: fade handles, keyframe diamonds, transition, trim edges, clip body (trim edges stay reachable above and below the pill). Right-click selects it; the lane menu's «Удалить» removes it.
13. **Choices in the inspector**: icon segments when every option has an icon (directions, zoom), text segments (`Segmented`) for up to three named options (Стиль, Цвет, Вариант), a list otherwise and for a recipe's variants.
14. **«До»** drops the clips' effects (Light Leak included) and every layer's colour primitives — a «Плёнка» lift too; a transition keeps its mix, motion and sweep band.
15. **`get_state`** lists transitions with `{id, recipe, version, params, from, to, track_id, at_ms, duration_ms, known}` and the selected one as `selected_transition`; MCP `select` takes `transition`.
16. **Light Leak on a picture with alpha** (the logo PNG): its overlays lie on the picture's whole rectangle, transparent parts included, where Screen brightens what is below — E3 has no primitive that clips a layer to another layer's alpha. Shown to the owner at acceptance (Task 10, item 8); a matte, if wanted, is later work.
17. **The inspector's «Длительность»** shows the length as drawn (clamped to the shorter clip, «Не длиннее …» under it); committing that shown value is no change. Length and numbers go live into the transition while dragged and are recorded once on release (`LiveEdit` holds a transition as it holds a clip).

## Review Focus

1. **A project saved before E3 with the three old transition kinds** — it opens, every frame of every transition is byte-identical to the E2 picture, and saving writes recipes (never `kind`), with a transition that sat on no cut gone (tests: Task 3 `legacy_transitions_render_as_before`, `old_transition_kinds_become_recipes_on_their_cut`; rerun in Task 5).
2. **Every clip command keeps transitions on their cuts** — split, delete, ripple delete, delete range, move, trim, speed, insert, multicam cuts and batches leave no transition between clips that do not meet, re-anchor one whose outgoing clip was split, and undo restores the exact sequence (tests: Task 4 `transitions_follow_every_clip_command` — one undo entry per command, undo restores clips and transitions together — and `a_locked_track_keeps_its_transitions`).
3. **A transition card dropped near but not on a cut** — within 8 px of a cut on a video row it lands on that cut (the cut highlighted while dragging); on a clip body, a gap, an audio row or below the tracks nothing is applied and the reason shows; a locked track refuses (tests: Task 8 `a_card_lands_on_a_cut_within_reach_and_nowhere_else`, `a_card_click_uses_the_selected_clips_nearest_cut_edge`).
4. **Light Leak on a picture-in-picture or a vertical clip, and «До»** — the leak lies on the clip's own picture (its placement and crop), fades with it, never spills onto the canvas, and «До» shows no leak (tests: Task 5 `a_light_leak_lies_on_the_clips_picture`, `a_light_leak_on_a_vertical_clip_stays_on_its_picture`, `before_view_drops_the_leak`).
5. **Overlays and transitions never break culling or the fast path** — an opaque full-frame clip with a leak still hides the tracks below (not decoded), a clip without effects is still copied by the fast path, and a push leaves no seam (tests: Task 5 `overlays_keep_occlusion_and_the_fast_path`, `a_push_leaves_no_seam`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing primitive tests** — in `crates/scene/src/primitives.rs`, inside `mod tests`, change the import line to `use crate::{ColorAdjust, Effect, LayerId, Rgba, Vec2};` and append:

```rust
    fn stop(pos: f32, r: f32, g: f32, b: f32, a: f32) -> GradientStop {
        GradientStop { pos, colour: Rgba { r, g, b, a } }
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_gradient_maps_its_shape_to_t_and_mixes_premultiplied_stops() {
        let lin = Gradient { shape: GradientShape::Linear { angle: 0.0, offset: 10.0, length: 20.0 }, stops: vec![stop(0.0, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0), stop(1.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 0.5)] };
        assert_eq!(lin.t(5.0, 99.0), 0.0, "before its start: the first stop");
        assert_eq!(lin.t(20.0, -3.0), 0.5, "only the axis counts");
        assert_eq!(lin.t(40.0, 0.0), 1.0);
        let mid = lin.at(20.0, 0.0);
        // lerp((1, 0, 0, 1), (0, 0, 0.5, 0.5), ½): premultiplied, so the blue stop's alpha halves its blue.
        assert!(mid.iter().zip([0.5, 0.0, 0.25, 0.75]).all(|(a, b)| (a - b).abs() < 1e-12), "{mid:?}");
        let down = Gradient { shape: GradientShape::Linear { angle: std::f32::consts::FRAC_PI_2, offset: 0.0, length: 10.0 }, ..lin.clone() };
        assert!((down.t(123.0, 5.0) - 0.5).abs() < 1e-6, "π/2 runs down the y axis (y points down)");
        let radial = Gradient { shape: GradientShape::Radial { centre: Vec2::new(10.0, 10.0), radius: 5.0 }, stops: lin.stops.clone() };
        assert_eq!((radial.t(10.0, 10.0), radial.t(13.0, 14.0), radial.t(30.0, 10.0)), (0.0, 1.0, 1.0));
        let flat = Gradient { shape: GradientShape::Linear { angle: 0.0, offset: 0.0, length: 0.0 }, stops: vec![] };
        assert_eq!(flat.t(0.0005, 0.0), 0.5, "a length under 1e-3 counts as 1e-3");
        assert_eq!(Gradient { shape: GradientShape::Radial { centre: Vec2::new(f32::NAN, 0.0), radius: 1.0 }, stops: vec![] }.t(1.0, 1.0), 0.0, "a NaN t is 0");
    }

    #[test]
    fn gradient_stops_are_cleaned_up_and_a_shared_position_is_a_hard_edge() {
        let g = Gradient {
            shape: GradientShape::Radial { centre: Vec2::new(0.0, 0.0), radius: 1.0 },
            stops: vec![stop(0.75, 0.0, 1.0, 0.0, 1.0), stop(f32::NAN, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), stop(-2.0, 2.0, -1.0, 0.0, 1.5), stop(0.75, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0)],
        };
        let s = g.usable_stops();
        assert_eq!(s, vec![(0.0, [1.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0]), (0.75, [0.0, 1.0, 0.0, 1.0]), (0.75, [0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0])], "NaN dropped, clamped, sorted, equal positions kept in order");
        assert_eq!(stop_colour(&s, 0.75), [0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0], "at a shared position: the later stop");
        assert_eq!(stop_colour(&s, 0.9), [0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0], "past the last: the last");
        let half = stop_colour(&s, 0.375);
        assert!(half.iter().zip([0.5, 0.5, 0.0, 1.0]).all(|(a, b)| (a - b).abs() < 1e-12), "{half:?}");
        assert_eq!(stop_colour(&[], 0.5), [0.0; 4], "no stops: nothing");
    }

    #[test]
    fn noise_is_its_lattice_at_lattice_points_and_smooth_between() {
        let n = Noise { cell: 8.0, seed: 5, time: 0.0, colour: Rgba { r: 1.0, g: 0.5, b: 0.0, a: 1.0 }, amount: 1.0, contrast: 1.0 };
        let h = |seed: u64, i: i64, j: i64| kadr_core::hash::pcg32_hash(seed, Noise::lattice_index(i, j)) as f64 / u32::MAX as f64;
        assert_eq!(n.lattice(3, -2), h(5 ^ kadr_core::hash::fmix64(0), 3, -2), "time 0: slice 0 alone");
        assert_eq!(Noise::lattice_index(-1, 2), (2 << 32) | 0xFFFF_FFFF, "each coordinate taken as 32 bits");
        assert_eq!(n.value(24.0, 16.0), n.lattice(3, 2), "a lattice point is its value");
        let (a, b) = (n.lattice(3, 2), n.lattice(4, 2));
        assert!((n.value(28.0, 16.0) - (a + b) / 2.0).abs() < 1e-12, "half way between two lattice points");
        assert!((n.value(31.999, 20.0) - n.value(32.0, 20.0)).abs() < 1e-3, "continuous across a cell border");
        for x in 0..64 {
            let d = (n.value(x as f64 + 1.0, 9.5) - n.value(x as f64, 9.5)).abs();
            assert!(d <= 1.5 / 8.0, "x {x}: one pixel moves the value at most the smoothstep's slope per cell, {d}");
        }
        assert!((0..200).all(|x| (0.0..=1.0).contains(&n.value(x as f64 * 1.7, x as f64 * 0.3))));
    }

    #[test]
    fn noise_time_blends_two_slices_and_shade_maps_value_to_colour() {
        let n = Noise { cell: 4.0, seed: 9, time: 1.25, colour: Rgba { r: 1.0, g: 0.5, b: 0.0, a: 0.8 }, amount: 0.5, contrast: 2.0 };
        let (s0, s1, w) = n.slices();
        assert_eq!((s0, s1), (9 ^ kadr_core::hash::fmix64(1), 9 ^ kadr_core::hash::fmix64(2)));
        assert_eq!(w, smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, 0.25));
        let idx = Noise::lattice_index(-1, 4);
        let h = |s: u64| kadr_core::hash::pcg32_hash(s, idx) as f64 / u32::MAX as f64;
        assert!((n.lattice(-1, 4) - (h(s0) + (h(s1) - h(s0)) * w)).abs() < 1e-15);
        assert_eq!(Noise { time: 2.0, ..n }.lattice(-1, 4), h(s1), "a whole time is one slice");
        // m = clamp((0.6 − ½)·2 + ½, 0, 1) · ½ = 0.35 on the straight colour (1, 0.5, 0, 0.8).
        let c = n.shade(0.6);
        let m = 0.35;
        assert!(c.iter().zip([0.8 * m, 0.4 * m, 0.0, 0.8 * m]).all(|(a, b)| (a - b).abs() < 1e-12), "{c:?}");
        assert_eq!(n.shade(0.0)[3], 0.0, "below the contrast's floor: nothing");
        assert_eq!(Noise { cell: f32::NAN, ..n }.cell_size(), 1.0);
        assert_eq!(Noise { cell: 0.2, ..n }.cell_size(), 1.0);
        assert_eq!(Noise { time: f32::NAN, ..n }.slices().2, 0.0, "a non-finite time is 0");
        assert_eq!(Noise { amount: f32::NAN, ..n }.shade(0.9)[3], 0.0, "a non-finite amount is 0");
    }

    #[test]
    fn generators_dispatch_and_derived_layer_ids_differ() {
        let n = Noise { cell: 3.0, seed: 1, time: 0.5, colour: Rgba::BLACK, amount: 1.0, contrast: 1.0 };
        assert_eq!(crate::Generator::Noise(n).at(4.0, 5.0), n.at(4.0, 5.0));
        let base = 0x1234_5678_9abc_def0_0fed_cba9_8765_4321u128;
        let ids: Vec<LayerId> = (0..3).map(|k| LayerId::derived(base, k)).collect();
        assert!(ids.iter().all(|l| l.0 != base), "never the base's own id");
        assert!(ids[0] != ids[1] && ids[1] != ids[2] && ids[0] != ids[2]);
        assert_eq!(LayerId::derived(base, 1), LayerId::derived(base, 1));
    }
```

```rust
// ------------------------------------------------------------ generators (E3)

/// One colour of a [`Gradient`]: where it sits along the gradient (0..1)
/// and its straight colour.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct GradientStop {
    pub pos: f32,
    pub colour: crate::Rgba,
}

/// How a gradient runs in its layer's local pixels.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum GradientShape {
    /// Straight bands across the unit vector `u = (cos angle, sin angle)`
    /// (y down): `t = (dot(p, u) − offset) / length`.
    Linear { angle: f32, offset: f32, length: f32 },
    /// Circles about `centre`: `t = |p − centre| / radius`.
    Radial { centre: crate::Vec2, radius: f32 },
}

/// A smooth colour generator (effects spec §4 «Gradient»): a recipe gives
/// 2–3 stops along a linear or radial shape. Exact rules: crate docs,
/// "Generators".
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Gradient {
    pub shape: GradientShape,
    /// Any list is accepted as given; a renderer uses [`Gradient::usable_stops`].
    pub stops: Vec<GradientStop>,
}

/// A stop as a gradient uses it: its position in [0, 1] and its colour premultiplied.
pub type UsableStop = (f64, [f64; 4]);

impl Gradient {
    /// `t` of local point `(x, y)`, in [0, 1]: the shape's distance over its
    /// length or radius (below 1e-3, or not finite, it counts as 1e-3); a
    /// NaN `t` (a non-finite geometry) is 0.
    pub fn t(&self, x: f64, y: f64) -> f64 {
        let (d, len) = match self.shape {
            GradientShape::Linear { angle, offset, length } => {
                let (s, c) = (angle as f64).sin_cos();
                (x * c + y * s - offset as f64, length as f64)
            }
            GradientShape::Radial { centre, radius } => ((x - centre.x as f64).hypot(y - centre.y as f64), radius as f64),
        };
        let t = d / len.max(1e-3);
        if t.is_nan() { 0.0 } else { t.clamp(0.0, 1.0) }
    }

    /// The stops in use: non-finite positions dropped, the rest clamped
    /// into [0, 1] (−0 counts as 0) and stably sorted by position — stops
    /// sharing one are a hard edge, in the given order; each colour's
    /// straight components clamped into [0, 1] (a non-finite one 0) and
    /// premultiplied.
    pub fn usable_stops(&self) -> Vec<UsableStop> {
        let unit = |v: f32| if v.is_finite() { (v as f64).clamp(0.0, 1.0) } else { 0.0 };
        let mut s: Vec<UsableStop> = self
            .stops
            .iter()
            .filter(|s| s.pos.is_finite())
            .map(|s| {
                let a = unit(s.colour.a);
                (unit(s.pos) + 0.0, [unit(s.colour.r) * a, unit(s.colour.g) * a, unit(s.colour.b) * a, a])
            })
            .collect();
        s.sort_by(|a, b| a.0.total_cmp(&b.0));
        s
    }

    /// The premultiplied colour at local point `(x, y)`.
    pub fn at(&self, x: f64, y: f64) -> [f64; 4] {
        stop_colour(&self.usable_stops(), self.t(x, y))
    }
}

/// The colour of usable `stops` at `t`: transparent without stops; at or
/// before the first the first's, at or after the last the last's; else
/// linear, in premultiplied values, between the last stop at or before `t`
/// and the next one (of stops sharing a position the later counts).
pub fn stop_colour(stops: &[UsableStop], t: f64) -> [f64; 4] {
    let (Some(first), Some(last)) = (stops.first(), stops.last()) else { return [0.0; 4] };
    if t <= first.0 {
        return first.1;
    }
    if t >= last.0 {
        return last.1;
    }
    // first.0 < t < last.0: 1 ≤ k ≤ len − 1, and stops[k].0 > t ≥ stops[k − 1].0.
    let k = stops.partition_point(|s| s.0 <= t);
    let (a, b) = (stops[k - 1], stops[k]);
    let f = (t - a.0) / (b.0 - a.0);
    std::array::from_fn(|c| a.1[c] + (b.1[c] - a.1[c]) * f)
}

/// Soft value noise (effects spec §4 «Noise»: the light leak's texture): a
/// lattice every `cell` local pixels, smoothstep-bilinear between its
/// points, two time slices blended. Exact rules: crate docs, "Generators".
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Noise {
    /// Lattice spacing in local pixels (at least 1).
    pub cell: f32,
    pub seed: u64,
    /// Slices `⌊time⌋` and the next one, blended by the fraction.
    pub time: f32,
    /// Straight colour at full value.
    pub colour: crate::Rgba,
    /// 0..1: the coverage at full value.
    pub amount: f32,
    /// Around ½: 1 keeps the values, more makes harder patches.
    pub contrast: f32,
}

impl Noise {
    /// The lattice spacing: `cell`, but at least 1 (below 1 or not finite: 1).
    pub fn cell_size(&self) -> f64 {
        let s = self.cell as f64;
        if s.is_finite() && s >= 1.0 { s } else { 1.0 }
    }

    /// The two slices' seeds and the second's weight: with `n = ⌊time⌋`
    /// (a non-finite time counts as 0), `seed ^ fmix64(n)`,
    /// `seed ^ fmix64(n + 1)` and `smoothstep(0, 1, time − n)`.
    pub fn slices(&self) -> (u64, u64, f64) {
        let time = if self.time.is_finite() { self.time as f64 } else { 0.0 };
        let n = time.floor();
        let slice = |k: f64| self.seed ^ kadr_core::hash::fmix64(k as i64 as u64);
        (slice(n), slice(n + 1.0), smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, time - n))
    }

    /// The hash index of lattice point `(i, j)`: `j << 32 | i`, each taken
    /// as 32 bits (as [`Grain::cell_index`]).
    #[inline]
    pub fn lattice_index(i: i64, j: i64) -> u64 {
        ((j as u32 as u64) << 32) | i as u32 as u64
    }

    /// The value of lattice point `(i, j)`, in [0, 1]: `lerp(h₀, h₁, w)`
    /// with `h = pcg32_hash(slice seed, lattice_index) / (2³² − 1)`.
    pub fn lattice(&self, i: i64, j: i64) -> f64 {
        let (s0, s1, w) = self.slices();
        let idx = Self::lattice_index(i, j);
        let h = |s: u64| kadr_core::hash::pcg32_hash(s, idx) as f64 / u32::MAX as f64;
        let (a, b) = (h(s0), h(s1));
        a + (b - a) * w
    }

    /// The noise at local point `(x, y)`, in [0, 1]: the cell `(i, j) =
    /// (⌊x/s⌋, ⌊y/s⌋)`, its four corners blended by [`smooth_bilerp`] at
    /// `(x/s − i, y/s − j)`. A non-finite coordinate counts as 0.
    pub fn value(&self, x: f64, y: f64) -> f64 {
        let s = self.cell_size();
        let finite = |v: f64| if v.is_finite() { v } else { 0.0 };
        let (gx, gy) = (finite(x / s), finite(y / s));
        let (fi, fj) = (gx.floor(), gy.floor());
        let (i, j) = (fi as i64, fj as i64);
        let (i1, j1) = (i.wrapping_add(1), j.wrapping_add(1));
        smooth_bilerp([self.lattice(i, j), self.lattice(i1, j), self.lattice(i, j1), self.lattice(i1, j1)], gx - fi, gy - fj)
    }

    /// Value `v` as a premultiplied colour: `m = clamp((v − ½)·contrast + ½,
    /// 0, 1) · clamp(amount, 0, 1)` (a non-finite contrast counts as 1, a
    /// non-finite amount as 0) times the straight colour clamped into [0, 1]:
    /// `(r·a·m, g·a·m, b·a·m, a·m)`.
    pub fn shade(&self, v: f64) -> [f64; 4] {
        let unit = |c: f32| if c.is_finite() { (c as f64).clamp(0.0, 1.0) } else { 0.0 };
        let contrast = if self.contrast.is_finite() { self.contrast as f64 } else { 1.0 };
        let shaped = ((v - 0.5) * contrast + 0.5).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
        let m = if shaped.is_nan() { 0.0 } else { shaped } * unit(self.amount);
        let a = unit(self.colour.a) * m;
        [unit(self.colour.r) * a, unit(self.colour.g) * a, unit(self.colour.b) * a, a]
    }

    /// The premultiplied colour at local point `(x, y)`.
    pub fn at(&self, x: f64, y: f64) -> [f64; 4] {
        self.shade(self.value(x, y))
    }
}

/// `lerp(lerp(l00, l10, wx), lerp(l01, l11, wx), wy)` with
/// `wx = smoothstep(0, 1, fx)`, `wy = smoothstep(0, 1, fy)`; `l = [l00, l10, l01, l11]`.
#[inline]
pub fn smooth_bilerp(l: [f64; 4], fx: f64, fy: f64) -> f64 {
    let (wx, wy) = (smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, fx), smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, fy));
    let top = l[0] + (l[1] - l[0]) * wx;
    let bottom = l[2] + (l[3] - l[2]) * wx;
    top + (bottom - top) * wy
}
```

```rust
impl LayerId {
    /// The `n`-th layer made for what `base` names — a clip effect's
    /// overlays, a transition's band: `base` with its top bit flipped, xor
    /// `n` — never `base` itself, one id per `n`.
    pub fn derived(base: u128, n: u32) -> LayerId {
        LayerId(base ^ (1 << 127) ^ n as u128)
    }
}
```

```rust
/// A layer that makes its own picture (effects spec §4 «генераторы»):
/// plain data; exact formulas in the crate docs, "Generators".
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum Generator {
    Gradient(Gradient),
    Noise(Noise),
}

impl Generator {
    /// The premultiplied colour at local point `(x, y)` (the normative helper).
    pub fn at(&self, x: f64, y: f64) -> [f64; 4] {
        match self {
            Generator::Gradient(g) => g.at(x, y),
            Generator::Noise(n) => n.at(x, y),
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The contract** — in `crates/scene/src/lib.rs`: in "Values and buffers", replace `([`LayerContent::Solid`],` with `([`LayerContent::Solid`], a generator's colours,`. After the "Point operations" section's last paragraph (before `//! ## Value domain`) insert:

```rust
//! ## Generators
//!
//! A [`LayerContent::Generator`] layer has no texels: its sample at an
//! output pixel centre is the generator's premultiplied value at that
//! centre's **local** position `l` — canvas → local as in Coordinates
//! (local pixels are canvas pixels at the layer's scale 1, `(0, 0)` the
//! content rectangle's top left; for a `Placement::fill` layer `l` is the
//! canvas position) — [`Generator::at`]`(l)`, normative, computed in f64.
//! There is no sampling clamp (a generator is defined everywhere); the
//! crop limits coverage only. Effects, opacity, coverage and blend apply as
//! for a Solid layer, and a renderer gives a generator layer no input.
//!
//! - **Gradient**: `t` = [`Gradient::t`] — linear `(x·cos a + y·sin a −
//!   offset) / max(length, 1e-3)`, radial `|l − centre| / max(radius,
//!   1e-3)`; NaN → 0; clamped into [0, 1]. The colour is [`stop_colour`] of
//!   [`Gradient::usable_stops`] at `t`: stops with a finite position,
//!   clamped into [0, 1], stably sorted, colours clamped and premultiplied;
//!   the first's before them, the last's after, linear in premultiplied
//!   values between the last stop at or before `t` and the next; none:
//!   transparent.
//! - **Noise**: with `s` = [`Noise::cell_size`], `(i, j) = (⌊x/s⌋, ⌊y/s⌋)`
//!   (a non-finite coordinate counts as 0), `v` = [`smooth_bilerp`] of
//!   [`Noise::lattice`] at the cell's four corners and `(x/s − i, y/s − j)`;
//!   `L(i, j)` blends the slices of [`Noise::slices`] — `⌊time⌋` and the
//!   next, seeds `seed ^ fmix64(n)`, weight `smoothstep(0, 1, fract)` — of
//!   `pcg32_hash(slice seed, `[`Noise::lattice_index`]`) / (2³² − 1)`; the
//!   colour is [`Noise::shade`]`(v)`: `m = clamp((v − ½)·contrast + ½, 0,
//!   1)·clamp(amount, 0, 1)` times the clamped straight colour, premultiplied.
//!   A renderer may read the lattice from a table of its own: the values are
//!   the same numbers.
//!
```

```rust
    fn premul_u8(p: [f64; 4]) -> [u8; 4] {
        p.map(|v| (v.clamp(0.0, 1.0) * 255.0 + 0.5).floor() as u8)
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_linear_gradient_runs_along_its_angle_in_premultiplied_steps() {
        let canvas = SizeU::new(8, 2);
        let g = Gradient { shape: GradientShape::Linear { angle: 0.0, offset: 0.0, length: 8.0 }, stops: vec![GradientStop { pos: 0.0, colour: Rgba::TRANSPARENT }, GradientStop { pos: 1.0, colour: RED }] };
        let l = layer(LayerContent::Generator(Generator::Gradient(g)), Placement::fill(canvas));
        let out = render(&scene((8, 2), (8, 2), Rgba::TRANSPARENT, vec![l]), vec![LayerInput::None]);
        for x in 0..8u32 {
            let v = ((x as f64 + 0.5) / 8.0 * 255.0 + 0.5).floor() as u8;
            assert!(near(px(&out, 8, x, 1), [v, 0, 0, v]), "x {x}: red × t, premultiplied: {:?}", px(&out, 8, x, 1));
        }

    /// A generator's sample is the normative helper at the pixel centre's
    /// local position — also on a scaled, turned layer.
    #[test]
    fn generators_draw_their_normative_values_in_local_pixels() {
        let canvas = SizeU::new(40, 30);
        let pip = Placement { size: Vec2::new(40.0, 30.0), anchor: Vec2::new(0.5, 0.5), position: Vec2::new(20.0, 15.0), scale: Vec2::new(0.5, 0.5), rotation: 0.3 };
        let gens = [
            Generator::Gradient(Gradient {
                shape: GradientShape::Radial { centre: Vec2::new(8.0, 6.0), radius: 30.0 },
                stops: vec![GradientStop { pos: 0.0, colour: RED }, GradientStop { pos: 0.6, colour: Rgba { a: 0.5, ..BLUE } }, GradientStop { pos: 1.0, colour: Rgba::TRANSPARENT }],
            }),
            Generator::Noise(Noise { cell: 7.0, seed: 3, time: 0.4, colour: Rgba { r: 1.0, g: 0.6, b: 0.2, a: 1.0 }, amount: 0.9, contrast: 1.5 }),
        ];
        let mut checked = 0;
        for gen in gens {
            for placement in [Placement::fill(canvas), pip] {
                let l = layer(LayerContent::Generator(gen.clone()), placement);
                let out = render(&scene((40, 30), (40, 30), Rgba::TRANSPARENT, vec![l]), vec![LayerInput::None]);
                let inv = placement.to_canvas().inverse().unwrap();
                for y in (1..30).step_by(4) {
                    for x in (1..40).step_by(5) {
                        let (lx, ly) = inv.apply(x as f64 + 0.5, y as f64 + 0.5);

                            checked += 1;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        assert!(checked > 60, "{checked}");
    }

    /// The renderer reads the noise lattice from a per-layer table and hashes
    /// what the table does not hold: both are the normative lattice.
    #[test]
    fn the_noise_table_and_direct_hashing_agree() {
        let n = Noise { cell: 1.0, seed: 11, time: 2.7, colour: WHITE, amount: 1.0, contrast: 1.0 };
        let canvas = SizeU::new(24, 16);
        let small = layer(LayerContent::Generator(Generator::Noise(n)), Placement::fill(canvas));
        // A crop far past 65 536 lattice cells: no table, every point hashed.
        let mut big = small.clone();
        big.crop = RectF::new(-400.0, -400.0, 424.0, 416.0);
        let a = render(&scene((24, 16), (24, 16), Rgba::TRANSPARENT, vec![big]), vec![LayerInput::None]);
        let b = render(&scene((24, 16), (24, 16), Rgba::TRANSPARENT, vec![small]), vec![LayerInput::None]);
        assert_eq!(a, b);
        assert!(a.chunks(4).any(|p| p[3] > 180) && a.chunks(4).any(|p| p[3] < 80), "varied");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_generator_takes_no_input_and_draws_in_one_pass() {
        let c = SizeU::new(8, 8);
        let g = layer(LayerContent::Generator(Generator::Noise(Noise { cell: 3.0, seed: 1, time: 0.0, colour: WHITE, amount: 0.5, contrast: 1.0 })), Placement::fill(c));
        let s = scene((8, 8), (8, 8), Rgba::BLACK, vec![g]);
        let (_, stats) = render_with(&mut CpuRenderer::new(), &s, vec![LayerInput::None]);
        assert_eq!((stats.passes, stats.layers_drawn, stats.fast_paths), (1, 1, 0));
        let inputs = RenderInputs { layers: vec![LayerInput::Missing(MissingReason::Offline)] };
        let mut out = vec![0u8; 8 * 8 * 4];
        let r = CpuRenderer::new().render(&PreparedFrame { scene: &s, inputs: &inputs }, &mut RenderTarget::Cpu(CpuTarget::packed(8, 8, &mut out)));
        assert!(matches!(r, Err(RenderError::InputMismatch(m)) if m.contains("generator")), "a generator's input is None");
    }

    #[test]
    fn over_puts_the_incoming_side_over_the_outgoing_one() {
        let c = SizeU::new(10, 2);
        let right_half = Placement { size: Vec2::new(5.0, 2.0), anchor: Vec2::new(0.0, 0.0), position: Vec2::new(5.0, 0.0), scale: Vec2::new(1.0, 1.0), rotation: 0.0 };
        let t = transition(TransitionOp::Over, 0.3, c, vec![solid(RED, Placement::fill(c))], vec![solid(Rgba { a: 0.5, ..BLUE }, right_half)]);
        let out = render(&scene((10, 2), (10, 2), Rgba::BLACK, vec![t]), vec![LayerInput::Transition { from: vec![LayerInput::None], to: vec![LayerInput::None] }]);
        assert_eq!(px(&out, 10, 2, 0), [255, 0, 0, 255], "only the outgoing side");
        // to (0, 0, 128, 128) stored; out = to + from·(1 − 128/255).
        assert!(near(px(&out, 10, 7, 0), [127, 0, 128, 255]), "{:?}", px(&out, 10, 7, 0));
    }
```

```rust
/// A gradient, its axis and stops taken once per layer; the per-pixel
/// formula is `Gradient::t` with `stop_colour`, in f64.
struct GradientFx {
    shape: GradShape,
    stops: Vec<UsableStop>,
}

enum GradShape {
    Linear { c: f64, s: f64, offset: f64, len: f64 },
    Radial { cx: f64, cy: f64, r: f64 },
}

impl GradientFx {
    fn new(g: &Gradient) -> GradientFx {
        let shape = match g.shape {
            GradientShape::Linear { angle, offset, length } => {
                let (s, c) = (angle as f64).sin_cos();
                GradShape::Linear { c, s, offset: offset as f64, len: length as f64 }
            }
            GradientShape::Radial { centre, radius } => GradShape::Radial { cx: centre.x as f64, cy: centre.y as f64, r: radius as f64 },
        };
        GradientFx { shape, stops: g.usable_stops() }
    }
}

impl Sampler for GradientFx {
    #[inline(always)]
    fn sample(&self, u: f64, v: f64) -> [f32; 4] {
        let (d, len) = match self.shape {
            GradShape::Linear { c, s, offset, len } => (u * c + v * s - offset, len),
            GradShape::Radial { cx, cy, r } => ((u - cx).hypot(v - cy), r),
        };
        let t = d / len.max(1e-3);
        let t = if t.is_nan() { 0.0 } else { t.clamp(0.0, 1.0) };
        stop_colour(&self.stops, t).map(|x| x as f32)
    }
}

/// `Noise::lattice` from the slices' streams: `pcg32_hash(seed, i)` is
/// `pcg32_at(pcg32_stream(seed), i)`.
#[inline(always)]
fn lattice_value(streams: (u64, u64), w: f64, i: i64, j: i64) -> f64 {
    let idx = Noise::lattice_index(i, j);
    let (a, b) = (pcg32_at(streams.0, idx) as f64 / u32::MAX as f64, pcg32_at(streams.1, idx) as f64 / u32::MAX as f64);
    a + (b - a) * w
}

/// Value noise with the lattice the layer's crop can read (two cells of
/// margin for the coverage band) in a table — the "low resolution" image —
/// and every other point hashed (a crop of more than 65 536 cells has no table).
struct NoiseFx {
    noise: Noise,
    s: f64,
    streams: (u64, u64),
    w: f64,
    i0: i64,
    j0: i64,
    cols: usize,
    rows: usize,
    table: Vec<f64>,
}

impl NoiseFx {
    fn new(n: &Noise, crop: RectF) -> NoiseFx {
        let s = n.cell_size();
        let (s0, s1, w) = n.slices();
        let streams = (pcg32_stream(s0), pcg32_stream(s1));
        let cell = |v: f32| (v as f64 / s).floor() as i64;
        let (i0, j0) = (cell(crop.x0).saturating_sub(2), cell(crop.y0).saturating_sub(2));
        let (i1, j1) = (cell(crop.x1).saturating_add(2), cell(crop.y1).saturating_add(2));
        let span = |a: i64, b: i64| b.saturating_sub(a).saturating_add(1).max(0) as u64;
        let (cols, rows) = (span(i0, i1), span(j0, j1));
        let fits = cols.checked_mul(rows).is_some_and(|c| c <= 1 << 16);
        let (cols, rows) = if fits { (cols as usize, rows as usize) } else { (0, 0) };
        let table = (0..rows).flat_map(|r| (0..cols).map(move |c| (c, r))).map(|(c, r)| lattice_value(streams, w, i0 + c as i64, j0 + r as i64)).collect();
        NoiseFx { noise: *n, s, streams, w, i0, j0, cols, rows, table }
    }

    #[inline(always)]
    fn lattice(&self, i: i64, j: i64) -> f64 {
        let (c, r) = (i.wrapping_sub(self.i0), j.wrapping_sub(self.j0));
        if c >= 0 && r >= 0 && (c as usize) < self.cols && (r as usize) < self.rows {
            self.table[r as usize * self.cols + c as usize]
        } else {
            lattice_value(self.streams, self.w, i, j)
        }
    }
}

impl Sampler for NoiseFx {
    #[inline(always)]
    fn sample(&self, u: f64, v: f64) -> [f32; 4] {
        let finite = |x: f64| if x.is_finite() { x } else { 0.0 };
        let (gx, gy) = (finite(u / self.s), finite(v / self.s));
        let (fi, fj) = (gx.floor(), gy.floor());
        let (i, j) = (fi as i64, fj as i64);
        let (i1, j1) = (i.wrapping_add(1), j.wrapping_add(1));
        let value = smooth_bilerp([self.lattice(i, j), self.lattice(i1, j), self.lattice(i, j1), self.lattice(i1, j1)], gx - fi, gy - fj);
        self.noise.shade(value).map(|x| x as f32)
    }
}

/// A generator layer: its normative colour at every covered pixel's local
/// position (`plan.tex` is `plan.inv` here), then the layer's chain,
/// opacity, coverage and blend — one pass.
fn draw_generator(data: &mut [u8], stride: usize, g: &Grid, layer: &Layer, gen: &Generator, passes: &mut u32) -> Drawn {
    let Some(plan) = Plan::new(g, layer) else { return Drawn::Nothing };
    match gen {
        Generator::Gradient(gr) => draw_sampled(data, stride, &plan, &GradientFx::new(gr)),
        Generator::Noise(n) => draw_sampled(data, stride, &plan, &NoiseFx::new(n, layer.crop)),
    }
    *passes += 1;
    Drawn::Sampled
}
```

```rust
/// What making a generator's colour costs per covered pixel, besides the
/// composite (one sample + blend ≈ 1): a gradient a few multiplies, the
/// noise four lattice reads and two smoothsteps. Checked against the E3
/// bench in Task 10.
fn generator_ops(g: &Generator) -> f64 {
    match g {
        Generator::Gradient(_) => 0.3,
        Generator::Noise(_) => 0.6,
    }
}
```

```rust
#[test]
fn sanity_generators_are_their_helpers_and_over_stacks_the_sides() {
    let cv = (16, 8);
    let g = Generator::Gradient(Gradient {
        shape: GradientShape::Linear { angle: 0.0, offset: 0.0, length: 16.0 },
        stops: vec![GradientStop { pos: 0.0, colour: rgba(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0) }, GradientStop { pos: 1.0, colour: rgba(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0) }],
    });
    let out = reference_of(&scene(cv, cv, rgba(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0), vec![full_layer(cv, LayerContent::Generator(g))]));
    // t = 3.5 / 16 = 0.21875: premultiplied white × t = 55.78 → 56.
    assert_eq!(px(&out, 16, 3, 2), [56, 56, 56, 56]);
    let t = transition(cv, TransitionOp::Over, 0.7, vec![solid(cv, rgba(1.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0))], vec![solid(cv, rgba(0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 0.5))]);
    let out = reference_of(&scene(cv, cv, Rgba::BLACK, vec![t]));
    // to stored (0, 0, 128, 128): 1 − 128/255 = 0.49804 of red → 127.
    assert_eq!(px(&out, 16, 5, 5), [127, 0, 128, 255]);
}
```

```rust
    /// A gradient (linear or radial, 0–3 stops, degenerate lengths now and
    /// then) or a noise (cells from half a pixel to 40, any time, contrast
    /// and an amount past 1).
    fn generator(&mut self) -> Generator {
        let r = &mut self.rng;
        let colour = |r: &mut Rng| Rgba { r: r.f() as f32, g: r.f() as f32, b: r.f() as f32, a: if r.chance(0.3) { 1.0 } else { r.f() as f32 } };
        if r.chance(0.5) {
            let n = r.int(0, 3);
            let stops = (0..n).map(|_| GradientStop { pos: r.range(-0.2, 1.2) as f32, colour: colour(r) }).collect();
            let shape = if r.chance(0.5) {
                GradientShape::Linear { angle: r.range(0.0, 7.0) as f32, offset: r.range(-40.0, 40.0) as f32, length: if r.chance(0.05) { 0.0 } else { r.range(1.0, 120.0) as f32 } }
            } else {
                GradientShape::Radial { centre: Vec2::new(r.range(-20.0, 120.0) as f32, r.range(-20.0, 120.0) as f32), radius: r.range(0.0, 150.0) as f32 }
            };
            Generator::Gradient(Gradient { shape, stops })
        } else {
            Generator::Noise(Noise { cell: [0.5, 1.0, 3.0, 9.0, 40.0][r.int(0, 4) as usize], seed: r.next(), time: r.range(-3.0, 5.0) as f32, colour: colour(r), amount: r.range(0.0, 1.2) as f32, contrast: r.range(0.0, 4.0) as f32 })
        }
    }
```

```rust
/// Generators on full-frame and turned picture-in-picture layers, in
/// Normal and Screen, under a chain, inside a transition's side and behind
/// `Over`, into letterboxed, scaled and pillarboxed outputs.
#[test]
fn cpu_renderer_matches_the_reference_on_generators() {
    let canvas = SizeU::new(48, 27);
    let (cw, ch) = (48.0f32, 27.0f32);
    let gens = [
        Generator::Gradient(Gradient { shape: GradientShape::Linear { angle: 0.7, offset: -5.0, length: 30.0 }, stops: vec![GradientStop { pos: 0.0, colour: rgba(1.0, 0.6, 0.2, 0.0) }, GradientStop { pos: 0.5, colour: rgba(1.0, 0.6, 0.2, 1.0) }, GradientStop { pos: 1.0, colour: rgba(1.0, 0.6, 0.2, 0.0) }] }),
        Generator::Gradient(Gradient { shape: GradientShape::Radial { centre: Vec2::new(4.0, 3.0), radius: 40.0 }, stops: vec![GradientStop { pos: 0.0, colour: rgba(1.0, 0.4, 0.6, 1.0) }, GradientStop { pos: 1.0, colour: rgba(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0) }] }),
        Generator::Noise(Noise { cell: 5.0, seed: 77, time: 1.6, colour: rgba(0.4, 0.7, 1.0, 1.0), amount: 1.0, contrast: 1.6 }),
        Generator::Noise(Noise { cell: 1.0, seed: 3, time: -0.4, colour: rgba(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.7), amount: 0.8, contrast: 3.0 }),
    ];
    let pip = Placement { size: Vec2::new(cw, ch), anchor: Vec2::new(0.5, 0.5), position: Vec2::new(cw * 0.65, ch * 0.4), scale: Vec2::new(0.45, 0.45), rotation: 0.3 };
    let outputs = [(48, 27), (24, 14), (96, 54), (40, 30), (64, 27)];
    let chain = vec![Effect::ColorAdjust(ColorAdjust { exposure: 0.5, saturation: 0.7, ..ColorAdjust::NEUTRAL })];
    let mut renderer = CpuRenderer::new();
    let mut renders = 0;
    for (gi, g) in gens.iter().enumerate() {
        for placement in [Placement::fill(canvas), pip] {
            for (blend, effects) in [(BlendMode::Normal, vec![]), (BlendMode::Screen, vec![]), (BlendMode::Screen, chain.clone())] {
                let gl = Layer { id: LayerId(2), content: LayerContent::Generator(g.clone()), crop: placement.full_crop(), placement, opacity: 0.8, blend, effects };
                let base = solid((48, 27), rgba(0.2, 0.3, 0.25, 1.0));
                let side = |l: Layer| transition((48, 27), TransitionOp::Over, 0.5, vec![solid((48, 27), rgba(0.6, 0.1, 0.1, 1.0))], vec![l]);
                for layers in [vec![base.clone(), gl.clone()], vec![base.clone(), side(gl.clone())]] {
                    for &(ow, oh) in &outputs {
                        let out = SizeU::new(ow, oh);
                        assert!(!has_boundary_tie(canvas, out), "{ow}x{oh}");
                        let scene = FrameScene { time: Time::ZERO, canvas, output: OutputSpec::new(out, RenderQuality::Export), background: Rgba::BLACK, layers: layers.clone() };
                        let inputs = RenderInputs { layers: solid_inputs(&scene.layers) };
                        let (expected, ties) = render_reference_with_ties(&scene, &inputs);
                        let mut actual = vec![0xAAu8; ow as usize * oh as usize * 4];
                        renderer.render(&PreparedFrame { scene: &scene, inputs: &inputs }, &mut RenderTarget::Cpu(CpuTarget::packed(ow, oh, &mut actual))).unwrap();
                        for (at, (e, a)) in expected.iter().zip(&actual).enumerate() {
                            let pix = at / 4;
                            assert!(e.abs_diff(*a) <= 1 + ties[pix].min(1), "generator {gi}, {blend:?}, output {ow}x{oh}: pixel ({}, {}) channel {}: reference {e}, renderer {a}", pix % ow as usize, pix / ow as usize, at % 4);
                        }
                        renders += 1;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    assert_eq!(renders, 4 * 2 * 3 * 2 * 5);
}
```

```rust
fn generator_layer(g: Generator, placement: Placement) -> Layer {
    layer(LayerContent::Generator(g), placement)
}

fn stop(pos: f32, c: Rgba) -> GradientStop {
    GradientStop { pos, colour: c }
}

#[test]
fn gradients() {
    // A warm band across the video in Screen, and a cold radial glow from
    // the top-left corner of a turned picture-in-picture in Screen.
    let warm = rgb(1.0, 0.75, 0.45);
    let band = Gradient { shape: GradientShape::Linear { angle: 0.4, offset: 90.0, length: 110.0 }, stops: vec![stop(0.0, Rgba { a: 0.0, ..warm }), stop(0.5, warm), stop(1.0, Rgba { a: 0.0, ..warm })] };
    let mut band_layer = generator_layer(Generator::Gradient(band), Placement::fill(CANVAS));
    band_layer.blend = BlendMode::Screen;
    let pip = placement((320.0, 180.0), (230.0, 120.0), 0.4, 0.15);
    let cold = rgb(0.5, 0.75, 1.0);
    let glow = Gradient { shape: GradientShape::Radial { centre: Vec2::new(0.0, 0.0), radius: 300.0 }, stops: vec![stop(0.0, cold), stop(0.45, Rgba { a: 0.45, ..cold }), stop(1.0, Rgba { a: 0.0, ..cold })] };
    let mut glow_layer = generator_layer(Generator::Gradient(glow), pip);
    glow_layer.blend = BlendMode::Screen;
    let s = scene(OUT, vec![full(AlphaMode::Opaque), band_layer, media(pip, AlphaMode::Opaque), glow_layer]);
    check("gradients", s, vec![LayerInput::Cpu(video()), LayerInput::None, LayerInput::Cpu(checker(320, 180)), LayerInput::None]);
}

#[test]
fn noise() {
    // Coarse pink noise alone on the left half, the same in Screen over the photo on the right.
    let n = Noise { cell: 40.0, seed: 7, time: 0.6, colour: rgb(1.0, 0.45, 0.62), amount: 1.0, contrast: 1.6 };
    let left = placement((160.0, 180.0), (80.0, 90.0), 1.0, 0.0);
    let right = placement((160.0, 180.0), (240.0, 90.0), 1.0, 0.0);
    let mut over_photo = generator_layer(Generator::Noise(n), right);
    over_photo.blend = BlendMode::Screen;
    let s = scene(OUT, vec![generator_layer(Generator::Noise(n), left), media(right, AlphaMode::Opaque), over_photo]);
    check("noise", s, vec![LayerInput::None, LayerInput::Cpu(photo()), LayerInput::None]);
}

#[test]
fn over_40() {
    // A push 40 % through: the video leaves to the left, the checkerboard follows it in, edge to edge.
    let fill = Placement::fill(CANVAS);
    let from = media(Placement { position: Vec2::new(160.0 - 128.0, 90.0), ..fill }, AlphaMode::Opaque);
    let to = media(Placement { position: Vec2::new(160.0 + 192.0, 90.0), ..fill }, AlphaMode::Opaque);
    let t = transition(TransitionOp::Over, 0.4, vec![from], vec![to]);
    check("over_40", scene(OUT, vec![t]), vec![LayerInput::Transition { from: vec![LayerInput::Cpu(video())], to: vec![LayerInput::Cpu(checker(320, 180))] }]);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing transition tests** — create `crates/effects/src/transition.rs` with only this test module and add `pub mod transition;` and `pub mod leak;` to `crates/effects/src/lib.rs` (after `pub mod look;`), plus `pub use transition::{default_duration, transition_params, transition_seed, TransitionBuild, TransitionCtx};` after `pub use registry::*;`:

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::registry::recipe;
    use kadr_scene::Generator;

    fn ctx(progress: f64) -> TransitionCtx {
        TransitionCtx { progress, canvas: SizeU::new(1920, 1080), output: SizeU::new(1920, 1080), seed: 7 }
    }

    fn choice(v: &str) -> ParamValue {
        ParamValue::Choice(v.into())
    }

    fn build(id: &str, set: &[(&str, ParamValue)], c: &TransitionCtx) -> TransitionBuild {
        let def = recipe(id).unwrap();
        let mut stored = transition_params(def);
        stored.extend(set.iter().map(|(k, v)| (k.to_string(), v.clone())));
        def.compile_cut(&Params::resolve(def.params, &stored, |_| None), c).expect("a cut recipe")
    }

    /// D1, saved semantics: the recipes the old kinds became draw what the
    /// kinds drew — the same op, the same linear progress, nothing else.
    #[test]
    fn the_old_kinds_compile_to_their_old_ops_with_linear_progress() {
        let plain = |b: &TransitionBuild| b.from_shift == (0.0, 0.0) && b.to_shift == (0.0, 0.0) && b.from_effects.is_empty() && b.to_effects.is_empty() && b.overlay.is_none();
        for p in [0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.987_654_321, 1.0] {
            let fade = build("clean_fade", &[("style", choice("clean"))], &ctx(p));
            assert_eq!((fade.op, fade.progress), (TransitionOp::Dissolve, p as f32));
            let dip = build("fade_through_color", &[("color", choice("black"))], &ctx(p));
            assert_eq!((dip.op, dip.progress), (TransitionOp::DipToColor(Rgba::BLACK), p as f32));
            let wipe = build("wipe", &[("direction", choice("left")), ("softness", ParamValue::Number(0.0))], &ctx(p));
            assert_eq!((wipe.op, wipe.progress), (TransitionOp::Wipe { angle: std::f32::consts::PI, softness: 0.0 }, p as f32));
            assert!(plain(&fade) && plain(&dip) && plain(&wipe), "{p}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn film_lifts_both_sides_towards_the_middle_and_white_dips_to_white() {
        let film = |p: f64| build("clean_fade", &[("style", choice("film"))], &ctx(p));
        let lift = |b: &TransitionBuild| match b.from_effects.as_slice() {
            [Effect::ColorAdjust(a)] => a.exposure as f64,
            [] => 0.0,
            other => panic!("{other:?}"),
        };
        let mid = film(0.5);
        assert!((lift(&mid) - FILM_LIFT_EV).abs() < 1e-6 && mid.from_effects == mid.to_effects, "both sides, peaking half way");
        assert!(lift(&film(0.25)) > 0.0 && lift(&film(0.25)) < FILM_LIFT_EV * 0.75);
        for p in [0.0, 1.0] {
            assert!(film(p).from_effects.is_empty() && film(p).to_effects.is_empty(), "no lift at the window's ends: {p}");
        }
        assert_eq!(build("fade_through_color", &[("color", choice("white"))], &ctx(0.3)).op, TransitionOp::DipToColor(Rgba { r: 1.0, g: 1.0, b: 1.0, a: 1.0 }));
    }

    /// Сдвиг: the outgoing picture leaves, the incoming one follows edge to
    /// edge (Cinematic), and the seam lands on a whole output pixel.
    #[test]
    fn a_push_slides_both_sides_one_canvas_and_puts_the_seam_on_a_pixel() {
        for (dir, axis) in [("left", (-1.0, 0.0)), ("right", (1.0, 0.0)), ("up", (0.0, -1.0)), ("down", (0.0, 1.0))] {
            let start = build("smooth_push", &[("direction", choice(dir))], &ctx(0.0));
            assert_eq!((start.op, start.from_shift, start.to_shift), (TransitionOp::Over, (0.0, 0.0), (-axis.0, -axis.1)), "{dir}: the incoming one waits a canvas away");
            let end = build("smooth_push", &[("direction", choice(dir))], &ctx(1.0));
            assert_eq!((end.from_shift, end.to_shift), (axis, (0.0, 0.0)), "{dir}");
            for p in [0.1, 0.37, 0.5, 0.9] {
                let b = build("smooth_push", &[("direction", choice(dir))], &ctx(p));
                assert!(((b.from_shift.0 - b.to_shift.0) - axis.0).abs() < 1e-12 && ((b.from_shift.1 - b.to_shift.1) - axis.1).abs() < 1e-12, "{dir} {p}: edge to edge");
                let moved = b.from_shift.0.abs() + b.from_shift.1.abs();
                assert!((moved - Curve::Cinematic.apply(p)).abs() <= 1.0 / 1080.0, "{dir} {p}: Cinematic, give or take a pixel");
            }
        }
        // 1920 × 1080 into 1000 × 600: k = 0.52083, letterboxed by 18.75 px.
        let c = TransitionCtx { output: SizeU::new(1000, 600), ..ctx(0.43) };
        let k = 1000.0 / 1920.0;
        let left = build("smooth_push", &[("direction", choice("left"))], &c);
        let seam = k * 1920.0 * (1.0 + left.from_shift.0);
        assert!((seam - seam.round()).abs() < 1e-6, "the seam on a whole pixel: {seam}");
        let down = build("smooth_push", &[("direction", choice("down"))], &c);
        let seam_y = k * 1080.0 * down.from_shift.1 + (600.0 - k * 1080.0) / 2.0;
        assert!((seam_y - seam_y.round()).abs() < 1e-6, "{seam_y}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_wipe_runs_in_its_direction_with_a_band_of_its_softness() {
        use std::f32::consts::{FRAC_PI_2, PI};
        let angle = |dir: &str| match build("wipe", &[("direction", choice(dir))], &ctx(0.5)).op {
            TransitionOp::Wipe { angle, .. } => angle,
            other => panic!("{other:?}"),
        };
        assert_eq!((angle("left"), angle("right"), angle("up"), angle("down")), (PI, 0.0, -FRAC_PI_2, FRAC_PI_2));
        let soft = |s: f64| match build("wipe", &[("softness", ParamValue::Number(s))], &ctx(0.5)).op {
            TransitionOp::Wipe { softness, .. } => softness as f64,
            other => panic!("{other:?}"),
        };
        assert_eq!(soft(0.0), 0.0, "a hard edge");
        assert!((soft(1.0) - WIPE_SOFT_DIAGONAL * 1920f64.hypot(1080.0)).abs() < 1e-3);
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_sweep_band_crosses_the_canvas_above_a_dissolve() {
        let band = |p: f64, dir: &str| {
            let b = build("light_sweep", &[("direction", choice(dir))], &ctx(p));
            assert_eq!(b.op, TransitionOp::Dissolve);
            assert!((b.progress as f64 - Curve::EaseInOut.apply(p)).abs() < 1e-6);
            let o = b.overlay.expect("a band");
            assert_eq!((o.blend, o.opacity), (BlendMode::Screen, SWEEP_OPACITY as f32));
            let Generator::Gradient(g) = o.generator else { panic!("a gradient") };
            g
        };
        let points = [(0.0, 0.0), (1920.0, 0.0), (0.0, 1080.0), (1920.0, 1080.0), (960.0, 540.0)];
        for dir in ["left", "right", "up", "down"] {
            for p in [0.0, 1.0] {
                let g = band(p, dir);
                assert!(points.iter().all(|&(x, y)| g.at(x, y)[3] < 1e-9), "{dir} at {p}: off the canvas");
            }
            let g = band(0.5, dir);
            assert!((g.t(960.0, 540.0) - 0.5).abs() < 1e-3 && (g.at(960.0, 540.0)[3] - 1.0).abs() < 1e-3, "{dir}: its peak crosses the centre half way");
        }
        assert!(band(0.3, "right").t(960.0, 540.0) > 0.5, "moving right: at 0.3 the peak is still left of the centre");
        let white = band(0.5, "right");
        let b = build("light_sweep", &[("color", choice("white"))], &ctx(0.5));
        let Some(Overlay { generator: Generator::Gradient(g), .. }) = b.overlay else { panic!() };
        assert_eq!(g.stops[1].colour, Rgba { r: 1.0, g: 1.0, b: 1.0, a: 1.0 });
        assert_ne!(white.stops[1].colour, g.stops[1].colour, "the default is warm");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_cut_recipe_s_defaults_and_a_new_transition_s_length() {
        assert_eq!(transition_params(recipe("light_sweep").unwrap()), BTreeMap::from([("direction".to_string(), choice("left")), ("color".to_string(), choice("warm"))]));
        assert_eq!(transition_params(recipe("wipe").unwrap())["softness"], ParamValue::Number(0.25));
        assert_eq!(default_duration(FrameRate::FPS_30), Time::from_millis(500));
        assert_eq!(default_duration(FrameRate::FPS_29_97), FrameRate::FPS_29_97.frame_to_time(15), "0.5 s on whole frames");
        let id = TransitionId::new();
        assert_eq!(transition_seed(id), transition_seed(id));
        assert_ne!(transition_seed(id), transition_seed(TransitionId::new()));
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing Light Leak tests** — create `crates/effects/src/leak.rs` with only:

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::registry::recipe;
    use kadr_core::Time;
    use kadr_project::ParamValue;
    use kadr_scene::{GradientShape, SizeU};

    fn ctx(progress: f64, aspect: f64) -> CompileCtx {
        CompileCtx { progress, aspect, ..CompileCtx::still(SizeU::new(1920, 1080), Time::ZERO, 99) }
    }

    fn leak(set: &[(&str, ParamValue)], c: &CompileCtx) -> Vec<Overlay> {
        let def = recipe("light_leak").unwrap();
        let stored = set.iter().map(|(k, v)| (k.to_string(), v.clone())).collect();
        let out = def.compile_clip(&Params::resolve(def.params, &stored, |_| None), c);
        assert!(out.placement.is_identity() && out.effects.is_empty(), "a leak only adds light");
        out.overlays
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_light_leak_is_noise_and_a_glow_in_screen_on_the_picture() {
        let o = leak(&[], &ctx(0.0, 16.0 / 9.0));
        assert_eq!(o.len(), 2);
        assert!(o.iter().all(|o| o.blend == BlendMode::Screen));
        assert_eq!((o[0].opacity, o[1].opacity), (NOISE_OPACITY as f32, GLOW_OPACITY as f32), "«Обычно»: the leak as designed");
        let Generator::Noise(n) = &o[0].generator else { panic!("noise first") };
        assert_eq!((n.cell, n.colour), ((NOISE_CELL * 1080.0) as f32, leak_colour("warm")));
        let Generator::Gradient(g) = &o[1].generator else { panic!("then the glow") };
        assert_eq!(g.shape, GradientShape::Radial { centre: Vec2::new(0.0, 0.0), radius: (GLOW_RADIUS * 1920f64.hypot(1080.0)) as f32 }, "variant 1: the top-left corner at progress 0");
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_leak_lies_on_the_picture_of_the_clip_s_aspect() {
        let c = CompileCtx::still(SizeU::new(1920, 1080), Time::ZERO, 1);
        assert_eq!(c.picture(), (1920.0, 1080.0));
        assert_eq!(CompileCtx { aspect: 9.0 / 16.0, ..c }.picture(), (607.5, 1080.0), "a vertical clip, pillarboxed");
        assert_eq!(CompileCtx { aspect: 4.0, ..c }.picture(), (1920.0, 480.0), "a wide one, letterboxed");
        assert_eq!(CompileCtx { aspect: f64::NAN, ..c }.picture(), (1920.0, 1080.0), "no usable aspect: the canvas's");
        let o = leak(&[("variant", ParamValue::Choice("2".into()))], &ctx(1.0, 9.0 / 16.0));
        let Generator::Gradient(g) = &o[1].generator else { panic!() };
        let GradientShape::Radial { centre, .. } = g.shape else { panic!() };
        assert!((0.0..=607.5).contains(&centre.x) && (0.0..=1080.0).contains(&centre.y), "on the vertical picture: {centre:?}");
        let Generator::Noise(n) = &o[0].generator else { panic!() };
        assert_eq!(n.cell, (NOISE_CELL * 607.5) as f32, "the picture's short side, not the canvas's");
    }

    #[test]
    fn strength_scales_the_leak_and_colours_variants_and_progress_change_it() {
        let at = |s: f64| leak(&[("strength", ParamValue::Number(s))], &ctx(0.5, 16.0 / 9.0));
        assert_eq!(at(0.3)[1].opacity, (GLOW_OPACITY * crate::intensity(0.3)) as f32, "«Мягко» = half");
        assert_eq!(at(1.0)[1].opacity, (GLOW_OPACITY * crate::intensity(1.0)).min(1.0) as f32);
        let colour = |c: &str| match &leak(&[("color", ParamValue::Choice(c.into()))], &ctx(0.5, 16.0 / 9.0))[0].generator {
            Generator::Noise(n) => n.colour,
            _ => panic!(),
        };
        assert!(colour("warm") != colour("pink") && colour("pink") != colour("cold"));
        assert!(colour("warm").r > colour("warm").b && colour("cold").b > colour("cold").r);
        let seed = |v: &str| match &leak(&[("variant", ParamValue::Choice(v.into()))], &ctx(0.5, 16.0 / 9.0))[0].generator {
            Generator::Noise(n) => n.seed,
            _ => panic!(),
        };
        assert!(seed("1") != seed("2") && seed("2") != seed("3"), "each variant its own noise");
        assert_ne!(leak(&[], &ctx(0.2, 16.0 / 9.0)), leak(&[], &ctx(0.8, 16.0 / 9.0)), "it drifts with progress");
        assert_eq!(leak(&[], &ctx(0.5, 16.0 / 9.0)), leak(&[], &ctx(0.5, 16.0 / 9.0)), "deterministic");
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The registry** — in `crates/effects/src/registry.rs`:

Imports: add `use crate::leak;` and `use crate::transition::{self, TransitionBuild, TransitionCtx};` after `use crate::motion;`.

Add to `impl CompileCtx` (after `still`):

```rust
    /// The clip's picture in canvas pixels at its scale 1 — its placement's
    /// size: the picture of aspect `aspect` fitted into the canvas (a
    /// non-finite or non-positive aspect counts as the canvas's). An
    /// overlay's geometry is in these local pixels.
    pub fn picture(&self) -> (f64, f64) {
        let (cw, ch) = (self.canvas.w as f64, self.canvas.h.max(1) as f64);
        let a = if self.aspect.is_finite() && self.aspect > 0.0 { self.aspect } else { cw / ch };
        if cw / ch > a { (ch * a, ch) } else { (cw, cw / a) }
    }
```

Replace the `Compiled` doc, struct and impl with:

```rust
/// What one effect adds to a frame of its clip: motion recipes move the
/// picture, colour recipes (Look, Vignette) add primitives after the clip's
/// correction, light recipes (Light Leak) add generator layers over the
/// picture; audio operations (E4) join here. A transition's frame is a
/// [`TransitionBuild`].
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Compiled {
    pub placement: PlacementDelta,
    pub effects: Vec<kadr_scene::Effect>,
    /// Generator layers over the clip's picture, in order (D7): the
    /// evaluator puts each on the clip layer's placement and crop, directly
    /// above it.
    pub overlays: Vec<Overlay>,
}

impl Default for Compiled {
    /// Nothing: the picture stays where it is, unchanged.
    fn default() -> Self {
        Compiled { placement: PlacementDelta::IDENTITY, effects: vec![], overlays: vec![] }
    }
}

impl Compiled {
    pub fn motion(placement: PlacementDelta) -> Compiled {
        Compiled { placement, ..Compiled::default() }
    }

    /// A colour effect's frame: primitives only, the picture stays where it is.
    pub fn colour(effects: Vec<kadr_scene::Effect>) -> Compiled {
        Compiled { effects, ..Compiled::default() }
    }

    /// A light effect's frame: generator layers over the picture.
    pub fn overlays(overlays: Vec<Overlay>) -> Compiled {
        Compiled { overlays, ..Compiled::default() }
    }
}

/// A generator layer an effect draws over a picture (D7, refinement 6):
/// its geometry in the picture's local pixels (`CompileCtx::picture`), its
/// blend, and its opacity before the picture layer's own.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Overlay {
    pub generator: kadr_scene::Generator,
    pub blend: kadr_scene::BlendMode,
    pub opacity: f32,
}

/// How a recipe compiles (refinement 6): a clip effect's frame, or a
/// transition's on a cut.
#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
pub enum Compile {
    Clip(fn(&Params, &CompileCtx) -> Compiled),
    Cut(fn(&Params, &TransitionCtx) -> TransitionBuild),
}
```

In `RecipeDef` replace `pub compile: fn(&Params, &CompileCtx) -> Compiled,` with `pub compile: Compile,`, and add to `impl RecipeDef`:

```rust
    /// A clip effect's frame; nothing for a transition recipe.
    pub fn compile_clip(&self, p: &Params, ctx: &CompileCtx) -> Compiled {
        match self.compile {
            Compile::Clip(f) => f(p, ctx),
            Compile::Cut(_) => Compiled::default(),
        }
    }

    /// A transition's frame; `None` for a clip recipe.
    pub fn compile_cut(&self, p: &Params, ctx: &TransitionCtx) -> Option<TransitionBuild> {
        match self.compile {
            Compile::Cut(f) => Some(f(p, ctx)),
            Compile::Clip(_) => None,
        }
    }
```

```rust
static REGISTRY: LazyLock<[RecipeDef; 13]> = LazyLock::new(|| {
    use Compile::{Clip, Cut};
    [
        RecipeDef { id: "smooth_zoom", category: Category::Motion, target: Target::Clip, version: 1, params: &[ZOOM_DIRECTION, STRENGTH, POINT], variants: None, cost: CostClass::Cheap, compile: Clip(motion::smooth_zoom) },
        RecipeDef { id: "cinematic_push", category: Category::Motion, target: Target::Clip, version: 1, params: &[STRENGTH, POINT], variants: None, cost: CostClass::Cheap, compile: Clip(motion::cinematic_push) },
        RecipeDef { id: "punch_in", category: Category::Motion, target: Target::Clip, version: 1, params: &[STRENGTH, MOMENT, POINT], variants: None, cost: CostClass::Cheap, compile: Clip(motion::punch_in) },
        RecipeDef { id: "smooth_pan", category: Category::Motion, target: Target::Clip, version: 1, params: &[PAN_DIRECTION, STRENGTH], variants: None, cost: CostClass::Cheap, compile: Clip(motion::smooth_pan) },
        RecipeDef { id: "handheld", category: Category::Motion, target: Target::Clip, version: 1, params: &[STRENGTH], variants: None, cost: CostClass::Cheap, compile: Clip(motion::handheld) },
        RecipeDef { id: "look", category: Category::Look, target: Target::Clip, version: 1, params: &*LOOK_PARAMS, variants: Some("preset"), cost: CostClass::Medium, compile: Clip(look::look) },
        RecipeDef { id: "vignette", category: Category::Light, target: Target::Clip, version: 1, params: &[STRENGTH, SOFTNESS], variants: None, cost: CostClass::Cheap, compile: Clip(look::vignette) },
        RecipeDef { id: "light_leak", category: Category::Light, target: Target::Clip, version: 1, params: &[STRENGTH, LEAK_COLOR, LEAK_VARIANT], variants: None, cost: CostClass::Medium, compile: Clip(leak::light_leak) },
        RecipeDef { id: "clean_fade", category: Category::Transition, target: Target::Cut, version: 1, params: &[FADE_STYLE], variants: None, cost: CostClass::Cheap, compile: Cut(transition::clean_fade) },
        RecipeDef { id: "fade_through_color", category: Category::Transition, target: Target::Cut, version: 1, params: &[DIP_COLOR], variants: None, cost: CostClass::Cheap, compile: Cut(transition::fade_through_color) },
        RecipeDef { id: "smooth_push", category: Category::Transition, target: Target::Cut, version: 1, params: &[CUT_DIRECTION], variants: None, cost: CostClass::Cheap, compile: Cut(transition::smooth_push) },
        RecipeDef { id: "light_sweep", category: Category::Transition, target: Target::Cut, version: 1, params: &[CUT_DIRECTION, SWEEP_COLOR], variants: None, cost: CostClass::Medium, compile: Cut(transition::light_sweep) },
        RecipeDef { id: "wipe", category: Category::Transition, target: Target::Cut, version: 1, params: &[CUT_DIRECTION, WIPE_SOFTNESS], variants: None, cost: CostClass::Cheap, compile: Cut(transition::wipe) },
    ]
});
```
