- [ ] **Step 5: The recipes** — above the test module of `crates/effects/src/transition.rs`:

```rust
//! Transitions (effects spec §5 «Переходы», D4, D5): recipes on a cut. A
//! transition's `compile` turns the window's progress into a mix of the two
//! sides (a `TransitionOp`), a canvas shift of each side, colour primitives
//! on each side and a generator above the mix — a [`TransitionBuild`]; the
//! evaluator (`kadr-timeline::scene`) applies it to the clips' own layers.
//! `left` means the pictures (or the edge) move leftwards: the incoming one
//! comes from the right.

use crate::params::{ParamKind, Params};
use crate::registry::{Overlay, RecipeDef};
use kadr_core::ease::Curve;
use kadr_core::{FrameRate, Time, TransitionId};
use kadr_project::ParamValue;
use kadr_scene::{BlendMode, ColorAdjust, Effect, Generator, Gradient, GradientShape, GradientStop, Rgba, SizeU, TransitionOp};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

/// A new transition's length (spec §5): 0.5 s.
pub const DEFAULT_SECS: f64 = 0.5;
/// «Плёнка»: the exposure lift half way, EV (`FILM_LIFT_EV · sin(π·p)`).
pub const FILM_LIFT_EV: f64 = 0.35;
/// Шторка at softness 1: a band this fraction of the canvas diagonal wide.
pub const WIPE_SOFT_DIAGONAL: f64 = 0.25;
/// Световая волна: the band's length along its direction, as a fraction of
/// the canvas's extent there, and its peak opacity.
pub const SWEEP_BAND: f64 = 0.35;
pub const SWEEP_OPACITY: f64 = 0.85;

/// What a transition's `compile` knows (D5).
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct TransitionCtx {
    /// Linear progress through the (clamped) window, 0..1.
    pub progress: f64,
    pub canvas: SizeU,
    /// The output being made: Сдвиг puts its seam on its pixel grid.
    pub output: SizeU,
    /// From the transition's id ([`transition_seed`]).
    pub seed: u64,
}

/// One frame of a transition (D5, refinement 5).
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct TransitionBuild {
    pub op: TransitionOp,
    /// The mix's progress.
    pub progress: f32,
    /// How far each side moves on the canvas, in canvas widths (x) and
    /// heights (y) — its clip's overlays with it.
    pub from_shift: (f64, f64),
    pub to_shift: (f64, f64),
    /// Colour primitives appended to each side's clip layer.
    pub from_effects: Vec<Effect>,
    pub to_effects: Vec<Effect>,
    /// A full-canvas generator above the mix.
    pub overlay: Option<Overlay>,
}

impl TransitionBuild {
    /// A plain mix: `op` at `progress` (clamped into 0..1, NaN → 0), nothing else.
    pub fn mix(op: TransitionOp, progress: f64) -> TransitionBuild {
        let p = if progress.is_nan() { 0.0 } else { progress.clamp(0.0, 1.0) };
        TransitionBuild { op, progress: p as f32, from_shift: (0.0, 0.0), to_shift: (0.0, 0.0), from_effects: vec![], to_effects: vec![], overlay: None }
    }
}

/// Every declared parameter of a cut recipe at its default (a cut has no
/// point or moment).
pub fn transition_params(def: &RecipeDef) -> BTreeMap<String, ParamValue> {
    def.params
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|d| {
            let v = match d.kind {
                ParamKind::Number { default, .. } => ParamValue::Number(default),
                ParamKind::Choice { default, .. } => ParamValue::Choice(default.to_string()),
                ParamKind::Point | ParamKind::Moment => return None,
            };
            Some((d.key.to_string(), v))
        })
        .collect()
}

/// A new transition's length at `rate`: [`DEFAULT_SECS`] on whole frames, at least two.
pub fn default_duration(rate: FrameRate) -> Time {
    rate.snap(Time::from_secs_f64(DEFAULT_SECS)).max(rate.frame_to_time(2))
}

/// A transition's noise seed: from its id.
pub fn transition_seed(id: TransitionId) -> u64 {
    let u = id.0.as_u128();
    (u as u64) ^ ((u >> 64) as u64)
}

/// The unit direction a key names (y down): `left` (the default), `right`, `up`, `down`.
fn direction(key: &str) -> (f64, f64) {
    match key {
        "right" => (1.0, 0.0),
        "up" => (0.0, -1.0),
        "down" => (0.0, 1.0),
        _ => (-1.0, 0.0),
    }
}

/// Растворение (Clean Fade): a dissolve; «Плёнка» lifts both sides'
/// exposure towards the middle, `FILM_LIFT_EV · sin(π·p)`.
pub fn clean_fade(p: &Params, ctx: &TransitionCtx) -> TransitionBuild {
    let mut b = TransitionBuild::mix(TransitionOp::Dissolve, ctx.progress);
    if p.choice("style") == "film" {
        let lift = FILM_LIFT_EV * (std::f64::consts::PI * b.progress as f64).sin();
        if lift > 1e-6 {
            let e = Effect::ColorAdjust(ColorAdjust { exposure: lift as f32, ..ColorAdjust::NEUTRAL });
            (b.from_effects, b.to_effects) = (vec![e.clone()], vec![e]);
        }
    }
    b
}

/// Через цвет (Fade Through Color): a dip to black or white.
pub fn fade_through_color(p: &Params, ctx: &TransitionCtx) -> TransitionBuild {
    let colour = if p.choice("color") == "white" { Rgba { r: 1.0, g: 1.0, b: 1.0, a: 1.0 } } else { Rgba::BLACK };
    TransitionBuild::mix(TransitionOp::DipToColor(colour), ctx.progress)
}

/// Сдвиг (Smooth Push): both pictures slide one canvas length in the
/// direction (Cinematic) — the outgoing one leaves, the incoming one
/// follows it in, edge to edge, `Over` — with the seam on a whole output
/// pixel (refinement 4).
pub fn smooth_push(p: &Params, ctx: &TransitionCtx) -> TransitionBuild {
    let (dx, dy) = direction(p.choice("direction"));
    let e = seam_on_pixels(Curve::Cinematic.apply(ctx.progress), dx + dy > 0.0, dx != 0.0, ctx);
    TransitionBuild { from_shift: (dx * e, dy * e), to_shift: (dx * (e - 1.0), dy * (e - 1.0)), ..TransitionBuild::mix(TransitionOp::Over, ctx.progress) }
}

/// `e` (how far the pictures have moved, in canvas lengths) moved so the
/// seam between them — at `e` of the canvas from its start when they move
/// right or down, `1 − e` when left or up — falls on a whole output pixel
/// of the contract's map `output = k·canvas + o`. 0 and 1 stay; an empty
/// output changes nothing.
fn seam_on_pixels(e: f64, towards_positive: bool, horizontal: bool, ctx: &TransitionCtx) -> f64 {
    let (cw, ch) = (ctx.canvas.w as f64, ctx.canvas.h as f64);
    let (ow, oh) = (ctx.output.w as f64, ctx.output.h as f64);
    let k = (ow / cw).min(oh / ch);
    if !(k.is_finite() && k > 0.0) || e <= 0.0 || e >= 1.0 {
        return e;
    }
    let (len, o) = if horizontal { (cw, (ow - k * cw) / 2.0) } else { (ch, (oh - k * ch) / 2.0) };
    let seam = if towards_positive { e * len } else { (1.0 - e) * len };
    let snapped = ((k * seam + o).round() - o) / k;
    (if towards_positive { snapped / len } else { 1.0 - snapped / len }).clamp(0.0, 1.0)
}

/// Световая волна's colours: warm (the default), white, cold.
fn sweep_colour(key: &str) -> Rgba {
    match key {
        "white" => Rgba { r: 1.0, g: 1.0, b: 1.0, a: 1.0 },
        "cold" => Rgba { r: 0.72, g: 0.86, b: 1.0, a: 1.0 },
        _ => Rgba { r: 1.0, g: 0.86, b: 0.62, a: 1.0 },
    }
}

/// Световая волна (Light Sweep): a dissolve (Smooth) under a soft band of
/// light crossing the canvas in the direction, in Screen — a band before the
/// canvas at the start of the window, past it at the end.
pub fn light_sweep(p: &Params, ctx: &TransitionCtx) -> TransitionBuild {
    let u = Curve::EaseInOut.apply(ctx.progress);
    let (dx, dy) = direction(p.choice("direction"));
    let (cw, ch) = (ctx.canvas.w as f64, ctx.canvas.h as f64);
    // The canvas's extent along the direction: its corners' dot products.
    let dots = [0.0, cw * dx, ch * dy, cw * dx + ch * dy];
    let lo = dots.iter().copied().fold(f64::INFINITY, f64::min);
    let hi = dots.iter().copied().fold(f64::NEG_INFINITY, f64::max);
    let band = SWEEP_BAND * (hi - lo);
    let offset = (lo - band) + (hi - lo + band) * u;
    let c = sweep_colour(p.choice("color"));
    let clear = Rgba { a: 0.0, ..c };
    let stops = vec![GradientStop { pos: 0.0, colour: clear }, GradientStop { pos: 0.5, colour: c }, GradientStop { pos: 1.0, colour: clear }];
    let gradient = Gradient { shape: GradientShape::Linear { angle: dy.atan2(dx) as f32, offset: offset as f32, length: band as f32 }, stops };
    TransitionBuild {
        overlay: Some(Overlay { generator: Generator::Gradient(gradient), blend: BlendMode::Screen, opacity: SWEEP_OPACITY as f32 }),
        ..TransitionBuild::mix(TransitionOp::Dissolve, u)
    }
}

/// Шторка (Wipe): the straight edge in the direction; softness 1 = a band
/// a quarter of the canvas diagonal wide, 0 a hard edge. `left` is the old
/// wipe (`xfade=wipeleft`, angle π).
pub fn wipe(p: &Params, ctx: &TransitionCtx) -> TransitionBuild {
    use std::f32::consts::{FRAC_PI_2, PI};
    let angle = match p.choice("direction") {
        "right" => 0.0,
        "up" => -FRAC_PI_2,
        "down" => FRAC_PI_2,
        _ => PI,
    };
    let diagonal = (ctx.canvas.w as f64).hypot(ctx.canvas.h as f64);
    let softness = (p.number("softness") * WIPE_SOFT_DIAGONAL * diagonal) as f32;
    TransitionBuild::mix(TransitionOp::Wipe { angle, softness }, ctx.progress)
}
```

Above the test module of `crates/effects/src/leak.rs`:

```rust
//! Засветка (Light Leak, effects spec §5 «Свет», D7): light leaking into
//! the picture from a corner — coarse value noise and a soft radial glow,
//! both tinted, in Screen over the clip's own picture (also on a
//! picture-in-picture), drifting slowly with the effect's progress.
//! Strength scales their opacity by `k = s / 0.6`. The numbers are tuned
//! with the owner (refinement 8): only here.

use crate::params::{intensity, Params};
use crate::registry::{CompileCtx, Compiled, Overlay};
use kadr_core::hash::fmix64;
use kadr_scene::{BlendMode, Generator, Gradient, GradientShape, GradientStop, Noise, Rgba, Vec2};

/// The glow's and the noise's opacity at «Обычно».
pub const GLOW_OPACITY: f64 = 0.55;
pub const NOISE_OPACITY: f64 = 0.4;
/// The glow's centre travels this fraction of the picture over the whole progress.
pub const DRIFT: f64 = 0.2;
/// The noise's time over the whole progress, in slices.
pub const NOISE_SLICES: f64 = 2.0;
/// The noise lattice: this fraction of the picture's short side.
pub const NOISE_CELL: f64 = 0.45;
pub const NOISE_CONTRAST: f64 = 1.6;
/// The glow's radius: this fraction of the picture's diagonal.
pub const GLOW_RADIUS: f64 = 0.85;

/// The tint of a colour key: warm (the default), pink, cold.
pub fn leak_colour(key: &str) -> Rgba {
    match key {
        "pink" => Rgba { r: 1.0, g: 0.45, b: 0.62, a: 1.0 },
        "cold" => Rgba { r: 0.45, g: 0.7, b: 1.0, a: 1.0 },
        _ => Rgba { r: 1.0, g: 0.6, b: 0.25, a: 1.0 },
    }
}

/// A variant's corner (fractions of the picture) and its glow's drift over
/// the whole progress: "1" top left, "2" top right, "3" bottom left.
fn path(variant: &str) -> ((f64, f64), (f64, f64)) {
    match variant {
        "2" => ((1.0, 0.0), (-DRIFT, DRIFT / 2.0)),
        "3" => ((0.0, 1.0), (DRIFT, -DRIFT / 2.0)),
        _ => ((0.0, 0.0), (DRIFT, DRIFT / 2.0)),
    }
}

/// The `light_leak` recipe: noise, then the glow, both in Screen at their
/// opacity × k (at most 1), seeded by the effect and its variant.
pub fn light_leak(p: &Params, ctx: &CompileCtx) -> Compiled {
    let k = intensity(p.number("strength"));
    let colour = leak_colour(p.choice("color"));
    let variant = p.choice("variant");
    let (w, h) = ctx.picture();
    let u = if ctx.progress.is_finite() { ctx.progress.clamp(0.0, 1.0) } else { 0.0 };
    let ((x0, y0), (dx, dy)) = path(variant);
    let centre = Vec2::new(((x0 + dx * u) * w) as f32, ((y0 + dy * u) * h) as f32);
    let stop = |pos: f32, a: f32| GradientStop { pos, colour: Rgba { a, ..colour } };
    let glow = Gradient { shape: GradientShape::Radial { centre, radius: (GLOW_RADIUS * w.hypot(h)) as f32 }, stops: vec![stop(0.0, 1.0), stop(0.4, 0.45), stop(1.0, 0.0)] };
    let salt = variant.bytes().fold(0u64, |a, b| a.wrapping_mul(31).wrapping_add(b as u64));
    let noise = Noise { cell: (NOISE_CELL * w.min(h)) as f32, seed: ctx.seed ^ fmix64(salt), time: (u * NOISE_SLICES) as f32, colour, amount: 1.0, contrast: NOISE_CONTRAST as f32 };
    let opacity = |base: f64| (base * k).clamp(0.0, 1.0) as f32;
    Compiled::overlays(vec![
        Overlay { generator: Generator::Noise(noise), blend: BlendMode::Screen, opacity: opacity(NOISE_OPACITY) },
        Overlay { generator: Generator::Gradient(glow), blend: BlendMode::Screen, opacity: opacity(GLOW_OPACITY) },
    ])
}
```

`crates/effects/src/json.rs`: after `params_from_json_in` add

```rust
/// Client parameters of a transition recipe — numbers and choice strings
/// (a cut has no point or moment) — each checked like a command would.
pub fn cut_params_from_json(def: &RecipeDef, json: &Map<String, Value>) -> Result<BTreeMap<String, ParamValue>, String> {
    let mut out = BTreeMap::new();
    for (k, v) in json {
        let d = def
            .param(k)
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("{} has no parameter {k:?} (it has {})", def.id, def.params.iter().map(|d| d.key).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")))?;
        let value = match d.kind {
            ParamKind::Number { .. } => ParamValue::Number(v.as_f64().ok_or_else(|| format!("{k} must be a number"))?),
            ParamKind::Choice { .. } => ParamValue::Choice(v.as_str().ok_or_else(|| format!("{k} must be a string"))?.to_string()),
            ParamKind::Point | ParamKind::Moment => return Err(format!("{k} is not a parameter a transition takes")),
        };
        d.check(&value)?;
        out.insert(k.clone(), value);
    }
    Ok(out)
}
```

```rust
    #[test]
    fn transition_recipes_go_on_cuts_and_take_numbers_and_choices() {
        let all: Vec<Value> = registry().iter().map(recipe_json).collect();
        let wipe = all.iter().find(|r| r["id"] == "wipe").unwrap();
        assert_eq!((wipe["category"].as_str(), wipe["target"].as_str()), (Some("transition"), Some("cut")));
        let def = recipe("wipe").unwrap();
        let p = cut_params_from_json(def, &obj(json!({"direction": "up", "softness": 0.5}))).unwrap();
        assert_eq!((p["direction"].clone(), p["softness"].clone()), (ParamValue::Choice("up".into()), ParamValue::Number(0.5)));
        let err = |j: Value| cut_params_from_json(def, &obj(j)).unwrap_err();
        assert!(err(json!({"direction": "sideways"})).contains("one of left, right, up, down"));
        assert!(err(json!({"softness": 2})).contains("softness must be 0..1"));
        assert!(err(json!({"strength": 0.5})).contains("wipe has no parameter \"strength\""));
    }
```

```rust
/// Засветка in each colour at «Обычно», a third of the way through its
/// clip, over the photo: the overlays the recipe compiles, on the picture's
/// layer as the evaluator puts them (refinement 7).
#[test]
fn light_leaks() {
    let def = kadr_effects::recipe("light_leak").expect("E3 recipe");
    for colour in ["warm", "pink", "cold"] {
        let stored = [("color".to_string(), kadr_project::ParamValue::Choice(colour.into()))].into_iter().collect();
        let params = kadr_effects::Params::resolve(def.params, &stored, |_| None);
        let ctx = kadr_effects::CompileCtx { progress: 0.3, ..kadr_effects::CompileCtx::still(CANVAS, Time::ZERO, 7) };
        let base = full(AlphaMode::Opaque);
        let mut layers = vec![base.clone()];
        for o in def.compile_clip(&params, &ctx).overlays {
            layers.push(Layer { content: LayerContent::Generator(o.generator), opacity: o.opacity * base.opacity, blend: o.blend, effects: vec![], ..base.clone() });
        }
        let inputs = std::iter::once(LayerInput::Cpu(photo())).chain(std::iter::repeat_n(LayerInput::None, layers.len() - 1)).collect();
        check(&format!("light_leak_{colour}"), scene(OUT, layers), inputs);
    }
}
```

**Interfaces:**

- Produces:
  - `kadr_project::Transition { pub id: TransitionId, pub recipe: String, pub version: u16, pub params: BTreeMap<String, ParamValue>, pub from: ClipId, pub to: ClipId, pub duration: Time, pub extra: BTreeMap<String, serde_json::Value> }` (`Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize`; `extra` flattened); `Transition::new(recipe, version, params, from, to, duration) -> Transition`; `Transition::legacy(kind: TransitionKind, from, to, duration) -> Transition`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Pin the E2 picture of the old transitions** — `crates/project-scenes/Cargo.toml` `[dev-dependencies]`: add `png = "0.17"` and `serde_json.workspace = true`. Create `crates/project-scenes/tests/legacy_transitions.rs` (it uses only APIs that stay — the JSON of a file, `io::parse`, the snapshot and the renderer — so the same test runs before and after the model changes):

```rust
//! Review focus 1 (D1, saved semantics): a project saved before E3 — its
//! transitions named by the old `kind` — opens and renders every frame of
//! every transition exactly as the E2 build did. The golden image was
//! rendered by the E2 evaluator (E3 Task 3, Step 2), before the model
//! changed; the migrated recipes must keep matching it byte for byte.

use kadr_core::color::AlphaMode;
use kadr_core::{AssetId, ColorInfo, CpuFrame, FrameRate, MediaInfo, MediaKind, Time, TimeRange, VideoInfo};
use kadr_playback::SceneSource;
use kadr_project::{io, Clip, MediaAsset, Project};
use kadr_project_scenes::ProjectScenes;
use kadr_render::{CpuRenderer, CpuTarget, LayerInput, MissingReason, PreparedFrame, RenderInputs, RenderTarget, Renderer};
use kadr_scene::{Layer, LayerContent, OutputSpec, RenderQuality, SizeU};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Arc;

const W: u32 = 64;
const H: u32 = 36;

fn asset(name: &str) -> MediaAsset {
    let video = VideoInfo { width: W, height: H, frame_rate: Some(FrameRate::FPS_30), variable_frame_rate: false, codec: "h264".into(), pixel_format: "yuv420p".into(), rotation: 0, sar: (1, 1), color: None };
    MediaAsset::new(name, MediaInfo { kind: MediaKind::Video, duration: Time::from_secs(60), container: "mp4".into(), size_bytes: 0, video: Some(video), audio: None, timecode: None })
}

fn frame(px: impl Fn(u32, u32) -> [u8; 4]) -> Arc<CpuFrame> {
    let bytes = (0..H).flat_map(|y| (0..W).map(move |x| (x, y))).flat_map(|(x, y)| px(x, y)).collect();
    Arc::new(CpuFrame::from_rgba8(W, H, ColorInfo { alpha: AlphaMode::Opaque, ..ColorInfo::WORKING_SDR }, bytes))
}

/// A format-3 file as Kadr wrote it before E3: clips a (0..2 s) and b
/// (2..4 s) on V1 of a 64 × 36, 30 fps sequence and a 1 s transition of
/// `kind` at their cut — plus, for the dissolve, one at 1 s that sits on no
/// cut (the E2 evaluator skipped it; E3 drops it on load).
fn legacy_file(kind: &str) -> (Vec<u8>, HashMap<AssetId, Arc<CpuFrame>>) {
    let mut p = Project::new("legacy");
    let seq = p.sequence_mut();
    (seq.frame_rate, seq.width, seq.height) = (FrameRate::FPS_30, W, H);
    let (a, b) = (asset("a.mp4"), asset("b.mp4"));
    let ca = Clip::new(a.id, "a", TimeRange::new(Time::from_secs(10), Time::from_secs(12)), Time::ZERO);
    let cb = Clip::new(b.id, "b", TimeRange::new(Time::from_secs(20), Time::from_secs(22)), Time::from_secs(2));
    p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips.extend([ca, cb]);
    let frames = HashMap::from([(a.id, frame(|x, y| [(x * 4) as u8, (y * 7) as u8, 128, 255])), (b.id, frame(|x, y| [(255 - x * 3) as u8, 90, (y * 6) as u8, 255]))]);
    p.assets.extend([a, b]);
    let mut v = serde_json::to_value(&p).unwrap();
    let track = v["sequences"][0]["tracks"][0]["id"].clone();
    let f = |ms: i64| Time::from_millis(ms).flicks();
    let mut list = vec![serde_json::json!({"id": kadr_core::TransitionId::new(), "kind": kind, "track": track, "at": f(2_000), "duration": f(1_000)})];
    if kind == "cross_dissolve" {
        list.push(serde_json::json!({"id": kadr_core::TransitionId::new(), "kind": kind, "track": track, "at": f(1_000), "duration": f(400)}));
    }
    v["sequences"][0]["transitions"] = serde_json::Value::Array(list);
    v["format_version"] = serde_json::json!(3);
    (serde_json::to_vec(&v).unwrap(), frames)
}

fn inputs(layers: &[Layer], frames: &HashMap<AssetId, Arc<CpuFrame>>) -> Vec<LayerInput> {
    layers
        .iter()
        .map(|l| match &l.content {
            LayerContent::Media { media, .. } => frames.get(&media.media).map_or(LayerInput::Missing(MissingReason::Offline), |f| LayerInput::Cpu(f.clone())),
            LayerContent::Transition(t) => LayerInput::Transition { from: inputs(&t.from, frames), to: inputs(&t.to, frames) },
            _ => LayerInput::None,
        })
        .collect()
}

/// The three kinds (rows) at five frames of the window [1.5 s, 2.5 s) (columns).
fn strip() -> (u32, u32, Vec<u8>) {
    let at = [45, 54, 60, 67, 74];
    let (sw, sh) = (W * at.len() as u32, H * 3);
    let mut out = vec![0u8; (sw * sh * 4) as usize];
    for (row, kind) in ["cross_dissolve", "dip_to_black", "wipe"].iter().enumerate() {
        let (bytes, frames) = legacy_file(kind);
        let p = io::parse(&bytes, Path::new("legacy.kadr")).expect("a pre-E3 file opens");
        let scenes = ProjectScenes::new(&p, false);
        for (col, n) in at.iter().enumerate() {
            let scene = scenes.scene_at(FrameRate::FPS_30.frame_to_time(*n), &OutputSpec::new(SizeU::new(W, H), RenderQuality::Export));
            let ins = RenderInputs { layers: inputs(&scene.layers, &frames) };
            let mut buf = vec![0u8; (W * H * 4) as usize];
            CpuRenderer::new().render(&PreparedFrame { scene: &scene, inputs: &ins }, &mut RenderTarget::Cpu(CpuTarget::packed(W, H, &mut buf))).unwrap();
            for y in 0..H {
                let at = (((row as u32 * H + y) * sw + col as u32 * W) * 4) as usize;
                out[at..at + (W * 4) as usize].copy_from_slice(&buf[(y * W * 4) as usize..((y + 1) * W * 4) as usize]);
            }
        }
    }
    (sw, sh, out)
}

fn golden_path() -> PathBuf {
    PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("tests").join("golden").join("legacy_transitions.png")
}

fn read_png(path: &Path) -> Option<(u32, u32, Vec<u8>)> {
    let mut reader = png::Decoder::new(std::fs::File::open(path).ok()?).read_info().expect("golden PNG header");
    let mut buf = vec![0; reader.output_buffer_size()];
    let info = reader.next_frame(&mut buf).expect("golden PNG data");
    buf.truncate(info.buffer_size());
    Some((info.width, info.height, buf))
}

fn write_png(path: &Path, w: u32, h: u32, data: &[u8]) {
    std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
    let mut enc = png::Encoder::new(std::io::BufWriter::new(std::fs::File::create(path).unwrap()), w, h);
    enc.set_color(png::ColorType::Rgba);
    enc.set_depth(png::BitDepth::Eight);
    enc.write_header().unwrap().write_image_data(data).unwrap();
}

#[test]
fn legacy_transitions_render_as_before() {
    let (w, h, out) = strip();
    let path = golden_path();
    if std::env::var_os("KADR_UPDATE_GOLDEN").is_some_and(|v| v == "1") {
        write_png(&path, w, h, &out);
        return;
    }
    let (gw, gh, golden) = read_png(&path).expect("no golden: it is blessed once, at the E2 evaluator (E3 Task 3, Step 2)");
    assert_eq!((gw, gh), (w, h));
    let diff = out.iter().zip(&golden).filter(|(a, b)| a != b).count();
    assert_eq!(diff, 0, "{diff} bytes differ from the E2 picture");
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the failing model tests** — create `crates/project/src/transitions.rs` with only this test module and add `pub mod transitions;` (after `pub mod sequence;`) and `pub use transitions::*;` (after `pub use sequence::*;`) to `crates/project/src/lib.rs`:

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::Clip;
    use kadr_core::{AssetId, FrameRate, TimeRange};

    fn s(x: i64) -> Time {
        Time::from_secs(x)
    }

    /// V1: a 0..4 s, b 4..8 s, c 8..12 s (each its own medium), A1 empty;
    /// a dissolve on a|b, a wipe on b|c.
    fn seq3() -> (Sequence, [ClipId; 3]) {
        let mut seq = Sequence::new("s", FrameRate::FPS_25, 1920, 1080);
        let clip = |a: i64, b: i64| Clip::new(AssetId::new(), "c", TimeRange::new(s(100 + a), s(100 + b)), s(a));
        let cs = [clip(0, 4), clip(4, 8), clip(8, 12)];
        let ids = cs.each_ref().map(|c| c.id);
        seq.tracks[0].clips.extend(cs);
        seq.transitions = vec![Transition::legacy(TransitionKind::CrossDissolve, ids[0], ids[1], s(1)), Transition::legacy(TransitionKind::Wipe, ids[1], ids[2], s(1))];
        (seq, ids)
    }

    /// A split as the engine makes it: the left half keeps the id, the
    /// right one continues it (same medium, next timeline and source time).
    fn split(seq: &mut Sequence, id: ClipId, at: Time) -> ClipId {
        let (ti, ci) = seq.locate_clip(id).unwrap();
        let mut right = seq.tracks[ti].clips[ci].clone();
        let left = &mut seq.tracks[ti].clips[ci];
        let src = left.source_time_at(at);
        (left.timeline_out, left.source_out) = (at, src);
        right.id = ClipId::new();
        (right.timeline_in, right.source_in) = (at, src);
        let rid = right.id;
        seq.tracks[ti].clips.insert(ci + 1, right);
        rid
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_split_of_from_re_anchors_to_the_piece_at_the_cut_and_one_of_to_keeps_it() {
        let (mut seq, [a, b, _]) = seq3();
        let a2 = split(&mut seq, a, s(2));
        let a3 = split(&mut seq, a2, s(3));
        assert!(seq.reconcile_transitions().is_empty());
        assert_eq!((seq.transitions[0].from, seq.transitions[0].to), (a3, b), "the piece that now ends at the cut");
        let b2 = split(&mut seq, b, s(6));
        assert!(seq.reconcile_transitions().is_empty());
        assert_eq!((seq.transitions[0].to, seq.transitions[1].from), (b, b2), "b keeps its start, and b|c follows b's right half");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_pair_that_no_longer_meets_is_dropped_and_a_disabled_one_stays() {
        let (mut seq, _) = seq3();
        seq.tracks[0].clips[0].timeline_out = s(3);
        assert_eq!(seq.reconcile_transitions().len(), 1, "a gap at the cut");
        assert_eq!((seq.transitions.len(), seq.transitions[0].to), (1, seq.tracks[0].clips[2].id), "b|c stays");
        let (mut seq, [_, b, _]) = seq3();
        seq.tracks[0].clips.retain(|x| x.id != b);
        assert_eq!(seq.reconcile_transitions().len(), 2, "both cuts of a deleted clip go");
        let (mut seq, [a, ..]) = seq3();
        let a2 = split(&mut seq, a, s(2));
        seq.tracks[0].clips.retain(|x| x.id != a2);
        assert_eq!(seq.reconcile_transitions().len(), 1, "a piece of `from` removed: a gap again");
        let (mut seq, _) = seq3();
        seq.tracks[1].clips = std::mem::take(&mut seq.tracks[0].clips);
        assert_eq!(seq.reconcile_transitions().len(), 2, "no transitions on sound (D11)");
        let (mut seq, _) = seq3();
        seq.tracks[0].clips[0].enabled = false;
        assert!(seq.reconcile_transitions().is_empty(), "a switched-off clip keeps its transition (it draws nothing while off)");
    }

    #[test]
    fn one_transition_per_cut_and_the_lookups() {
        let (mut seq, [a, b, c]) = seq3();
        let extra = Transition::legacy(TransitionKind::DipToBlack, a, b, s(2));
        seq.transitions.push(extra.clone());
        assert_eq!(seq.reconcile_transitions(), vec![extra], "the first on a cut stays");
        let t = seq.transitions[1].clone();
        assert_eq!(seq.transition(t.id), Some(&t));
        assert_eq!((seq.transition_cut(&t), seq.transition_limit(&t)), (Some((0, s(8))), Some(s(4))));
        let stale = Transition::legacy(TransitionKind::Wipe, c, a, s(1));
        assert_eq!((seq.transition_cut(&stale), seq.transition_limit(&stale)), (None, None), "c and a do not meet");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_cut_is_named_by_a_time_within_slack_only_when_it_is_the_one() {
        let (seq, [a, b, _]) = seq3();
        let track = seq.tracks[0].id;
        assert_eq!(seq.cut_near(track, s(4), Time::ZERO), Some((a, b, s(4))));
        let half = Time(FrameRate::FPS_25.frame_duration().flicks() / 2);
        assert_eq!(seq.cut_near(track, s(4) + Time(half.flicks() - 1), half).map(|c| c.2), Some(s(4)));
        assert_eq!(seq.cut_near(track, s(4) + half + Time(1), half), None, "more than half a frame away");
        assert_eq!(seq.cut_near(track, s(6), s(3)), None, "two cuts within reach: neither is named");
        assert_eq!(seq.cut_near(seq.tracks[1].id, s(4), half), None, "the audio track has no cut");
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_old_kinds_name_their_recipes() {
        let (r, p) = TransitionKind::Wipe.recipe();
        assert_eq!((r, p["direction"].clone(), p["softness"].clone()), ("wipe", ParamValue::Choice("left".into()), ParamValue::Number(0.0)));
        assert_eq!(TransitionKind::CrossDissolve.recipe(), ("clean_fade", BTreeMap::from([("style".to_string(), ParamValue::Choice("clean".into()))])));
        assert_eq!(TransitionKind::DipToBlack.recipe().1["color"], ParamValue::Choice("black".into()));
        let t = Transition::legacy(TransitionKind::DipToBlack, ClipId::new(), ClipId::new(), s(1));
        assert_eq!((t.recipe.as_str(), t.version, t.extra.is_empty()), ("fade_through_color", 1, true));
    }

    #[derive(serde::Deserialize)]
    struct Holder {
        #[serde(deserialize_with = "deserialize_transitions")]
        transitions: Vec<Transition>,
    }

    #[test]
    fn transitions_load_leniently_and_keep_ids_unique() {
        let id = TransitionId::new();
        let ok = |id: TransitionId| serde_json::json!({"id": id, "recipe": "wipe", "from": ClipId::new(), "to": ClipId::new(), "duration": 100});
        let list = serde_json::json!([7, {"recipe": "x"}, ok(id), ok(id), {"id": TransitionId::new(), "recipe": "a", "from": "nope", "to": ClipId::new(), "duration": 1}]);
        let h: Holder = serde_json::from_value(serde_json::json!({ "transitions": list })).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(h.transitions.len(), 1, "the first of an id wins, the unreadable go");
        assert_eq!((h.transitions[0].version, h.transitions[0].params.is_empty()), (1, true), "defaults");
        for bad in [serde_json::json!(null), serde_json::json!("x"), serde_json::json!({"a": 1})] {
            let h: Holder = serde_json::from_value(serde_json::json!({ "transitions": bad })).unwrap();
            assert!(h.transitions.is_empty());
        }
    }
}
```

```rust
    /// Two clips on V1 of `v2_project` meeting at 10 s; returns their ids.
    fn with_pair(v: &mut serde_json::Value) -> (kadr_core::ClipId, kadr_core::ClipId) {
        let f = |s: i64| s * kadr_core::FLICKS_PER_SECOND;
        let (a, b) = (kadr_core::ClipId::new(), kadr_core::ClipId::new());
        let clip = |id: kadr_core::ClipId, tin: i64| serde_json::json!({"id": id, "asset": kadr_core::AssetId::new(), "name": "c", "source_in": 0, "source_out": f(10), "timeline_in": f(tin), "timeline_out": f(tin + 10)});
        v["sequences"][0]["tracks"][0]["clips"] = serde_json::json!([clip(a, 0), clip(b, 10)]);
        (a, b)
    }

    /// D1, on every load (format 2 and 3 alike): the old kinds become their
    /// recipes on the clip pair at their cut; one on no cut is dropped; the
    /// old fields are never written again.
    #[test]
    fn old_transition_kinds_become_recipes_on_their_cut() {
        let f = |s: i64| s * kadr_core::FLICKS_PER_SECOND;
        for format in [2, 3] {
            let mut v = v2_project();
            v["format_version"] = serde_json::json!(format);
            let (a, b) = with_pair(&mut v);
            let track = v["sequences"][0]["tracks"][0]["id"].clone();
            let tr = |kind: &str, at: i64| serde_json::json!({"id": kadr_core::TransitionId::new(), "kind": kind, "track": track, "at": at, "duration": f(1)});
            for (kind, recipe, params) in [
                ("cross_dissolve", "clean_fade", serde_json::json!({"style": {"choice": "clean"}})),
                ("dip_to_black", "fade_through_color", serde_json::json!({"color": {"choice": "black"}})),
                ("wipe", "wipe", serde_json::json!({"direction": {"choice": "left"}, "softness": {"number": 0.0}})),
            ] {
                v["sequences"][0]["transitions"] = serde_json::json!([tr(kind, f(10)), tr(kind, f(5))]);
                let p = parse(&serde_json::to_vec(&v).unwrap(), Path::new("old.kadr")).unwrap();
                let t = &p.sequence().transitions;
                assert_eq!(t.len(), 1, "{kind}: the one at 5 s sat on no cut (invisible before): dropped");
                assert_eq!((t[0].recipe.as_str(), t[0].version, t[0].from, t[0].to, t[0].duration), (recipe, 1, a, b, kadr_core::Time::from_secs(1)), "{kind}");
                let saved = serde_json::to_value(&p).unwrap();
                let s = &saved["sequences"][0]["transitions"][0];
                assert_eq!(s["params"], params, "{kind}");
                assert!(s.get("kind").is_none() && s.get("track").is_none() && s.get("at").is_none(), "{kind}: the old fields are never written");
                assert_eq!(saved["format_version"], serde_json::json!(3), "no new format (R2)");
            }
        }
    }

    /// Bad transition data never fails the project and never panics.

    fn malformed_transitions_are_dropped_not_fatal() {
        for transitions in [
            serde_json::json!("x"),
            serde_json::json!(null),
            serde_json::json!([7, "s", null, [1]]),
            serde_json::json!([{"kind": "cross_dissolve"}]),

            serde_json::json!([{"kind": "wipe", "track": 5, "at": "x"}]),

            with_pair(&mut v);
            v["sequences"][0]["transitions"] = transitions.clone();
            let p = parse(&serde_json::to_vec(&v).unwrap(), Path::new("bad.kadr")).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{transitions}: {e}"));

    /// Spec §2: a transition from a newer Kadr — recipe, version, an unknown

    #[test]

        let mut p = crate::Project::new("future");
        let f = kadr_core::Time::from_secs;
        let a = crate::Clip::new(kadr_core::AssetId::new(), "a", kadr_core::TimeRange::new(f(0), f(4)), f(0));
        let b = crate::Clip::new(kadr_core::AssetId::new(), "b", kadr_core::TimeRange::new(f(0), f(4)), f(4));
        let params = [("amount".to_string(), crate::ParamValue::Number(0.4)), ("shape".to_string(), crate::ParamValue::Unknown(serde_json::json!({"star": 5})))].into();
        let mut t = crate::Transition::new("iris_v2", 3, params, a.id, b.id, f(1));

        assert_eq!(to_json(&loaded), saved, "parse → save gives the same bytes");
        assert_eq!(loaded, p);
    }

    /// D2 at load: a saved transition whose clips no longer meet (a

        let b = crate::Clip::new(kadr_core::AssetId::new(), "b", kadr_core::TimeRange::new(f(0), f(4)), f(5));
        p.sequence_mut().transitions.push(crate::Transition::legacy(crate::TransitionKind::Wipe, a.id, b.id, f(1)));
        p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips.extend([a, b]);
        assert!(parse(&to_json(&p), Path::new("gap.kadr")).unwrap().sequence().transitions.is_empty());
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The model** — above the test module of `crates/project/src/transitions.rs`:

```rust
//! Transitions on cuts (effects spec §2, D1, D2): a recipe of `kadr-effects`
//! (`Target::Cut`) with its parameters, anchored to the pair of clips that
//! meet at the cut — the outgoing `from` and the incoming `to` — and a
//! length the evaluator clamps to both. Everything here is data and its
//! consistency: a transition follows its clips (`reconcile_transitions`),
//! what it looks like is the recipe's. A recipe, version, parameter or field
//! this build does not know is kept verbatim (spec §2).

use crate::{ParamValue, Sequence, Track, TrackKind};
use kadr_core::{ClipId, Time, TrackId, TransitionId};
use serde::de::Deserializer;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use serde_json::Value;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

fn one() -> u16 {
    1
}

/// The three kinds Kadr wrote before E3 (format 3 kept them as `kind`):
/// understood when a file is loaded and from MCP / AI clients, never written.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum TransitionKind {
    CrossDissolve,
    DipToBlack,
    Wipe,
}

impl TransitionKind {
    /// The recipe and parameters a kind became (D1, spec §2) — drawing the
    /// picture the kind drew: `cross_dissolve` → `clean_fade {style: clean}`,
    /// `dip_to_black` → `fade_through_color {color: black}`, `wipe` →
    /// `wipe {direction: left, softness: 0}`.
    pub fn recipe(self) -> (&'static str, BTreeMap<String, ParamValue>) {
        let choice = |k: &str, v: &str| (k.to_string(), ParamValue::Choice(v.to_string()));
        match self {
            TransitionKind::CrossDissolve => ("clean_fade", BTreeMap::from([choice("style", "clean")])),
            TransitionKind::DipToBlack => ("fade_through_color", BTreeMap::from([choice("color", "black")])),
            TransitionKind::Wipe => ("wipe", BTreeMap::from([choice("direction", "left"), ("softness".to_string(), ParamValue::Number(0.0))])),
        }
    }
}

/// A transition on the cut between `from` and `to` (D1).
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Transition {
    pub id: TransitionId,
    /// Registry id of a cut recipe, e.g. "clean_fade" (`kadr-effects`).
    pub recipe: String,
    /// The recipe's `compile` version this transition was made with.
    #[serde(default = "one")]
    pub version: u16,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub params: BTreeMap<String, ParamValue>,
    /// The outgoing clip: the cut is its `timeline_out`.
    pub from: ClipId,
    /// The incoming clip, starting at the cut.
    pub to: ClipId,
    /// As set; the evaluator clamps it to both clips.
    pub duration: Time,
    /// Fields from a newer Kadr, kept verbatim.
    #[serde(flatten)]
    pub extra: BTreeMap<String, Value>,
}

impl Transition {
    pub fn new(recipe: impl Into<String>, version: u16, params: BTreeMap<String, ParamValue>, from: ClipId, to: ClipId, duration: Time) -> Transition {
        Transition { id: TransitionId::new(), recipe: recipe.into(), version, params, from, to, duration, extra: BTreeMap::new() }
    }

    /// A transition of an old `kind` on the cut `from` | `to` (D1).
    pub fn legacy(kind: TransitionKind, from: ClipId, to: ClipId, duration: Time) -> Transition {
        let (recipe, params) = kind.recipe();
        Transition::new(recipe, 1, params, from, to, duration)
    }
}

/// `Sequence::transitions` from a file; never fails the load. An entry
/// that is not a transition — not an object, a missing or mistyped field,
/// an old `kind` the migration could not place — is dropped with a warning,
/// and so is a later one with an id already seen.
pub fn deserialize_transitions<'de, D: Deserializer<'de>>(d: D) -> Result<Vec<Transition>, D::Error> {
    let Value::Array(items) = Value::deserialize(d)? else {
        tracing::warn!("ignoring sequence transitions that are not a list");
        return Ok(vec![]);
    };
    let mut out: Vec<Transition> = Vec::with_capacity(items.len());
    for item in items {
        match serde_json::from_value::<Transition>(item) {
            Ok(t) if out.iter().any(|x| x.id == t.id) => tracing::warn!(id = %t.id, "dropping a duplicate transition"),
            Ok(t) => out.push(t),
            Err(err) => tracing::warn!(error = %err, "dropping an unreadable transition"),
        }
    }
    Ok(out)
}

impl Sequence {
    /// The one cut of `track` within `slack` of `at` — a clip and the next
    /// one meeting there — as `(from, to, cut)`; `None` when there is none
    /// or more than one (MCP and AI name a cut by a millisecond time, D9).
    pub fn cut_near(&self, track: TrackId, at: Time, slack: Time) -> Option<(ClipId, ClipId, Time)> {
        let t = self.track(track)?;
        let mut cuts = t.clips.windows(2).filter(|w| w[0].timeline_out == w[1].timeline_in && (w[0].timeline_out - at).abs() <= slack);
        match (cuts.next(), cuts.next()) {
            (Some(w), None) => Some((w[0].id, w[1].id, w[0].timeline_out)),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    pub fn transition(&self, id: TransitionId) -> Option<&Transition> {
        self.transitions.iter().find(|t| t.id == id)
    }

    /// Where `tr` is: its track's index and its cut — `None` unless its
    /// clips meet on one video track (`from` right before `to`).
    pub fn transition_cut(&self, tr: &Transition) -> Option<(usize, Time)> {
        let (ti, ci) = self.locate_clip(tr.from)?;
        let t = &self.tracks[ti];
        let next = t.clips.get(ci + 1).filter(|n| n.id == tr.to && n.timeline_in == t.clips[ci].timeline_out)?;
        (t.kind == TrackKind::Video).then_some((ti, next.timeline_in))
    }

    /// The longest `tr` can be: the shorter of its two clips (`None` where it has no cut).
    pub fn transition_limit(&self, tr: &Transition) -> Option<Time> {
        self.transition_cut(tr)?;
        Some(self.clip(tr.from)?.duration().min(self.clip(tr.to)?.duration()))
    }

    /// Keeps every transition on its cut after the clips changed (D2,
    /// refinement 10): one stays while `to` is on a video track and the clip
    /// now ending where `to` starts is `from` — or a piece `from` was split
    /// into, reached from it through pieces each continuing the one before
    /// (same medium, next timeline and source time) — to which it is
    /// re-anchored; one transition per cut (the first). The rest are removed
    /// and returned. A switched-off clip keeps its transition.
    pub fn reconcile_transitions(&mut self) -> Vec<Transition> {
        let mut dropped = vec![];
        let mut cuts: Vec<ClipId> = vec![];
        let tracks = &self.tracks;
        self.transitions.retain_mut(|tr| match follow(tracks, tr.from, tr.to) {
            Some(from) if !cuts.contains(&tr.to) => {
                cuts.push(tr.to);
                tr.from = from;
                true
            }
            _ => {
                dropped.push(tr.clone());
                false
            }
        });
        dropped
    }
}

/// The clip ending where `to` starts, reached from `from` through the
/// pieces a split cut `from` into; `None` when `to` is gone or not on a
/// video track, or the chain breaks before the cut.
fn follow(tracks: &[Track], from: ClipId, to: ClipId) -> Option<ClipId> {
    let track = tracks.iter().find(|t| t.clips.iter().any(|c| c.id == to))?;
    if track.kind != TrackKind::Video {
        return None;
    }
    let cut = track.clips.iter().find(|c| c.id == to)?.timeline_in;
    let mut i = track.clips.iter().position(|c| c.id == from)?;
    loop {
        let c = &track.clips[i];
        if c.timeline_out == cut {
            return Some(c.id);
        }
        let next = track.clips.get(i + 1)?;
        if next.timeline_in != c.timeline_out || next.asset != c.asset || next.source_in != c.source_out {
            return None;
        }
        i += 1;
    }
}
```

```rust
/// Every sequence object; malformed levels are skipped.
fn for_each_sequence(value: &mut serde_json::Value, mut f: impl FnMut(&mut serde_json::Value)) {
    let Some(seqs) = value.get_mut("sequences").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_array_mut) else { return };
    for s in seqs {
        f(s);
    }
}

/// A transition with `kind` and no `recipe` — as Kadr wrote them before E3,
/// its cut named by `track` and `at` — becomes the kind's recipe
/// (`TransitionKind::recipe`, D1) on the clip pair there: the clip of that
/// track ending at `at` (`from`) and the one starting there (`to`). One on
/// no cut (invisible before) is dropped with a log line; anything else
/// malformed is left to the lenient loader. Never panics.
fn migrate_transitions(seq: &mut serde_json::Value) {
    use serde_json::{json, Value};
    // (track id, clip id, timeline_in, timeline_out) of every readable clip.
    let clips: Vec<(Value, Value, i64, i64)> = seq
        .get("tracks")
        .and_then(Value::as_array)
        .into_iter()
        .flatten()
        .flat_map(|t| {
            let track = t.get("id").cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null);
            t.get("clips").and_then(Value::as_array).into_iter().flatten().filter_map(move |c| Some((track.clone(), c.get("id")?.clone(), c.get("timeline_in")?.as_i64()?, c.get("timeline_out")?.as_i64()?)))
        })
        .collect();
    let Some(list) = seq.get_mut("transitions").and_then(Value::as_array_mut) else { return };
    list.retain_mut(|tr| {
        let Some(o) = tr.as_object_mut() else { return true };
        if o.contains_key("recipe") {
            return true;
        }
        let Some(kind) = o.get("kind").and_then(|k| serde_json::from_value::<crate::TransitionKind>(k.clone()).ok()) else { return true };
        let pair = o.get("track").cloned().zip(o.get("at").and_then(Value::as_i64)).and_then(|(track, at)| {
            let from = clips.iter().find(|c| c.0 == track && c.3 == at)?.1.clone();
            let to = clips.iter().find(|c| c.0 == track && c.2 == at)?.1.clone();
            Some((from, to))
        });
        let Some((from, to)) = pair else {
            tracing::warn!(transition = ?o.get("id"), "dropping an old transition that sits on no cut");
            return false;
        };
        let (recipe, params) = kind.recipe();
        for k in ["kind", "track", "at"] {
            o.remove(k);
        }
        o.insert("recipe".into(), json!(recipe));
        o.insert("version".into(), json!(1));
        o.insert("params".into(), serde_json::to_value(&params).unwrap_or_else(|_| json!({})));
        o.insert("from".into(), from);
        o.insert("to".into(), to);
        true
    });
}
```

`crates/timeline/src/scene.rs`: import `use kadr_project::{Clip, ColorAdjust as ClipColor, Project, Sequence, Track, TrackKind, Transform, Transition};` and `use kadr_effects::{Params, RecipeDef, TransitionCtx};`. Replace `transition_layer` (and its doc) with:

```rust
/// The valid transition on `track` whose window contains `t` (D1): one of
/// the two cuts of the clip under `t` — the earlier first — whose clips
/// meet, are enabled and carry a transition the registry runs (a stale or
/// unknown one is never a candidate, so it never hides a valid one). Like
/// export, its length is clamped to both neighbours — `min(duration,
/// outgoing, incoming)` — and under two frames there is no transition. A
/// window lies inside its two clips, so only the cuts of the clip under `t`
/// can reach `t`.
fn active_transition<'a>(seq: &'a Sequence, track: &'a Track, t: Time) -> Option<(&'a Transition, &'static RecipeDef, &'a Clip, &'a Clip, TimeRange)> {
    let k = track.clips.partition_point(|c| c.timeline_in <= t).checked_sub(1)?;
    if t >= track.clips[k].timeline_out {
        return None; // a gap: no window reaches into it
    }
    let min_len = seq.frame_rate.frame_to_time(2);
    let pairs = [k.checked_sub(1).map(|j| (j, k)), (k + 1 < track.clips.len()).then_some((k, k + 1))];
    pairs.into_iter().flatten().find_map(|(a, b)| {
        let (from, to) = (&track.clips[a], &track.clips[b]);
        if from.timeline_out != to.timeline_in || !from.enabled || !to.enabled {
            return None;
        }
        let tr = seq.transitions.iter().find(|tr| tr.from == from.id && tr.to == to.id)?;
        let def = kadr_effects::transition_definition(tr)?;
        let d = tr.duration.min(from.duration()).min(to.duration());
        let w = window(to.timeline_in, d);
        (d >= min_len && w.contains(t)).then_some((tr, def, from, to, w))
    })
}

/// The transition layer on `track` at `t` ([`active_transition`]): its
/// recipe's mix at the window's linear progress (D5). A side that draws
/// nothing at `t` (scale ≤ 0, empty crop, no picture) is an empty,
/// transparent side and the other side still mixes against it (keyframes
/// spec §3.5); with both empty there is no layer. (Task 5 adds the build's
/// shifts, side colour and overlay, and the output size.)
fn transition_layer(project: &Project, seq: &Sequence, track: &Track, t: Time, canvas: SizeU) -> Option<Layer> {
    let (tr, def, outgoing, incoming, w) = active_transition(seq, track, t)?;
    let progress = ((t - w.start).flicks() as f64 / w.duration().flicks() as f64).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
    let params = Params::resolve(def.params, &tr.params, |_| None);
    let build = def.compile_cut(&params, &TransitionCtx { progress, canvas, output: canvas, seed: kadr_effects::transition_seed(tr.id) })?;
    let frame = seq.frame_rate.frame_duration();
    let from = clip_layer(project, outgoing, t, canvas, frame);
    let to = clip_layer(project, incoming, t, canvas, frame);
    if from.is_none() && to.is_none() {
        return None;
    }
    let placement = Placement::fill(canvas);
    Some(Layer {
        id: LayerId::from(tr.id),
        content: LayerContent::Transition(Box::new(TransitionLayer { op: build.op, progress: build.progress, from: from.into_iter().collect(), to: to.into_iter().collect() })),
        crop: placement.full_crop(),
        placement,
        opacity: 1.0,
        blend: BlendMode::Normal,
        effects: vec![],
    })
}
```

Its tests: replace the helper `add_transition` with

```rust
    /// A transition of the old `kind` (its D1 recipe) on the cut at `at_ms` of track `track`.
    fn add_transition(p: &mut Project, track: usize, at_ms: i64, dur_ms: i64, kind: TransitionKind) -> TransitionId {
        let (from, to, _) = p.sequence().cut_near(p.sequence().tracks[track].id, Time::from_millis(at_ms), Time::ZERO).expect("a cut there");
        let tr = Transition::legacy(kind, from, to, Time::from_millis(dur_ms));
        let id = tr.id;
        p.sequence_mut().transitions.push(tr);
        id
    }

    /// A dissolve between two clips that need not meet (a stale one, as a hand-edited file may hold).
    fn pair_transition(p: &mut Project, from: ClipId, to: ClipId, dur_ms: i64) -> TransitionId {
        let tr = Transition::legacy(TransitionKind::CrossDissolve, from, to, Time::from_millis(dur_ms));
        let id = tr.id;
        p.sequence_mut().transitions.push(tr);
        id
    }
```

### Task 4: `SetTransition` / `UpdateTransition` / `RemoveTransition`, and transitions following every clip command (`kadr-timeline`)

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/timeline/src/commands.rs` (variants, labels, `EditError::{NotACut, TransitionNotFound}`, `error_key`, `apply` → `apply_one` + reconcile, the three commands)
- Create: `crates/timeline/tests/transition_edits.rs`
- Modify (callers of the interim `AddTransition`): `crates/ai/src/command.rs` (`AddTransition` arm), `apps/editor/src/timeline_ui.rs` (`add_dissolve`)
- Modify: `crates/i18n/locales/ru.json`, `en.json`; `ARCHITECTURE.md` row `kadr-timeline` (:61)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 3's `Transition`, `Sequence::{reconcile_transitions, transition_cut}`, `kadr_effects::transition_definition`; Task 2's `transition_params`, `Target::Cut`.
- Produces:
  - `EditCommand::SetTransition { from: ClipId, to: ClipId, recipe: String, version: u16, params: BTreeMap<String, ParamValue>, duration: Time }`, `EditCommand::UpdateTransition { id: TransitionId, params: BTreeMap<String, ParamValue>, duration: Option<Time> }` (empty `params` = unchanged), `EditCommand::RemoveTransition(TransitionId)`; `AddTransition` is gone.
  - `EditError::NotACut` (`err.edit.not_a_cut`), `EditError::TransitionNotFound` (`err.edit.transition_not_found`).
  - Labels `cmd.set_transition`, `cmd.update_transition`, `cmd.remove_transition`; named ones for callers: `cmd.transition_named` («Переход: {name}»), `cmd.update_transition_named` («Изменение перехода: {name}»).
  - `EditCommand::apply` reconciles transitions after every successful top-level command (D2); `Batch` children run unreconciled until the batch ends.

```rust
//! Transition commands (effects spec §7, D3) and transitions following
//! their cuts through every clip command (D2): one undo entry each, and
//! undo restores clips and transitions together.

use kadr_core::{ClipId, FrameRate, MediaInfo, MediaKind, Time, TimeRange, TransitionId, VideoInfo};
use kadr_project::*;
use kadr_timeline::*;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

fn s(x: i64) -> Time {
    Time::from_secs(x)
}
fn ms(x: i64) -> Time {
    Time::from_millis(x)
}

fn video(dur: Time) -> MediaInfo {
    let v = VideoInfo { width: 1920, height: 1080, frame_rate: Some(FrameRate::FPS_25), variable_frame_rate: false, codec: "h264".into(), pixel_format: "yuv420p".into(), rotation: 0, sar: (1, 1), color: None };
    MediaInfo { kind: MediaKind::Video, duration: dur, container: "mp4".into(), size_bytes: 0, video: Some(v), audio: None, timecode: None }
}

/// V1: a 0..4 s, b 4..8 s, c 8..12 s (each its own 60 s medium, source
/// from 10 s), an empty V2 above, A1; a dissolve on a|b, a wipe on b|c.
fn setup() -> (Project, EditEngine, [ClipId; 3], [TransitionId; 2]) {
    let mut p = Project::new("t");
    p.sequence_mut().frame_rate = FrameRate::FPS_25;
    p.sequence_mut().tracks.insert(1, Track::new(TrackKind::Video, "V2"));
    let mut ids = vec![];
    for (i, start) in [0, 4, 8].into_iter().enumerate() {
        let a = MediaAsset::new(format!("m{i}.mp4"), video(s(60)));
        let c = Clip::new(a.id, format!("c{i}"), TimeRange::new(s(10), s(14)), s(start));
        ids.push(c.id);
        p.assets.push(a);
        p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips.push(c);
    }
    let ids = [ids[0], ids[1], ids[2]];
    let (t1, t2) = (Transition::legacy(TransitionKind::CrossDissolve, ids[0], ids[1], s(1)), Transition::legacy(TransitionKind::Wipe, ids[1], ids[2], s(1)));
    let tids = [t1.id, t2.id];
    p.sequence_mut().transitions = vec![t1, t2];
    (p, EditEngine::new(), ids, tids)
}

/// Every transition as (id, from, to, cut): the cut `None` where its clips do not meet.
fn cuts(p: &Project) -> Vec<(TransitionId, ClipId, ClipId, Option<Time>)> {
    let seq = p.sequence();
    seq.transitions.iter().map(|t| (t.id, t.from, t.to, seq.transition_cut(t).map(|(_, at)| at))).collect()
}

/// Which of `tids` are still there, in order.
fn kept(p: &Project, tids: [TransitionId; 2]) -> Vec<TransitionId> {
    p.sequence().transitions.iter().map(|t| t.id).filter(|id| tids.contains(id)).collect()
}

fn set(from: ClipId, to: ClipId, recipe: &str, params: &[(&str, ParamValue)], duration: Time) -> EditCommand {
    let version = kadr_effects::recipe(recipe).map_or(1, |d| d.version);
    EditCommand::SetTransition { from, to, recipe: recipe.into(), version, params: params.iter().map(|(k, v)| (k.to_string(), v.clone())).collect(), duration }
}

type Cmd = dyn Fn(&Project, [ClipId; 3]) -> EditCommand;
type Want = dyn Fn(&Project, [ClipId; 3], [TransitionId; 2]);

/// D2, review focus 2: every command that changes clips leaves no
/// transition between clips that do not meet — in its own one undo entry —
/// keeps (re-anchoring if need be) those whose clips still meet, and undo
/// restores the sequence exactly.
#[test]
fn transitions_follow_every_clip_command() {
    let check = |name: &str, cmd: &Cmd, want: &Want| {
        let (mut p, mut e, ids, tids) = setup();
        let before = p.sequence().clone();
        let entries = p.history_log.len();
        let c = cmd(&p, ids);
        e.execute(&mut p, c).unwrap_or_else(|err| panic!("{name}: {err}"));
        assert_eq!(p.history_log.len(), entries + 1, "{name}: one undo entry");
        assert!(cuts(&p).iter().all(|c| c.3.is_some()), "{name}: a transition between clips that do not meet: {:?}", cuts(&p));
        want(&p, ids, tids);
        e.undo(&mut p);
        assert_eq!(p.sequence(), &before, "{name}: undo restores clips and transitions together");
    };
    check("split of from", &|_, _| EditCommand::Split { at: s(2), clips: None }, &|p, [a, b, _], [t1, _]| {
        let t = p.sequence().transition(t1).unwrap();
        assert_ne!(t.from, a, "re-anchored to the right half");
        assert_eq!((p.sequence().clip(t.from).unwrap().timeline_out, t.to), (s(4), b));
    });
    check("split of to", &|_, [_, b, _]| EditCommand::Split { at: s(6), clips: Some(vec![b]) }, &|p, [a, b, _], [t1, t2]| {
        let (x, y) = (p.sequence().transition(t1).unwrap(), p.sequence().transition(t2).unwrap());
        assert_eq!((x.from, x.to), (a, b), "b keeps its start");
        assert_ne!(y.from, b, "b|c follows b's right half");
    });
    check("delete from", &|_, [a, _, _]| EditCommand::DeleteClips { clips: vec![a], ripple: false }, &|p, _, [t1, t2]| assert_eq!(kept(p, [t1, t2]), vec![t2]));
    check("ripple delete to", &|_, [_, b, _]| EditCommand::DeleteClips { clips: vec![b], ripple: true }, &|p, _, tids| assert!(kept(p, tids).is_empty(), "a and c now meet, without a transition"));
    check("ripple delete after the pair", &|_, [_, _, c]| EditCommand::DeleteClips { clips: vec![c], ripple: true }, &|p, _, [t1, t2]| assert_eq!(kept(p, [t1, t2]), vec![t1]));
    check("delete range over a cut", &|_, _| EditCommand::DeleteRange { range: TimeRange::new(s(3), s(5)), ripple: false }, &|p, _, [t1, t2]| assert_eq!(kept(p, [t1, t2]), vec![t2]));
    check("ripple delete range inside a clip", &|_, _| EditCommand::DeleteRange { range: TimeRange::new(s(9), s(10)), ripple: true }, &|p, _, tids| assert_eq!(kept(p, tids), tids.to_vec(), "c lost a middle part; b|c still meets"));
    check("move to away", &|_, [_, _, c]| EditCommand::MoveClips { clips: vec![c], delta: s(10), track_delta: 0 }, &|p, _, [t1, t2]| assert_eq!(kept(p, [t1, t2]), vec![t1]));
    check("move the pairs together to V2", &|_, ids| EditCommand::MoveClips { clips: ids.to_vec(), delta: s(20), track_delta: 1 }, &|p, _, tids| {
        assert_eq!(kept(p, tids), tids.to_vec());
        assert_eq!(cuts(p).iter().map(|c| c.3).collect::<Vec<_>>(), [Some(s(24)), Some(s(28))]);
        assert_eq!(p.sequence().transition_cut(&p.sequence().transitions[0]).unwrap().0, 1, "on V2 now");
    });
    check("trim opening a gap", &|_, [a, _, _]| EditCommand::TrimClip { clip: a, edge: TrimEdge::End, to: s(3), ripple: false }, &|p, _, [t1, t2]| assert_eq!(kept(p, [t1, t2]), vec![t2]));
    check("ripple trim keeping the pair", &|_, [a, _, _]| EditCommand::TrimClip { clip: a, edge: TrimEdge::End, to: s(3), ripple: true }, &|p, _, tids| {
        assert_eq!(kept(p, tids), tids.to_vec());
        assert_eq!(cuts(p)[0].3, Some(s(3)), "the cut moved with the trim");
    });
    check("trim start of to", &|_, [_, b, _]| EditCommand::TrimClip { clip: b, edge: TrimEdge::Start, to: s(5), ripple: false }, &|p, _, [t1, t2]| assert_eq!(kept(p, [t1, t2]), vec![t2]));
    check("speed of from", &|_, [a, _, _]| EditCommand::SetClipProperty { clip: a, prop: ClipProperty::Speed(2.0) }, &|p, _, [t1, t2]| assert_eq!(kept(p, [t1, t2]), vec![t2], "a got shorter: a gap"));
    check(
        "insert at the cut",
        &|p, _| {
            let mut c = Clip::new(p.assets[2].id, "x", TimeRange::new(s(30), s(32)), s(4));
            c.id = ClipId::new();
            EditCommand::InsertClip { track: p.sequence().tracks[0].id, clip: c, mode: InsertMode::Insert }
        },
        &|p, _, [t1, t2]| assert_eq!(kept(p, [t1, t2]), vec![t2], "a clip between a and b"),
    );
    check("switching b off keeps both", &|_, [_, b, _]| EditCommand::SetClipProperty { clip: b, prop: ClipProperty::Enabled(false) }, &|p, _, tids| assert_eq!(kept(p, tids), tids.to_vec()));
    check(
        "a batch splitting from twice",
        &|_, _| EditCommand::Batch { label: "x".into(), commands: vec![EditCommand::Split { at: s(1), clips: None }, EditCommand::Split { at: s(3), clips: None }] },
        &|p, _, [t1, _]| assert_eq!(p.sequence().clip(p.sequence().transition(t1).unwrap().from).unwrap().timeline_in, s(3), "the last piece"),
    );
}

/// D3: a recipe on a cut — validated, its missing parameters at their
/// defaults — replacing the cut's transition in place; the same again is
/// no entry.
#[test]
fn set_transition_validates_fills_defaults_and_replaces_in_place() {
    let (mut p, mut e, [a, b, c], [t1, _]) = setup();
    let entries = p.history_log.len();
    e.execute(&mut p, set(a, b, "smooth_push", &[], ms(500))).unwrap();
    let t = p.sequence().transition(t1).expect("the cut's transition keeps its id");
    assert_eq!((t.recipe.as_str(), t.params["direction"].clone(), t.duration), ("smooth_push", ParamValue::Choice("left".into()), ms(500)));
    assert_eq!(p.sequence().transitions.len(), 2, "one per cut");
    assert_eq!(e.execute(&mut p, set(a, b, "smooth_push", &[("direction", ParamValue::Choice("left".into()))], ms(500))), Err(EditError::NoOp), "the same again");
    assert_eq!(p.history_log.len(), entries + 1);
    e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::RemoveTransition(t1)).unwrap();
    e.execute(&mut p, set(a, b, "light_sweep", &[("color", ParamValue::Choice("cold".into()))], s(1))).unwrap();
    let fresh = p.sequence().transitions.iter().find(|t| t.to == b).unwrap();
    assert_ne!(fresh.id, t1, "a cut without one gets a new transition");
    assert_eq!(fresh.params["direction"], ParamValue::Choice("left".into()), "defaults filled in");
    assert_eq!(e.execute(&mut p, set(a, b, "iris", &[], s(1))), Err(EditError::UnknownRecipe("iris".into())));
    assert_eq!(e.execute(&mut p, set(a, b, "look", &[], s(1))), Err(EditError::EffectTarget), "a clip recipe is no transition");
    assert!(matches!(e.execute(&mut p, set(a, b, "wipe", &[("softness", ParamValue::Number(3.0))], s(1))), Err(EditError::InvalidParam(m)) if m.contains("softness")));
    assert_eq!(e.execute(&mut p, set(a, b, "wipe", &[], ms(70))), Err(EditError::InvalidRange), "under two frames (80 ms at 25 fps)");
    assert_eq!(e.execute(&mut p, set(a, c, "wipe", &[], s(1))), Err(EditError::NotACut));
    assert_eq!(e.execute(&mut p, set(b, a, "wipe", &[], s(1))), Err(EditError::NotACut));
    let mut audio = p.clone();
    let clips = std::mem::take(&mut audio.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips);
    let a1 = audio.sequence().tracks.iter().position(|t| t.kind == TrackKind::Audio).unwrap();
    audio.sequence_mut().tracks[a1].clips = clips;
    assert_eq!(EditEngine::new().execute(&mut audio, set(a, b, "wipe", &[], s(1))), Err(EditError::KindMismatch), "no transitions on sound (D11)");
}

#[test]
fn update_transition_merges_parameters_and_length_and_never_changes_the_unknown() {
    let (mut p, mut e, _, [t1, t2]) = setup();
    e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::UpdateTransition { id: t2, params: BTreeMap::from([("softness".to_string(), ParamValue::Number(0.5))]), duration: Some(ms(600)) }).unwrap();
    let t = p.sequence().transition(t2).unwrap();
    assert_eq!((t.params["softness"].clone(), t.params["direction"].clone(), t.duration), (ParamValue::Number(0.5), ParamValue::Choice("left".into()), ms(600)), "merged");
    assert_eq!(e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::UpdateTransition { id: t2, params: BTreeMap::new(), duration: Some(ms(600)) }), Err(EditError::NoOp));
    assert!(matches!(e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::UpdateTransition { id: t1, params: BTreeMap::from([("style".to_string(), ParamValue::Choice("sepia".into()))]), duration: None }), Err(EditError::InvalidParam(_))));
    assert_eq!(e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::UpdateTransition { id: t1, params: BTreeMap::new(), duration: Some(ms(40)) }), Err(EditError::InvalidRange));
    assert_eq!(e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::UpdateTransition { id: TransitionId::new(), params: BTreeMap::new(), duration: Some(s(1)) }), Err(EditError::TransitionNotFound));
    p.sequence_mut().transitions[0].recipe = "iris".into();
    assert_eq!(e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::UpdateTransition { id: t1, params: BTreeMap::new(), duration: Some(s(2)) }), Err(EditError::UnknownRecipe("iris".into())), "a newer Kadr's transition is only removed");
    e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::RemoveTransition(t1)).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::RemoveTransition(t1)), Err(EditError::NoOp));
}

#[test]
fn a_locked_track_keeps_its_transitions() {
    let (mut p, mut e, [a, b, _], [t1, _]) = setup();
    p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].locked = true;
    assert!(matches!(e.execute(&mut p, set(a, b, "wipe", &[], s(1))), Err(EditError::TrackLocked(_))));
    assert!(matches!(e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::UpdateTransition { id: t1, params: BTreeMap::new(), duration: Some(s(2)) }), Err(EditError::TrackLocked(_))));
    assert!(matches!(e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::RemoveTransition(t1)), Err(EditError::TrackLocked(_))));
}

#[test]
fn transition_commands_have_their_labels() {
    let (_, _, [a, b, _], [t1, _]) = setup();
    assert_eq!(set(a, b, "wipe", &[], s(1)).label(), "cmd.set_transition");
```

```rust
    /// duration of at least two frames (the evaluator clamps it to both
    /// clips). The cut's transition, if any, is replaced in place (same

    SetTransition { from: ClipId, to: ClipId, recipe: String, version: u16, params: BTreeMap<String, ParamValue>, duration: Time },
    /// Sets the given parameters (validated, merged into the stored ones;
```

```rust
    /// Applies the command, then keeps every transition on its cut (D2):
    /// what a command does to clips it does to their transitions, in the
    /// same undo entry (`Sequence::reconcile_transitions`). A `Batch`'s

    pub fn apply(&self, seq: &mut Sequence, ctx: &EditContext) -> Result<(), EditError> {

        }
        Ok(())
    }
```

Replace the interim `AddTransition` arm and the `RemoveTransition` arm with:

```rust
            EditCommand::SetTransition { from, to, recipe, version, params, duration } => {
                let def = kadr_effects::recipe(recipe).filter(|d| d.version == *version).ok_or_else(|| EditError::UnknownRecipe(recipe.clone()))?;
                if def.target != kadr_effects::Target::Cut {
                    return Err(EditError::EffectTarget);
                }
                kadr_effects::validate(def.params, params).map_err(EditError::InvalidParam)?;
                if *duration < fr.frame_to_time(2) {
                    return Err(EditError::InvalidRange);
                }
                let (ti, ci) = seq.locate_clip(*from).ok_or(EditError::ClipNotFound)?;
                let track = &seq.tracks[ti];
                if track.kind != TrackKind::Video {
                    return Err(EditError::KindMismatch);
                }
                if track.locked {
                    return Err(EditError::TrackLocked(track.name.clone()));
                }
                if !track.clips.get(ci + 1).is_some_and(|n| n.id == *to && n.timeline_in == track.clips[ci].timeline_out) {
                    return Err(EditError::NotACut);
                }
                let mut values = kadr_effects::transition_params(def);
                values.extend(params.iter().map(|(k, v)| (k.clone(), v.clone())));
                match seq.transitions.iter_mut().find(|t| t.to == *to) {
                    Some(t) => {
                        let next = Transition { id: t.id, ..Transition::new(def.id, def.version, values, *from, *to, *duration) };
                        if *t == next {
                            return Err(EditError::NoOp);
                        }
                        *t = next;
                    }
                    None => seq.transitions.push(Transition::new(def.id, def.version, values, *from, *to, *duration)),
                }
                Ok(())
            }
            EditCommand::UpdateTransition { id, params, duration } => {
                let i = seq.transitions.iter().position(|t| t.id == *id).ok_or(EditError::TransitionNotFound)?;
                transition_track_unlocked(seq, &seq.transitions[i])?;
                let tr = &seq.transitions[i];
                // A recipe this build cannot run cannot be validated, so it is not changed (spec §7).
                let def = kadr_effects::transition_definition(tr).ok_or_else(|| EditError::UnknownRecipe(tr.recipe.clone()))?;
                kadr_effects::validate(def.params, params).map_err(EditError::InvalidParam)?;
                if duration.is_some_and(|d| d < fr.frame_to_time(2)) {
                    return Err(EditError::InvalidRange);
                }
                let mut next = tr.clone();
                next.params.extend(params.iter().map(|(k, v)| (k.clone(), v.clone())));
                if let Some(d) = duration {
                    next.duration = *d;
                }
                if next == *tr {
                    return Err(EditError::NoOp);
                }
                seq.transitions[i] = next;
                Ok(())
            }
            EditCommand::RemoveTransition(id) => {
                let Some(i) = seq.transitions.iter().position(|t| t.id == *id) else { return Err(EditError::NoOp) };
                transition_track_unlocked(seq, &seq.transitions[i])?;
                seq.transitions.remove(i);
                Ok(())
            }
```

and after `unlocked_mut`:

```rust
/// A transition is changed only on an unlocked track (a lock protects its
/// cuts too); one whose clips are gone can always go.
fn transition_track_unlocked(seq: &Sequence, tr: &Transition) -> Result<(), EditError> {
    match seq.locate_clip(tr.to).map(|(ti, _)| &seq.tracks[ti]) {
        Some(t) if t.locked => Err(EditError::TrackLocked(t.name.clone())),
        _ => Ok(()),
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing evaluator tests** — in `crates/timeline/tests/effects_eval.rs` change the `kadr_scene` import to `use kadr_scene::{BlendMode, Effect, Generator, GradientShape, Layer, LayerContent, LayerId, OutputSpec, Placement, RenderQuality, SizeU, TransitionOp, Vec2};` and append:

```rust
fn layers(p: &Project, t: Time) -> Vec<Layer> {
    evaluate(p, p.sequence(), t, &OutputSpec::new(SizeU::new(960, 540), RenderQuality::PreviewHigh)).layers
}

/// The clip of `project(first)` (10..20 s) and b (20..30 s, the same
/// medium from 50 s) with `recipe` (its defaults and `set`) on their cut, 1 s.
fn with_transition(recipe: &str, set: &[(&str, ParamValue)], first: Vec<AppliedEffect>) -> (Project, kadr_core::ClipId, kadr_core::ClipId, kadr_core::TransitionId) {
    let (mut p, a) = project(first);
    let b = Clip::new(p.assets[0].id, "b", TimeRange::new(Time::from_secs(50), Time::from_secs(60)), Time::from_secs(20));
    let bid = b.id;
    p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips.push(b);
    let def = kadr_effects::recipe(recipe).unwrap();
    let mut values = kadr_effects::transition_params(def);
    values.extend(set.iter().map(|(k, v)| (k.to_string(), v.clone())));
    let tr = Transition::new(recipe, def.version, values, a, bid, Time::from_secs(1));
    let tid = tr.id;
    p.sequence_mut().transitions.push(tr);
    (p, a, bid, tid)
}

/// D7, review focus 4: Засветка is two Screen generator layers right above
/// its clip, on the clip's own picture — its placement and crop, also on a
/// turned, cropped picture-in-picture — fading with it, with ids of their own.
#[test]
fn a_light_leak_lies_on_the_clips_picture() {
    let (mut p, id) = project(vec![fx("light_leak", &[])]);
    let c = clip_mut(&mut p, id);
    (c.transform.scale, c.transform.x, c.transform.rotation_deg, c.transform.crop_left, c.transform.opacity) = (0.4, 500.0, 7.0, 0.1, 0.8);
    let l = layers(&p, Time::from_secs(15));
    assert_eq!(l.len(), 3);
    let (clip, overlays) = (&l[0], &l[1..]);
    assert_eq!(clip.id, LayerId::from(id));
    for o in overlays {
        assert!(matches!(o.content, LayerContent::Generator(_)));
        assert_eq!((o.placement, o.crop, o.blend), (clip.placement, clip.crop, BlendMode::Screen));
        assert!(o.opacity > 0.0 && o.opacity <= clip.opacity, "the leak fades with its clip: {} vs {}", o.opacity, clip.opacity);
        assert!(o.effects.is_empty() && o.id != clip.id);
    }
    assert_ne!(overlays[0].id, overlays[1].id);
}

#[test]
fn a_light_leak_on_a_vertical_clip_stays_on_its_picture() {
    let (mut p, _) = project(vec![fx("light_leak", &[("variant", ParamValue::Choice("2".into()))])]);
    p.assets[0].info.video.as_mut().unwrap().rotation = 90;
    let l = layers(&p, Time::from_secs(19));
    assert_eq!(l[0].placement.size, Vec2::new(607.5, 1080.0), "a phone video, pillarboxed");
    let LayerContent::Generator(Generator::Gradient(g)) = &l[2].content else { panic!("the glow") };
    let GradientShape::Radial { centre, radius } = g.shape else { panic!() };
    assert!((0.0..=607.5).contains(&centre.x) && (0.0..=1080.0).contains(&centre.y), "{centre:?}");
    assert!((radius as f64 - kadr_effects::leak::GLOW_RADIUS * 607.5f64.hypot(1080.0)).abs() < 0.01);
    assert_eq!((l[2].placement, l[2].crop), (l[0].placement, l[0].crop), "drawn only on the picture, never on the bars");
}

#[test]
fn a_light_leak_at_zero_switched_off_or_unknown_adds_no_layer() {
    let mut off = fx("light_leak", &[]);
    off.enabled = false;
    let mut newer = fx("light_leak", &[]);
    newer.version = 2;
    for e in [fx("light_leak", &[("strength", ParamValue::Number(0.0))]), off, newer] {
        let (p, id) = project(vec![e.clone()]);
        assert_eq!(layers(&p, Time::from_secs(15)), layers(&without_effects(&p, id), Time::from_secs(15)), "{e:?}: the clip alone, unchanged");
    }
}

/// A split keeps the light: both halves carry the effect's id and its
/// progress range, so the overlays are the same at every frame.
#[test]
fn a_split_keeps_the_leak_where_it_was() {
    let (p, id) = project(vec![fx("light_leak", &[])]);
    let mut q = p.clone();
    let c = q.sequence().clip(id).unwrap().clone();
    let (l, r) = split_clip(&c, Time::from_secs(14), &mut LinkRemap::default());
    q.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips = vec![l, r];
    for ms in [12_000, 13_966, 14_000, 17_500] {
        let (a, b) = (layers(&p, Time::from_millis(ms)), layers(&q, Time::from_millis(ms)));
        assert_eq!(a[1..], b[1..], "{ms} ms: the same light on both halves");
    }
}

/// Сдвиг (D5, refinement 5): both sides move one canvas length, the
/// outgoing clip's overlays with it, mixed by `Over`.
#[test]
fn a_push_moves_both_sides_and_their_overlays() {
    let (p, a, b, tid) = with_transition("smooth_push", &[("direction", ParamValue::Choice("left".into()))], vec![fx("light_leak", &[])]);
    let l = layers(&p, Time::from_secs(20));
    assert_eq!((l.len(), l[0].id), (1, LayerId::from(tid)));
    let LayerContent::Transition(t) = &l[0].content else { panic!() };
    assert_eq!(t.op, TransitionOp::Over);
    assert_eq!(t.from.len(), 3, "a and its leak's two overlays");
    assert!(t.from.iter().all(|x| x.placement.position == t.from[0].placement.position), "the overlays move with their clip");
    let (fx_, tx) = (t.from[0].placement.position.x, t.to[0].placement.position.x);
    assert!(fx_ < 960.0 && tx > 960.0 && ((tx - fx_) - 1920.0).abs() < 0.01, "edge to edge, one canvas apart: {fx_} {tx}");
    assert_eq!((t.from[0].id, t.to[0].id), (LayerId::from(a), LayerId::from(b)));
}

#[test]
fn a_sweep_draws_its_band_above_the_mix_on_the_whole_canvas() {
    let (p, _, _, tid) = with_transition("light_sweep", &[], vec![]);
    let l = layers(&p, Time::from_secs(20));
    assert_eq!(l.len(), 2);
    assert!(matches!(l[0].content, LayerContent::Transition(_)));
    assert_eq!(l[1].id, LayerId::derived(LayerId::from(tid).0, 0));
    assert!(matches!(l[1].content, LayerContent::Generator(Generator::Gradient(_))));
    let fill = Placement::fill(SizeU::new(1920, 1080));
    assert_eq!((l[1].blend, l[1].placement, l[1].crop), (BlendMode::Screen, fill, fill.full_crop()));
    assert_eq!(layers(&p, Time::from_secs(19)).len(), 1, "outside the window: the clip alone");
}

#[test]
fn film_lifts_the_clip_layers_of_both_sides() {
    let (p, _, _, _) = with_transition("clean_fade", &[("style", ParamValue::Choice("film".into()))], vec![]);
    let l = layers(&p, Time::from_secs(20));
    let LayerContent::Transition(t) = &l[0].content else { panic!() };
    for side in [&t.from, &t.to] {
        assert!(matches!(side[0].effects.last(), Some(Effect::ColorAdjust(c)) if c.exposure > 0.3), "{:?}", side[0].effects);
    }
}

#[test]
fn a_transition_this_build_cannot_run_is_a_hard_cut() {
    let (mut p, a, b, _) = with_transition("wipe", &[], vec![]);
    p.sequence_mut().transitions[0].version = 9;

    assert_eq!(layers(&p, Time::from_millis(20_100))[0].id, LayerId::from(b));
}
```
