- [ ] **Step 4: Layers per track** — in `crates/timeline/src/scene.rs`: imports `use kadr_effects::{Overlay, Params, RecipeDef, TransitionBuild, TransitionCtx};`. In `evaluate` replace the loop body with `track_layers(project, seq, track, t, canvas, out.size, &mut scene.layers);`. Replace `track_layer` and the interim `transition_layer` with:

```rust
/// What `track` shows at `t`, bottom to top (D7): a transition and its
/// overlay, or the clip under `t` and its effects' overlays.
fn track_layers(project: &Project, seq: &Sequence, track: &Track, t: Time, canvas: SizeU, output: SizeU, out: &mut Vec<Layer>) {
    if transition_layers(project, seq, track, t, canvas, output, out) {
        return;
    }
    if let Some(clip) = track.clip_at(t).filter(|c| c.enabled) {
        out.extend(clip_layers(project, clip, t, canvas, seq.frame_rate.frame_duration()));
    }
}

/// The transition on `track` at `t` ([`active_transition`]) as its
/// recipe builds it at the window's linear progress (D5): each side is its
/// clip's layers — the clip's own, its overlays — shifted and coloured by
/// the build, mixed by its op; the build's overlay above. A side that draws
/// nothing at `t` is an empty, transparent side, the other still mixes
/// against it (keyframes spec §3.5); with both empty there is no transition
/// (`false`: the track shows its clip, which draws nothing either).
fn transition_layers(project: &Project, seq: &Sequence, track: &Track, t: Time, canvas: SizeU, output: SizeU, out: &mut Vec<Layer>) -> bool {
    let Some((tr, def, outgoing, incoming, w)) = active_transition(seq, track, t) else { return false };
    let progress = ((t - w.start).flicks() as f64 / w.duration().flicks() as f64).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
    let params = Params::resolve(def.params, &tr.params, |_| None);
    let Some(build) = def.compile_cut(&params, &TransitionCtx { progress, canvas, output, seed: kadr_effects::transition_seed(tr.id) }) else { return false };
    let frame = seq.frame_rate.frame_duration();
    let from = shift_side(clip_layers(project, outgoing, t, canvas, frame), build.from_shift, &build.from_effects, canvas);
    let to = shift_side(clip_layers(project, incoming, t, canvas, frame), build.to_shift, &build.to_effects, canvas);
    if from.is_empty() && to.is_empty() {
        return false;
    }
    out.extend(transition_stack(LayerId::from(tr.id), build, from, to, canvas));
    true
}

/// A transition's frame as layers — the evaluator's and the bench's one
/// construction: the mix (full-canvas placement and crop, opacity 1,
/// Normal, no effects, as the contract expects) of `from` and `to`, then
/// the build's overlay over the whole canvas (`LayerId::derived(id, 0)`).
pub fn transition_stack(id: LayerId, build: TransitionBuild, from: Vec<Layer>, to: Vec<Layer>, canvas: SizeU) -> Vec<Layer> {
    let fill = Placement::fill(canvas);
    let mix = TransitionLayer { op: build.op, progress: build.progress, from, to };
    let mut out = vec![Layer { id, content: LayerContent::Transition(Box::new(mix)), crop: fill.full_crop(), placement: fill, opacity: 1.0, blend: BlendMode::Normal, effects: vec![] }];
    if let Some(o) = build.overlay {
        out.push(Layer { id: LayerId::derived(id.0, 0), content: LayerContent::Generator(o.generator), crop: fill.full_crop(), placement: fill, opacity: o.opacity.clamp(0.0, 1.0), blend: o.blend, effects: vec![] });
    }
    out
}

/// A side of a transition (D5): `layers` — a clip's own layer first, then
/// its overlays — moved `shift` canvas widths and heights together, with
/// `effects` appended to the clip's own layer.
pub fn shift_side(mut layers: Vec<Layer>, shift: (f64, f64), effects: &[Effect], canvas: SizeU) -> Vec<Layer> {
    let (dx, dy) = ((shift.0 * canvas.w as f64) as f32, (shift.1 * canvas.h as f64) as f32);
    for (i, l) in layers.iter_mut().enumerate() {
        l.placement.position.x += dx;
        l.placement.position.y += dy;
        if i == 0 {
            l.effects.extend_from_slice(effects);
        }
    }
    layers
}

/// An overlay of a clip's effect as a layer on `base`, the clip's own
/// (D7, refinement 7): the clip's placement and crop — the light lies on
/// the picture and nowhere else — its opacity times the clip's (fades and
/// opacity take it along), its blend, no effects.
pub fn overlay_layer(base: &Layer, o: Overlay, id: LayerId) -> Layer {
    Layer { id, content: LayerContent::Generator(o.generator), placement: base.placement, crop: base.crop, opacity: (o.opacity * base.opacity).clamp(0.0, 1.0), blend: o.blend, effects: vec![] }
}

/// A clip's layers at `t` (D7): its own ([`clip_layer`]) and, right above
/// it, its effects' overlays — none when the clip draws nothing.
pub fn clip_layers(project: &Project, clip: &Clip, t: Time, canvas: SizeU, frame: Time) -> Vec<Layer> {
    let Some((layer, overlays)) = clip_parts(project, clip, t, canvas, frame) else { return vec![] };
    let lights: Vec<Layer> = overlays.into_iter().map(|(id, o)| overlay_layer(&layer, o, id)).collect();
    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(1 + lights.len());
    out.push(layer);
    out.extend(lights);
    out
}
```

Rename the body of `clip_layer` into `fn clip_parts(project: &Project, clip: &Clip, t: Time, canvas: SizeU, frame: Time) -> Option<(Layer, Vec<(LayerId, Overlay)>)>` (doc: "One clip at timeline time `t` … and its effects' overlays (not yet placed)"): its `let (placement, colour) = crate::fx::apply_effects(…);` becomes `let applied = crate::fx::apply_effects(clip, t, canvas, placement, crate::fx::crop_window(&st.transform));`, `effects.extend(applied.colour);`, `placement: applied.placement,` and it returns `Some((Layer { … }, applied.overlays))`. Then:

```rust
/// One clip at timeline time `t` (which may lie outside the clip, for
/// transition handles: the source time is extrapolated, then clamped) —
/// its own layer, without its effects' overlays ([`clip_layers`] has both).
/// `frame` is the sequence's frame duration (the fade-out ends one frame
/// before the clip's out-point).
pub fn clip_layer(project: &Project, clip: &Clip, t: Time, canvas: SizeU, frame: Time) -> Option<Layer> {
    clip_parts(project, clip, t, canvas, frame).map(|(l, _)| l)
}
```

In `cull`'s doc add the sentence "A generator layer (an overlay, a transition's band) never occludes: it is drawn over what is below it."

- [ ] **Step 6: Pixels end to end** — create `crates/project-scenes/tests/transitions_pixels.rs`:

```rust
//! E3 end to end, evaluator → renderer (effects spec §9): no seam in a
//! push, the leak never changes what is culled or copied, and every
//! transition's picture pinned by a golden.

use kadr_core::color::AlphaMode;
use kadr_core::{AssetId, ColorInfo, CpuFrame, FrameRate, MediaInfo, MediaKind, Time, TimeRange, VideoInfo};
use kadr_project::{Clip, MediaAsset, ParamValue, Project, Transition};
use kadr_render::{CpuRenderer, CpuTarget, LayerInput, MissingReason, PreparedFrame, RenderInputs, RenderStats, RenderTarget, Renderer};
use kadr_scene::{Layer, LayerContent, OutputSpec, RenderQuality, SizeU};
use kadr_timeline::scene::evaluate;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Arc;

const W: u32 = 64;
const H: u32 = 36;

type Frames = HashMap<AssetId, Arc<CpuFrame>>;

fn medium(name: &str) -> MediaAsset {
    let video = VideoInfo { width: W, height: H, frame_rate: Some(FrameRate::FPS_30), variable_frame_rate: false, codec: "h264".into(), pixel_format: "yuv420p".into(), rotation: 0, sar: (1, 1), color: None };
    MediaAsset::new(name, MediaInfo { kind: MediaKind::Video, duration: Time::from_secs(60), container: "mp4".into(), size_bytes: 0, video: Some(video), audio: None, timecode: None })
}

fn frame(px: impl Fn(u32, u32) -> [u8; 4]) -> Arc<CpuFrame> {
    let bytes = (0..H).flat_map(|y| (0..W).map(move |x| (x, y))).flat_map(|(x, y)| px(x, y)).collect();
    Arc::new(CpuFrame::from_rgba8(W, H, ColorInfo { alpha: AlphaMode::Opaque, ..ColorInfo::WORKING_SDR }, bytes))
}

/// a (0..2 s) then b (2..4 s) on V1 of a 64 × 36, 30 fps sequence, with
/// `recipe` (its defaults and `set`) on their cut for 1 s: the window is
/// frames 45..75.
fn pair(recipe: &str, set: &[(&str, ParamValue)], a_px: fn(u32, u32) -> [u8; 4], b_px: fn(u32, u32) -> [u8; 4]) -> (Project, Frames) {
    let mut p = Project::new("e3");
    let seq = p.sequence_mut();
    (seq.frame_rate, seq.width, seq.height) = (FrameRate::FPS_30, W, H);
    let (ma, mb) = (medium("a.mp4"), medium("b.mp4"));
    let ca = Clip::new(ma.id, "a", TimeRange::new(Time::from_secs(10), Time::from_secs(12)), Time::ZERO);
    let cb = Clip::new(mb.id, "b", TimeRange::new(Time::from_secs(20), Time::from_secs(22)), Time::from_secs(2));
    let def = kadr_effects::recipe(recipe).unwrap();
    let mut values = kadr_effects::transition_params(def);
    values.extend(set.iter().map(|(k, v)| (k.to_string(), v.clone())));
    p.sequence_mut().transitions.push(Transition::new(recipe, def.version, values, ca.id, cb.id, Time::from_secs(1)));
    p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips.extend([ca, cb]);
    let frames = HashMap::from([(ma.id, frame(a_px)), (mb.id, frame(b_px))]);
    p.assets.extend([ma, mb]);
    (p, frames)
}

fn inputs(layers: &[Layer], frames: &Frames) -> Vec<LayerInput> {
    layers
        .iter()
        .map(|l| match &l.content {
            LayerContent::Media { media, .. } => frames.get(&media.media).map_or(LayerInput::Missing(MissingReason::Offline), |f| LayerInput::Cpu(f.clone())),
            LayerContent::Transition(t) => LayerInput::Transition { from: inputs(&t.from, frames), to: inputs(&t.to, frames) },
            _ => LayerInput::None,
        })
        .collect()
}

fn render_at(p: &Project, frames: &Frames, t: Time, out: SizeU) -> (Vec<u8>, RenderStats) {
    let scene = evaluate(p, p.sequence(), t, &OutputSpec::new(out, RenderQuality::Export));
    let ins = RenderInputs { layers: inputs(&scene.layers, frames) };
    let mut buf = vec![0u8; (out.w * out.h * 4) as usize];
    let stats = CpuRenderer::new().render(&PreparedFrame { scene: &scene, inputs: &ins }, &mut RenderTarget::Cpu(CpuTarget::packed(out.w, out.h, &mut buf))).unwrap();
    (buf, stats)
}

/// Review focus 5, refinement 4: two opaque full-frame white pictures
/// pushed edge to edge leave no seam — inside the canvas (a pixel off its
/// edges) every pixel stays white at any output size and progress; a seam
/// would let the black background through.
#[test]
fn a_push_leaves_no_seam() {
    let white: fn(u32, u32) -> [u8; 4] = |_, _| [255, 255, 255, 255];
    for dir in ["left", "right", "up", "down"] {
        let (p, frames) = pair("smooth_push", &[("direction", ParamValue::Choice(dir.into()))], white, white);
        for (ow, oh) in [(64, 36), (50, 36), (96, 54), (37, 21), (160, 90)] {
            let k = (ow as f64 / W as f64).min(oh as f64 / H as f64);
            let (ox, oy) = ((ow as f64 - k * W as f64) / 2.0, (oh as f64 - k * H as f64) / 2.0);
            for n in [46, 51, 57, 60, 63, 70] {
                let (px, _) = render_at(&p, &frames, FrameRate::FPS_30.frame_to_time(n), SizeU::new(ow, oh));
                for y in 0..oh {
                    for x in 0..ow {
                        let (cx, cy) = (x as f64 + 0.5, y as f64 + 0.5);
                        if cx < ox + 1.0 || cx > ox + k * W as f64 - 1.0 || cy < oy + 1.0 || cy > oy + k * H as f64 - 1.0 {
                            continue; // the canvas's own edge, or a margin
                        }
                        let at = ((y * ow + x) * 4) as usize;
                        assert_eq!(px[at..at + 4], [255, 255, 255, 255], "{dir}, {ow}x{oh}, frame {n}: pixel ({x}, {y})");
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

/// Review focus 5: a Light Leak never changes what is culled or copied — an
/// opaque full-frame clip with a leak still hides the track below (it is
/// not decoded) and is still copied by the fast path, the light drawn over it.
#[test]
fn overlays_keep_occlusion_and_the_fast_path() {
    let mut p = Project::new("cull");
    let seq = p.sequence_mut();
    (seq.frame_rate, seq.width, seq.height) = (FrameRate::FPS_30, W, H);
    seq.tracks.insert(1, kadr_project::Track::new(kadr_project::TrackKind::Video, "V2"));
    let (low, top) = (medium("low.mp4"), medium("top.mp4"));
    p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips.push(Clip::new(low.id, "low", TimeRange::new(Time::ZERO, Time::from_secs(4)), Time::ZERO));
    let mut c = Clip::new(top.id, "top", TimeRange::new(Time::ZERO, Time::from_secs(4)), Time::ZERO);
    c.effects.push(kadr_effects::fresh(kadr_effects::recipe("light_leak").unwrap(), &c, Time::ZERO));
    p.sequence_mut().tracks[1].clips.push(c);
    let frames = HashMap::from([(low.id, frame(|x, _| [x as u8, 0, 0, 255])), (top.id, frame(|_, y| [0, (y * 7) as u8, 90, 255]))]);
    p.assets.extend([low, top]);
    let t = Time::from_secs(1);
    let scene = evaluate(&p, p.sequence(), t, &OutputSpec::new(SizeU::new(W, H), RenderQuality::Export));
    assert_eq!(scene.layers.len(), 3, "V1 culled: the top clip and its two lights");
    assert!(matches!(scene.layers[0].content, LayerContent::Media { .. }) && scene.layers[1..].iter().all(|l| matches!(l.content, LayerContent::Generator(_))));
    let (_, stats) = render_at(&p, &frames, t, SizeU::new(W, H));
    assert_eq!((stats.fast_paths, stats.passes, stats.layers_drawn), (1, 3, 3), "the clip copied, the light drawn over it");
    p.sequence_mut().tracks[1].clips[0].effects.clear();
    let (_, plain) = render_at(&p, &frames, t, SizeU::new(W, H));
    assert_eq!((plain.fast_paths, plain.passes), (1, 1));
}

fn golden_path(name: &str) -> PathBuf {
    PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("tests").join("golden").join(format!("{name}.png"))
}

fn read_png(path: &Path) -> Option<(u32, u32, Vec<u8>)> {
    let mut reader = png::Decoder::new(std::fs::File::open(path).ok()?).read_info().expect("golden PNG header");
    let mut buf = vec![0; reader.output_buffer_size()];
    let info = reader.next_frame(&mut buf).expect("golden PNG data");
    buf.truncate(info.buffer_size());
    Some((info.width, info.height, buf))
}

fn write_png(path: &Path, w: u32, h: u32, data: &[u8]) {
    std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
    let mut enc = png::Encoder::new(std::io::BufWriter::new(std::fs::File::create(path).unwrap()), w, h);
    enc.set_color(png::ColorType::Rgba);
    enc.set_depth(png::BitDepth::Eight);
    enc.write_header().unwrap().write_image_data(data).unwrap();
}

/// Every E3 transition (rows: Растворение «Плёнка», Через цвет white,
/// Сдвиг up, Световая волна cold to the right, Шторка down at softness ½)
/// a quarter, half and three quarters through its window (columns).
#[test]
fn e3_transitions() {
    let rows: Vec<(&str, Vec<(&str, ParamValue)>)> = vec![
        ("clean_fade", vec![("style", ParamValue::Choice("film".into()))]),
        ("fade_through_color", vec![("color", ParamValue::Choice("white".into()))]),
        ("smooth_push", vec![("direction", ParamValue::Choice("up".into()))]),
        ("light_sweep", vec![("direction", ParamValue::Choice("right".into())), ("color", ParamValue::Choice("cold".into()))]),
        ("wipe", vec![("direction", ParamValue::Choice("down".into())), ("softness", ParamValue::Number(0.5))]),
    ];
    let at = [52, 60, 67];
    let (sw, sh) = (W * at.len() as u32, H * rows.len() as u32);
    let mut out = vec![0u8; (sw * sh * 4) as usize];
    for (row, (recipe, set)) in rows.iter().enumerate() {
        let (p, frames) = pair(recipe, set, |x, y| [(x * 4) as u8, (y * 7) as u8, 128, 255], |x, y| [(255 - x * 3) as u8, 90, (y * 6) as u8, 255]);
        for (col, n) in at.iter().enumerate() {
            let (buf, _) = render_at(&p, &frames, FrameRate::FPS_30.frame_to_time(*n), SizeU::new(W, H));
            for y in 0..H {
                let o = (((row as u32 * H + y) * sw + col as u32 * W) * 4) as usize;
                out[o..o + (W * 4) as usize].copy_from_slice(&buf[(y * W * 4) as usize..((y + 1) * W * 4) as usize]);
            }
        }
    }
    let path = golden_path("e3_transitions");
    if std::env::var_os("KADR_UPDATE_GOLDEN").is_some_and(|v| v == "1") {
        write_png(&path, sw, sh, &out);
        return;
    }
    let (gw, gh, golden) = read_png(&path).expect("no golden: KADR_UPDATE_GOLDEN=1 cargo test -p kadr-project-scenes --test transitions_pixels, then look at it");
    assert_eq!((gw, gh), (sw, sh));
    let bad = out.iter().zip(&golden).filter(|(a, b)| a.abs_diff(**b) > 1).count();
    assert_eq!(bad, 0, "{bad} channels differ by more than 1 LSB");
}
```

Run: `cargo test -p kadr-project-scenes --test transitions_pixels a_push overlays && KADR_UPDATE_GOLDEN=1 cargo test -p kadr-project-scenes --test transitions_pixels e3_transitions && cargo test -p kadr-project-scenes`

```rust
    /// Two clips meeting on V1 at frame 301 of a 29.97 fps sequence
    /// (10.0434 s — not a whole millisecond).
    fn cut_project() -> (Project, ClipId, ClipId, Time) {
        let mut p = Project::new("cut");
        let fr = kadr_core::FrameRate::FPS_29_97;
        p.sequence_mut().frame_rate = fr;
        let cut = fr.frame_to_time(301);
        let info = MediaInfo { kind: MediaKind::Video, duration: Time::from_secs(60), container: "mp4".into(), size_bytes: 0, video: None, audio: None, timecode: None };
        let m = MediaAsset::new("m.mp4", info);
        let a = Clip::new(m.id, "a", TimeRange::new(Time::ZERO, cut), Time::ZERO);
        let b = Clip::new(m.id, "b", TimeRange::new(Time::from_secs(30), Time::from_secs(40)), cut);
        let (ai, bi) = (a.id, b.id);
        p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips.extend([a, b]);
        p.assets.push(m);
        (p, ai, bi, cut)
    }

        assert_eq!((params["direction"].clone(), params["softness"].clone()), (ParamValue::Choice("up".into()), ParamValue::Number(0.25)), "defaults filled in");
        let by_clips = validate(&[cmd(json!({"type": "add_transition", "from": a.to_string(), "to": b.to_string(), "kind": "dip_to_black", "duration_ms": 1000}))], &p, &perms).unwrap();
        let EditCommand::SetTransition { recipe, params, .. } = &by_clips[0] else { panic!() };

        let perms = Permissions::default();
        let set = cmd(json!({"type": "set_transition", "id": id, "params": {"softness": 0.5}, "duration_ms": 800}));
        assert!(matches!(&validate(&[set], &p, &perms).unwrap()[0], EditCommand::UpdateTransition { duration: Some(d), .. } if *d == Time::from_millis(800)));
        let same = cmd(json!({"type": "set_transition", "id": id, "params": {"direction": "left"}}));
        assert_eq!(validate(std::slice::from_ref(&same), &p, &perms).unwrap(), vec![], "a plan drops a no-op (R10)");
        assert!(validate_strict(&[same], &p, &perms).unwrap_err().to_string().contains("no change"));
        let again = cmd(json!({"type": "add_transition", "from": a.to_string(), "to": b.to_string(), "kind": "wipe", "duration_ms": 1000}));
        assert!(validate_strict(&[again], &p, &perms).unwrap_err().to_string().contains("no change"), "the old wipe again");
        let rm = cmd(json!({"type": "remove_transition", "id": id}));
        assert_eq!(validate(std::slice::from_ref(&rm), &p, &perms).unwrap(), vec![EditCommand::RemoveTransition(kadr_core::TransitionId::parse(&id).unwrap())]);
        assert!(matches!(validate(&[rm], &p, &Permissions { allow_destructive: false, max_commands: 10 }), Err(ValidationError::Permission { .. })));
        assert!(validate(&[cmd(json!({"type": "set_transition", "id": kadr_core::TransitionId::new().to_string(), "duration_ms": 900}))], &p, &perms).unwrap_err().to_string().contains("no transition"));
        p.sequence_mut().transitions[0].recipe = "iris".into();
        assert!(validate(&[cmd(json!({"type": "set_transition", "id": id, "duration_ms": 900}))], &p, &perms).unwrap_err().to_string().contains("newer Kadr"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_prompt_schema_and_context_know_the_transitions() {
        let s = schema_for_prompt();
        assert!(s.contains("\"add_transition\"") && s.contains("\"set_transition\"") && s.contains("\"remove_transition\""));
        for (id, ru) in [("clean_fade", "Растворение"), ("fade_through_color", "Через цвет"), ("smooth_push", "Сдвиг"), ("light_sweep", "Световая волна"), ("wipe", "Шторка"), ("light_leak", "Засветка")] {
            assert!(s.contains(&format!("   {id} «{ru}»")), "{id}: {s}");
        }
        assert!(s.contains("   wipe «Шторка», transition: direction left|right|up|down, softness 0..1 (default 0.25)"), "{s}");
        let (mut p, a, b, _) = cut_project();
        p.sequence_mut().transitions.push(Transition::legacy(TransitionKind::CrossDissolve, a, b, Time::from_secs(1)));
        p.sequence_mut().transitions.push(Transition::new("iris_v2", 4, [("secret".to_string(), ParamValue::Unknown(json!({"blob": [1]})))].into(), a, b, Time::from_secs(1)));
        let ctx = crate::context::project_context(&p, &Default::default());
        let t = &ctx["transitions"];
        assert_eq!((t[0]["recipe"].as_str(), t[0]["params"]["style"].as_str(), t[0]["known"].as_bool()), (Some("clean_fade"), Some("clean"), Some(true)));
        assert_eq!(t[0]["at_ms"], json!(p.sequence().transition_cut(&p.sequence().transitions[0]).unwrap().1.as_millis()));
        assert_eq!((t[1]["known"].as_bool(), t[1].get("params")), (Some(false), None), "a newer Kadr's transition: no raw data to the cloud (R11c)");
        assert!(!ctx.to_string().contains("blob"));
    }
```

```rust
    /// D9: `add_transition` by a millisecond time, `set_transition`,
    /// `remove_transition` through `edit`; `get_state` lists the transition.
    #[test]
    fn transitions_are_added_changed_and_removed_through_edit() {
        let (mut p, v, _) = project_with_pair();
        let b = Clip::new(p.assets[0].id, "b", TimeRange::new(Time::ZERO, Time::from_secs(4)), Time::from_secs(10));
        let bid = b.id;
        p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips.push(b);
        let add = serde_json::json!([{"type": "add_transition", "at_ms": 10_000, "recipe": "light_sweep", "params": {"color": "cold"}, "duration_ms": 600}]);
        let cmds = edit_commands(&p, &[], &add).unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(&cmds[..], [EditCommand::SetTransition { from, to, .. }] if *from == v && *to == bid));
        kadr_timeline::EditEngine::new().execute(&mut p, EditCommand::Batch { label: "mcp".into(), commands: cmds }).unwrap();
        let state = crate::mcp_state::timeline_json(p.sequence(), &[], Time::ZERO);
        let t = &state["transitions"][0];
        assert_eq!((t["recipe"].as_str(), t["params"]["color"].as_str(), t["at_ms"].as_i64(), t["duration_ms"].as_i64(), t["known"].as_bool()), (Some("light_sweep"), Some("cold"), Some(10_000), Some(600), Some(true)));
        assert_eq!((t["from"].as_str(), t["to"].as_str()), (Some(v.to_string().as_str()), Some(bid.to_string().as_str())));
        let id = t["id"].as_str().unwrap().to_string();
        assert!(edit_commands(&p, &[], &add).unwrap_err().message.contains("no change"));
        assert!(matches!(&edit_commands(&p, &[], &serde_json::json!([{"type": "set_transition", "id": id, "params": {"direction": "up"}}])).unwrap()[..], [EditCommand::UpdateTransition { .. }]));
        assert!(matches!(&edit_commands(&p, &[], &serde_json::json!([{"type": "remove_transition", "id": id}])).unwrap()[..], [EditCommand::RemoveTransition(_)]));
        let bad = edit_commands(&p, &[], &serde_json::json!([{"type": "add_transition", "at_ms": 10_000, "recipe": "wipe", "duration_ms": 500, "colour": "x"}])).unwrap_err();
        assert!(bad.message.contains("unknown field"), "{}", bad.message);
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `transition_json`** — in `crates/effects/src/json.rs`: import `use crate::registry::{definition, transition_definition, RecipeDef};` and `use kadr_project::{AppliedEffect, Clip, ParamValue, Sequence, SourceTime, Transition};`; after `effect_json` add:

```rust
/// A transition as `get_state` and the AI context show it (D9): recipe,
/// version, parameters, its clips, its track and cut (`null` where the
/// clips do not meet), its length; `known: false` for one this build
/// cannot run.
pub fn transition_json(seq: &Sequence, tr: &Transition) -> Value {
    let cut = seq.transition_cut(tr);
    json!({
        "id": tr.id.to_string(), "recipe": tr.recipe, "version": tr.version, "known": transition_definition(tr).is_some(),
        "params": tr.params.iter().map(|(k, v)| (k.clone(), plain_value_json(v))).collect::<Map<_, _>>(),
        "from": tr.from.to_string(), "to": tr.to.to_string(),
        "track_id": cut.map(|(ti, _)| seq.tracks[ti].id.to_string()),
        "at_ms": cut.map(|(_, at)| at.as_millis()),
        "duration_ms": tr.duration.as_millis(),
    })
}

/// A parameter value with no clip to place a moment on: as [`value_json`],
/// a moment by its source time only.
fn plain_value_json(v: &ParamValue) -> Value {
    match v {
        ParamValue::Number(n) => json!(n),
        ParamValue::Choice(c) => json!(c),
        ParamValue::Point { x, y } => json!({"x": x, "y": y}),
        ParamValue::Color(c) => json!(c),
        ParamValue::Moment(m) => json!({"source_ms": m.0.as_millis()}),
        ParamValue::Unknown(v) => v.clone(),
    }
}
```

```rust
            AiCommand::AddTransition { at_ms, from, to, recipe, kind, params, duration_ms, track_id, .. } => {
                let range = |msg: String| ValidationError::Range { index: i, msg };
                let duration = transition_length(i, *duration_ms, seq.frame_rate)?;
                let cuts = || kadr_effects::registry().iter().filter(|d| d.target == kadr_effects::Target::Cut).map(|d| d.id).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
                // The recipe: given, or the old kind's (D1).
                let (def, mut values) = match (recipe.as_deref(), kind) {
                    (Some(_), Some(_)) => return Err(range("give recipe or kind, not both".into())),
                    (None, None) => return Err(range(format!("give recipe (one of {})", cuts()))),
                    (Some(r), None) => {
                        let def = kadr_effects::recipe(r).ok_or_else(|| range(format!("unknown transition {r:?} (one of {})", cuts())))?;
                        if def.target != kadr_effects::Target::Cut {
                            return Err(range(format!("{r} is an effect for clips: use add_effect (transitions: {})", cuts())));
                        }
                        (def, BTreeMap::new())
                    }
                    (None, Some(k)) => {
                        let (r, p) = k.recipe();
                        (kadr_effects::recipe(r).ok_or_else(|| range(format!("{r} is not in this build")))?, p)
                    }
                };
                values.extend(kadr_effects::json::cut_params_from_json(def, params).map_err(range)?);
                // The cut: its two clips, or a time on a track.
                let (f, t) = match (from.as_deref(), to.as_deref(), at_ms) {
                    (Some(a), Some(b), None) => {
                        let (f, t) = (clip(i, a)?, clip(i, b)?);
                        let (ti, ci) = seq.locate_clip(f).unwrap();
                        let track = &seq.tracks[ti];
                        let meets = track.kind == TrackKind::Video && track.clips.get(ci + 1).is_some_and(|n| n.id == t && n.timeline_in == track.clips[ci].timeline_out);
                        if !meets {
                            return Err(range(format!("clips {a} and {b} do not meet at a cut on a video track")));
                        }
                        (f, t)
                    }
                    (None, None, Some(at)) => {
                        let time = in_seq(i, *at)?;
                        let track = match track_id.as_deref() {
                            Some(id) => seq.tracks.iter().find(|t| t.id.to_string() == id).ok_or_else(|| range(format!("unknown track {id}")))?,
                            None => seq.tracks.iter().find(|t| t.kind == TrackKind::Video).ok_or_else(|| range("no video track".into()))?,
                        };
                        if track.kind != TrackKind::Video {
                            return Err(range(format!("track {} is not a video track: transitions go on video", track.name)));
                        }
                        if track.locked {
                            return Err(range(format!("track {} is locked", track.name)));
                        }
                        let (f, t, _) = seq.cut_near(track.id, time, half_frame).ok_or_else(|| range(format!("no cut within half a frame of {at} ms on track {} (or more than one)", track.name)))?;
                        (f, t)
                    }
                    _ => return Err(range("name the cut by at_ms, or by from and to (clip ids)".into())),
                };
                let mut full = kadr_effects::transition_params(def);
                full.extend(values);
                // The cut has this transition already: no change (R10).
                if seq.transitions.iter().any(|x| x.to == t && x.from == f && x.recipe == def.id && x.version == def.version && x.params == full && x.duration == duration && x.extra.is_empty()) {
                    if strict {
                        return Err(range("no change: the cut already has this transition".into()));
                    }
                    out.push(None);
                    continue;
                }
                EditCommand::SetTransition { from: f, to: t, recipe: def.id.into(), version: def.version, params: full, duration }
            }
            AiCommand::SetTransition { id, params, duration_ms, .. } => {
                let range = |msg: String| ValidationError::Range { index: i, msg };
                let tr = TransitionId::parse(id).and_then(|x| seq.transition(x)).ok_or_else(|| range(format!("no transition {id}")))?;
                let def = kadr_effects::transition_definition(tr).ok_or_else(|| range(format!("transition {} ({}) was made by a newer Kadr and cannot be changed", tr.id, tr.recipe)))?;
                if params.is_empty() && duration_ms.is_none() {
                    return Err(range("give params and/or duration_ms".into()));
                }
                locked_transition(i, seq, tr)?;
                let values = kadr_effects::json::cut_params_from_json(def, params).map_err(range)?;
                let duration = duration_ms.map(|d| transition_length(i, d, seq.frame_rate)).transpose()?;
                let mut probe = tr.clone();
                probe.params.extend(values.clone());
                if let Some(d) = duration {
                    probe.duration = d;
                }
                if probe == *tr {
                    if strict {
                        return Err(range("no change: the transition already has these settings".into()));
                    }
                    out.push(None);
                    continue;
                }
                EditCommand::UpdateTransition { id: tr.id, params: values, duration }
            }
            AiCommand::RemoveTransition { id, .. } => {
                destructive(i)?;
                let tr = TransitionId::parse(id).and_then(|x| seq.transition(x)).ok_or_else(|| ValidationError::Range { index: i, msg: format!("no transition {id}") })?;
                locked_transition(i, seq, tr)?;
                EditCommand::RemoveTransition(tr.id)
            }
```

and after `fn check`:

```rust
/// A transition's length from the client: 40..10000 ms and at least two
/// frames at the sequence's rate (the engine's floor).
fn transition_length(index: usize, ms: i64, rate: kadr_core::FrameRate) -> Result<Time, ValidationError> {
    let t = Time::from_millis(ms);
    let two = rate.frame_to_time(2);
    if !(40..=10_000).contains(&ms) || t < two {
        let per_ms = kadr_core::FLICKS_PER_SECOND / 1000;
        let floor = (two.flicks() + per_ms - 1) / per_ms;
        return Err(ValidationError::Range { index, msg: format!("transition duration 40..10000 ms and at least two frames ({floor} ms), got {ms}") });
    }
    Ok(t)
}

/// A transition on a locked track is not changed.
fn locked_transition(index: usize, seq: &kadr_project::Sequence, tr: &kadr_project::Transition) -> Result<(), ValidationError> {
    match seq.locate_clip(tr.to).map(|(ti, _)| &seq.tracks[ti]) {
        Some(t) if t.locked => Err(ValidationError::Range { index, msg: format!("transition {} is on the locked track {}", tr.id, t.name) }),
        _ => Ok(()),
    }
}
```

(numbers and choice strings; omitted ones get defaults), duration_ms (at least two frames; drawn no longer than the shorter of the two clips), and the cut: at_ms (the cut within half a frame of it on track_id, default the first video track; exactly one, else rejected) or from + to (the clip ids meeting there). Transitions follow their clips: an edit after which the two no longer meet removes it, a split of the outgoing clip keeps it on the piece at the cut
```text
- \"set_transition\": id (from `get_state`'s `transitions`), params?, duration_ms? (merged; no change is rejected; a transition from a newer Kadr, known: false, cannot be changed, only removed)
```

```markdown
Переходы (`add_transition`, `set_transition`, `remove_transition`; команды
ассистента в `crates/ai/src/command.rs`): `add_transition` ставит рецепт с
целью `cut` из `list_effects` (`recipe`; старое `kind` —
`cross_dissolve`/`dip_to_black`/`wipe` — понимается как синоним:
`clean_fade` / `fade_through_color` чёрный / `wipe` влево) на склейку, которую
называет `at_ms` — стык (`timeline_out` одного клипа == `timeline_in`
следующего) не дальше полукадра от `at_ms` на `track_id` или первой
видеодорожке, ровно один, иначе отказ — или пара клипов `from` + `to`.
`duration_ms` — не меньше двух кадров; рисуется не длиннее короткого из двух
клипов. Один переход на склейку: повтор заменяет его (тот же id), повтор без
изменений — отказ «no change». `set_transition {id, params?, duration_ms?}`
сливает параметры; переход из более новой версии (`known: false`) только
удаляется. Переходы следуют своим клипам: правка, после которой клипы больше не
встречаются, удаляет переход в той же записи undo; split уходящего клипа
переносит переход на его часть у склейки. `get_state` перечисляет
`transitions` последовательности (`id`, `recipe`, `version`, `params`,
`from`, `to`, `track_id`, `at_ms`, `duration_ms`, `known`).
```

```rust
    pub(crate) fn seq() -> (Sequence, [ClipId; 2], TransitionId) {

        let v1 = *rs.iter().find(|r| r.track == 0).unwrap();

        assert_eq!(pill_at(&p, 160.0, v1.y + 5.0), None, "the fade-handle strip");
        assert_eq!(pill_at(&p, 160.0, v1.y + VIDEO_H - 4.0), None, "the keyframe strip");
        assert_eq!(pill_at(&p, 185.0, v1.y + TR_TOP + 5.0), None);
        let narrow = pills(&s, &rs, 2.0, 2.0);

        let long = pills(&s, &rs, 0.0, 40.0);

        s.tracks[0].clips[1].timeline_in = Time::from_secs(5);

        assert_eq!((wide[0].height, wide[0].width), (TR_H as f32, 80.0));

        assert_eq!((narrow[0].name.as_str(), narrow[0].selected), ("", false), "40 px: the cross");
        s.transitions[0].recipe = "iris".into();
        let unknown = views(&s, &pills(&s, &rs, 0.0, 80.0), None);
        assert_eq!((unknown[0].name.as_str(), unknown[0].known), (t("tr.unavailable").as_str(), false));

        assert!(tip(&s, id).unwrap().contains("iris"), "a newer Kadr's: named by its recipe id");
        assert_eq!(tip(&s, TransitionId::new()), None);
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The pills** — above the test module of `apps/editor/src/transitions_ui.rs`:

```rust
//! Transitions in the editor (effects spec §6.1–6.3, D8): their pills on
//! the timeline — drawn over the cut, hit, selected, named — and (Tasks 8
//! and 9) the cut a card lands on and the inspector's block. Pure parts are
//! free functions, tested without a window.

use crate::anim_ui;
use crate::app::App;
use crate::timeline_ui::{Row, VIDEO_H};
use crate::util::fmt_secs;
use crate::TransitionView;
use kadr_core::{Time, TransitionId};
use kadr_i18n::{t, tf};
use kadr_project::{Sequence, Transition};

/// A pill's top in its row and its height: the row's middle band, below
/// the fade handles' strip and above the keyframe strip (refinement 12).
pub const TR_TOP: f64 = 21.0;
pub const TR_H: f64 = 22.0;
/// The narrowest pill.
pub const TR_MIN_W: f64 = 16.0;
/// Narrower than this a pill shows a cross instead of its name.
pub const TR_NAME_W: f64 = 56.0;
const _: () = assert!(TR_TOP >= anim_ui::HEADER_H && TR_TOP + TR_H <= VIDEO_H - 2.0 - anim_ui::KEY_STRIP_H);

/// A transition's name in the UI: its recipe's, or «Недоступен» for one
/// this build cannot run.
pub fn transition_name(tr: &Transition) -> String {
    match kadr_effects::transition_definition(tr) {
        Some(d) => t(&d.name_key()),
        None => t("tr.unavailable"),
    }
}

/// A transition as the timeline shows it, in lane pixels.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Pill {
    pub id: TransitionId,
    pub x0: f64,
    pub x1: f64,
    pub y: f64,
    pub known: bool,
}

/// Every transition whose clips meet on a shown row (§6.3, D8): over its
/// window as drawn — `min(duration, its two clips)` centred on the cut — at
/// least [`TR_MIN_W`] wide, in the row's middle band.
pub fn pills(seq: &Sequence, rows: &[Row], scroll: f64, pps: f64) -> Vec<Pill> {
    seq.transitions
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|tr| {
            let (ti, cut) = seq.transition_cut(tr)?;
            let row = rows.iter().find(|r| r.track == ti)?;
            let d = tr.duration.min(seq.transition_limit(tr)?);
            let start = cut - Time(d.flicks() / 2);
            let x = |t: Time| (t.as_secs_f64() - scroll) * pps;
            let (mut x0, mut x1) = (x(start), x(start + d));
            if x1 - x0 < TR_MIN_W {
                let c = x(cut);
                (x0, x1) = (c - TR_MIN_W / 2.0, c + TR_MIN_W / 2.0);
            }
            Some(Pill { id: tr.id, x0, x1, y: row.y + TR_TOP, known: kadr_effects::transition_definition(tr).is_some() })
        })
        .collect()
}

/// The pill under lane point `(x, y)` — the last drawn, on top.
pub fn pill_at(pills: &[Pill], x: f64, y: f64) -> Option<TransitionId> {
    pills.iter().rev().find(|p| x >= p.x0 && x < p.x1 && y >= p.y && y < p.y + TR_H).map(|p| p.id)
}

/// The rows the timeline draws: named where the pill is wide enough, the selected one marked.
pub fn views(seq: &Sequence, pills: &[Pill], selected: Option<TransitionId>) -> Vec<TransitionView> {
    pills
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|p| {
            let tr = seq.transition(p.id)?;
            let w = p.x1 - p.x0;
            Some(TransitionView {
                id: p.id.to_string().into(),
                x: p.x0 as f32,
                y: p.y as f32,
                width: w as f32,
                height: TR_H as f32,
                name: if w >= TR_NAME_W { transition_name(tr) } else { String::new() }.into(),
                known: p.known,
                selected: selected == Some(p.id),
            })
        })
        .collect()
}

/// A pill's tooltip: «Шторка · 0,5 с» (the length drawn), or for a
/// transition from a newer Kadr why it can only be removed.
pub fn tip(seq: &Sequence, id: TransitionId) -> Option<String> {
    let tr = seq.transition(id)?;
    if kadr_effects::transition_definition(tr).is_none() {
        return Some(tf("tr.tip.unavailable", &[("name", &tr.recipe)]));
    }
    let d = tr.duration.min(seq.transition_limit(tr)?);
    Some(tf("tr.tip", &[("name", &transition_name(tr)), ("t", &fmt_secs(d))]))
}

impl App {
    /// The transition under lane point `(x, y)`, as `hit` sees it (none in blade mode).
    pub(crate) fn transition_at(&self, x: f64, y: f64) -> Option<TransitionId> {
        if self.tl.blade {
            return None;
        }
        let seq = self.project.sequence();
        pill_at(&pills(seq, &crate::timeline_ui::rows(seq, self.tl.scroll_y), self.tl.scroll, self.tl.pps), x, y)
    }

    /// Selects transition `id` (D8): the clip selection goes and the
    /// inspector shows the transition.
    pub fn select_transition(&mut self, id: TransitionId) {
        self.tl.selection.clear();
        self.tl.transition = Some(id);
        self.refresh_timeline();
        self.refresh_inspector();
    }

    /// The pill tooltip while the pointer rests on a transition (`None`:
    /// the pointer left the lanes); a drag's own tips are untouched.
    pub fn tr_hover(&mut self, at: Option<(f64, f64)>) {
        let hit = at.and_then(|(x, y)| Some((self.transition_at(x, y)?, x, y)));
        match hit.and_then(|(id, x, y)| Some((id, tip(self.project.sequence(), id)?, x, y))) {
            Some((id, text, x, y)) => {
                self.tl.tip_transition = Some(id);
                self.show_tip(text, x, y);
            }
            None => {
                if self.tl.tip_transition.take().is_some() {
                    self.ui().set_tl_tip_text("".into());
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```rust
    /// Review focus 3: a card lands on the cut within 8 px of the pointer on
    /// a video row; on a clip body, a gap, the empty V2, an audio row or
    /// below the tracks it does not.
    #[test]
    fn a_card_lands_on_a_cut_within_reach_and_nowhere_else() {
        let (mut s, [a, b], _) = seq();
        let rs = rows(&s, 0.0);
        let v1 = rs.iter().position(|r| r.track == 0).unwrap();
        let y = rs[v1].y + 30.0;
        let on = CutHit::Cut { from: a, to: b, cut: Time::from_secs(4), row: v1 };
        // At 40 px/s the cut (4 s) is x 160.
        assert_eq!(cut_hit(&s, &rs, 166.0, y, 0.0, 40.0), on);
        assert_eq!(cut_hit(&s, &rs, 152.0, y, 0.0, 40.0), on, "8 px is within reach");
        assert_eq!(cut_hit(&s, &rs, 151.0, y, 0.0, 40.0), CutHit::Off, "9 px: the clip body");
        assert_eq!(cut_hit(&s, &rs, 400.0, y, 0.0, 40.0), CutHit::Off, "past the clips");
        let v2 = rs.iter().find(|r| r.track == 1).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(cut_hit(&s, &rs, 160.0, v2.y + 30.0, 0.0, 40.0), CutHit::Off, "the empty V2");
        let a1 = rs.iter().find(|r| r.kind == TrackKind::Audio).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(cut_hit(&s, &rs, 160.0, a1.y + 20.0, 0.0, 40.0), CutHit::Off, "no transitions on sound");
        assert_eq!(cut_hit(&s, &rs, 160.0, 10_000.0, 0.0, 40.0), CutHit::Nothing, "below the tracks");
        assert_eq!(cut_ghost(&s, &rs, on, 0.0, 40.0), Some((140.0, rs[v1].y, 40.0, rs[v1].h)), "the would-be pill: the cut's 1 s kept");
        s.transitions.clear();
        let w = kadr_effects::default_duration(s.frame_rate).as_secs_f64() * 40.0;
        assert_eq!(cut_ghost(&s, &rs, on, 0.0, 40.0), Some((160.0 - w / 2.0, rs[v1].y, w, rs[v1].h)), "a new one: 0.5 s on whole frames");
        s.tracks[0].locked = true;
        assert_eq!(cut_hit(&s, &rs, 160.0, y, 0.0, 40.0), CutHit::Locked);
    }

    /// D8, refinement 11: a click puts the card on the selected clip's edge
    /// nearest the playhead where a neighbour meets it.
    #[test]
    fn a_card_click_uses_the_selected_clips_nearest_cut_edge() {
        let (mut s, [a, b], _) = seq();
        let c = Clip::new(AssetId::new(), "c", TimeRange::new(Time::ZERO, Time::from_secs(4)), Time::from_secs(8));
        let cid = c.id;
        s.tracks[0].clips.push(c);
        assert_eq!(click_cut(&s, Some(b), Time::from_secs(5)), Ok((a, b)), "b's start is nearer");
        assert_eq!(click_cut(&s, Some(b), Time::from_secs(7)), Ok((b, cid)), "b's end is nearer");
        assert_eq!(click_cut(&s, Some(a), Time::from_secs(30)), Ok((a, b)), "a has one cut edge");
        assert_eq!(click_cut(&s, None, Time::ZERO), Err("fx.hint.select_clip_cut"));
        s.tracks[0].clips[2].move_to(Time::from_secs(9));
        assert_eq!(click_cut(&s, Some(cid), Time::from_secs(9)), Err("fx.hint.no_cut"), "c meets nothing");
        s.tracks[0].locked = true;
        assert_eq!(click_cut(&s, Some(b), Time::ZERO), Err("fx.drop.locked"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_card_sets_its_recipe_keeping_the_cuts_length_and_sends_nothing_when_it_is_there() {
        let (mut s, [a, b], _) = seq();
        let push = kadr_effects::recipe("smooth_push").unwrap();
        let Some(EditCommand::Batch { label, commands }) = card_command(&s, push, a, b) else { panic!() };
        assert_eq!(label, tf("cmd.transition_named", &[("name", &t("fx.smooth_push"))]));
        assert!(matches!(&commands[..], [EditCommand::SetTransition { recipe, duration, .. }] if recipe == "smooth_push" && *duration == Time::from_secs(1)), "the cut's 1 s kept");
        let wipe = kadr_effects::recipe("wipe").unwrap();
        s.transitions[0].params = kadr_effects::transition_params(wipe);
        assert!(card_command(&s, wipe, a, b).is_none(), "already that transition");
        s.transitions.clear();
        let Some(EditCommand::Batch { commands, .. }) = card_command(&s, wipe, a, b) else { panic!() };
        assert!(matches!(&commands[..], [EditCommand::SetTransition { duration, .. }] if *duration == kadr_effects::default_duration(s.frame_rate)), "a new one: 0.5 s");
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The cut a card lands on** — in `apps/editor/src/transitions_ui.rs`: imports `use crate::effects_ui::Card;`, `use kadr_core::ClipId;`, `use kadr_effects::RecipeDef;`, `use kadr_project::TrackKind;`, `use kadr_timeline::EditCommand;`; add:

```rust
/// How near a cut (lane pixels) a dropped transition card lands on it (D8).
pub const CUT_ZONE: f64 = 8.0;

/// What a transition card at a lane point hits (D8).
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum CutHit {
    /// The cut nearest the pointer on a video row, within [`CUT_ZONE`]; `row` indexes the rows.
    Cut { from: ClipId, to: ClipId, cut: Time, row: usize },
    /// Such a cut on a locked track.
    Locked,
    /// A row, but no cut in reach (a clip body, a gap, an audio row).
    Off,
    /// No row.
    Nothing,
}

pub fn cut_hit(seq: &Sequence, rows: &[Row], x: f64, y: f64, scroll: f64, pps: f64) -> CutHit {
    let Some((ri, r)) = rows.iter().enumerate().find(|(_, r)| y >= r.y && y < r.y + r.h) else { return CutHit::Nothing };
    let Some(track) = seq.tracks.get(r.track).filter(|t| t.kind == TrackKind::Video) else { return CutHit::Off };
    let to_x = |t: Time| (t.as_secs_f64() - scroll) * pps;
    let near = track
        .clips
        .windows(2)
        .filter(|w| w[0].timeline_out == w[1].timeline_in)
        .map(|w| ((w[0].id, w[1].id, w[0].timeline_out), (to_x(w[0].timeline_out) - x).abs()))
        .filter(|(_, d)| *d <= CUT_ZONE)
        .min_by(|a, b| a.1.total_cmp(&b.1));
    match near {
        None => CutHit::Off,
        Some(_) if track.locked => CutHit::Locked,
        Some(((from, to, cut), _)) => CutHit::Cut { from, to, cut, row: ri },
    }
}

/// The length a card gives the cut `from` | `to`: the transition's there
/// (refinement 11), else a new one's (0.5 s), at least two frames.
fn card_duration(seq: &Sequence, to: ClipId) -> Time {
    let d = seq.transitions.iter().find(|t| t.to == to).map_or_else(|| kadr_effects::default_duration(seq.frame_rate), |t| t.duration);
    d.max(seq.frame_rate.frame_to_time(2))
}

/// The dragged card's outline (D8, the cut highlighted): the would-be pill
/// on the cut — its window as it would be drawn, at least [`TR_MIN_W`] —
/// over the row's full height.
pub fn cut_ghost(seq: &Sequence, rows: &[Row], hit: CutHit, scroll: f64, pps: f64) -> Option<(f64, f64, f64, f64)> {
    let CutHit::Cut { from, to, cut, row } = hit else { return None };
    let r = rows.get(row)?;
    let limit = seq.clip(from)?.duration().min(seq.clip(to)?.duration());
    let w = (card_duration(seq, to).min(limit).as_secs_f64() * pps).max(TR_MIN_W);
    let cx = (cut.as_secs_f64() - scroll) * pps;
    Some((cx - w / 2.0, r.y, w, r.h))
}

/// The cut a click on a transition card puts it on (D8, refinement 11): of
/// the selected video clip's two edges, those where a neighbour meets it,
/// the one nearest the playhead; `Err` the key of the hint why there is none.
pub fn click_cut(seq: &Sequence, clip: Option<ClipId>, playhead: Time) -> Result<(ClipId, ClipId), &'static str> {
    let Some((ti, ci)) = clip.and_then(|c| seq.locate_clip(c)).filter(|(ti, _)| seq.tracks[*ti].kind == TrackKind::Video) else { return Err("fx.hint.select_clip_cut") };
    let track = &seq.tracks[ti];
    if track.locked {
        return Err("fx.drop.locked");
    }
    let c = &track.clips[ci];
    let before = ci.checked_sub(1).map(|p| &track.clips[p]).filter(|p| p.timeline_out == c.timeline_in).map(|p| (p.id, c.id, c.timeline_in));
    let after = track.clips.get(ci + 1).filter(|n| n.timeline_in == c.timeline_out).map(|n| (c.id, n.id, c.timeline_out));
    [before, after].into_iter().flatten().min_by_key(|(_, _, cut)| (*cut - playhead).abs()).map(|(f, t, _)| (f, t)).ok_or("fx.hint.no_cut")
}

/// The edit a transition card makes on the cut `from` | `to` (D8): its
/// recipe at its defaults ([`card_duration`] long), one undo entry
/// «Переход: Сдвиг»; `None` when the cut has exactly this transition already.
pub fn card_command(seq: &Sequence, def: &'static RecipeDef, from: ClipId, to: ClipId) -> Option<EditCommand> {
    let params = kadr_effects::transition_params(def);
    let same = seq.transitions.iter().any(|t| t.to == to && t.from == from && t.recipe == def.id && t.version == def.version && t.params == params && t.extra.is_empty());
    if same {
        return None;
    }
    let set = EditCommand::SetTransition { from, to, recipe: def.id.into(), version: def.version, params, duration: card_duration(seq, to) };
    Some(EditCommand::Batch { label: tf("cmd.transition_named", &[("name", &t(&def.name_key()))]), commands: vec![set] })
}
```

and in `impl App`:

```rust
    /// What a transition card at window point `(wx, wy)` hits.
    fn cut_drop_at(&self, wx: f64, wy: f64) -> CutHit {
        let ui = self.ui();
        let (x, y) = (wx - ui.get_lanes_abs_x() as f64, wy - ui.get_lanes_abs_y() as f64);
        if x < 0.0 || y < 0.0 || x > ui.get_lanes_width() as f64 || y > ui.get_lanes_height() as f64 {
            return CutHit::Nothing;
        }
        let seq = self.project.sequence();
        cut_hit(seq, &crate::timeline_ui::rows(seq, self.tl.scroll_y), x, y, self.tl.scroll, self.tl.pps)
    }

    /// A transition card dragged (D8): the cut it would land on outlined,
    /// a refusal over any other row, the label saying which.
    pub fn fx_cut_moved(&mut self, card: Card, wx: f64, wy: f64) {
        let name = t(&card.name_key());
        let hit = self.cut_drop_at(wx, wy);
        let label = match hit {
            CutHit::Cut { .. } => tf("fx.drop.on_cut", &[("name", &name)]),
            CutHit::Locked => t("fx.drop.locked"),
            CutHit::Off | CutHit::Nothing => tf("fx.drag_hint_cut", &[("name", &name)]),
        };
        let seq = self.project.sequence();
        self.tl.ghost = cut_ghost(seq, &crate::timeline_ui::rows(seq, self.tl.scroll_y), hit, self.tl.scroll, self.tl.pps);
        let ui = self.ui();
        ui.set_drag_active(true);
        ui.set_drag_refused(matches!(hit, CutHit::Off | CutHit::Locked));
        ui.set_drag_x(wx as f32);
        ui.set_drag_y(wy as f32);
        ui.set_drag_label(label.into());
        self.refresh_timeline();
    }

    /// The end of a transition card's press (D8): a drag puts it on the
    /// cut under the pointer; a click on the selected transition's cut, or
    /// the selected clip's nearest cut edge. The transition ends selected.
    pub fn fx_cut_released(&mut self, card: Card, wx: f64, wy: f64, dragged: bool) {
        let seq = self.project.sequence();
        let target = if dragged {
            match self.cut_drop_at(wx, wy) {
                CutHit::Cut { from, to, .. } => Ok((from, to)),
                CutHit::Locked => Err("fx.drop.locked"),
                CutHit::Off => Err("fx.drop.cut_hint"),
                CutHit::Nothing => return,
            }
        } else {
            match self.tl.transition.and_then(|id| seq.transition(id)) {
                Some(tr) => Ok((tr.from, tr.to)),
                None => click_cut(seq, self.inspected(), self.playhead),
            }
        };
        match target {
            Ok((from, to)) => {
                self.card_transition(card, from, to);
            }
            Err(key) => self.toast_warn(t(key)),
        }
        self.refresh_timeline();
    }

    /// `card` on the cut `from` | `to`, then that transition selected (the
    /// inspector shows it); `true` when something was applied.
    pub fn card_transition(&mut self, card: Card, from: ClipId, to: ClipId) -> bool {
        let applied = match card_command(self.project.sequence(), card.def, from, to) {
            Some(cmd) => self.execute(cmd),
            None => {
                self.toast(tf("fx.already_cut", &[("name", &t(&card.name_key()))]));
                false
            }
        };
        if let Some(id) = self.project.sequence().transitions.iter().find(|t| t.to == to).map(|t| t.id) {
            self.select_transition(id);
        }
        applied
    }
```

Replace the `None => ThumbBase { … }` arm of `thumb_base` with `None => sample_base(),` and add:

```rust
/// What a card shows without a clip: the sample.
pub fn sample_base() -> ThumbBase {
    ThumbBase { clip: None, at: SAMPLE_AT, frame: SAMPLE_AT, state: 0 }
}

/// What `card` shows: a transition card always the sample pair (D8: a
/// schematic picture of the transition), any other card `base`.
pub fn base_for(card: Card, base: &ThumbBase) -> ThumbBase {
    if card.def.target == Target::Cut { sample_base() } else { *base }
}

/// The second sample, the day a transition card mixes the dusk into:
/// a bright sky over the sea and a white sail.
pub fn sample_frame_b() -> Arc<CpuFrame> {
    let mut px = vec![0u8; (SAMPLE_W * SAMPLE_H * 4) as usize];
    for y in 0..SAMPLE_H {
        for x in 0..SAMPLE_W {
            let (u, v) = (x as f32 / SAMPLE_W as f32, y as f32 / SAMPLE_H as f32);
            let mut c = if v < 0.55 { [0.45 + 0.3 * v, 0.68 + 0.2 * v, 0.95] } else { [0.05, 0.32 + 0.2 * (v - 0.55), 0.55 - 0.2 * (v - 0.55)] };
            if (0.3..0.36).contains(&u) && v > 0.3 + (0.36 - u) * 2.5 && v < 0.6 {
                c = [0.97, 0.97, 0.95];
            }
            let i = ((y * SAMPLE_W + x) * 4) as usize;
            for k in 0..3 {
                px[i + k] = (c[k].clamp(0.0, 1.0) * 255.0) as u8;
            }
            px[i + 3] = 255;
        }
    }
    Arc::new(CpuFrame::from_rgba8(SAMPLE_W, SAMPLE_H, ColorInfo { alpha: AlphaMode::Opaque, ..ColorInfo::WORKING_SDR }, px))
}

/// The path of the second sample's asset.
const SAMPLE_B: &str = "kadr-sample-b";
static SAMPLE_DAY: LazyLock<Arc<CpuFrame>> = LazyLock::new(sample_frame_b);

/// The two samples meeting at 2 s on a 16:9 sequence — the dusk, then the
/// day — with `card`'s transition at its defaults on the cut, 1 s: the
/// window is [1.5 s, 2.5 s).
pub fn transition_sample_project(card: Card) -> Project {
    let video = VideoInfo { width: SAMPLE_W, height: SAMPLE_H, frame_rate: Some(FrameRate::FPS_30), variable_frame_rate: false, codec: "sample".into(), pixel_format: "rgba".into(), rotation: 0, sar: (1, 1), color: None };
    let info = MediaInfo { kind: MediaKind::Video, duration: Time::from_secs(10), container: "sample".into(), size_bytes: 0, video: Some(video), audio: None, timecode: None };
    let (a, b) = (MediaAsset::new("kadr-sample", info.clone()), MediaAsset::new(SAMPLE_B, info));
    let ca = Clip::new(a.id, "dusk", TimeRange::new(Time::ZERO, Time::from_secs(2)), Time::ZERO);
    let cb = Clip::new(b.id, "day", TimeRange::new(Time::ZERO, Time::from_secs(2)), Time::from_secs(2));
    let mut p = Project::new("sample");
    p.sequence_mut().frame_rate = FrameRate::FPS_30;
    p.sequence_mut().transitions.push(Transition::new(card.def.id, card.def.version, kadr_effects::transition_params(card.def), ca.id, cb.id, Time::from_secs(1)));
    p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips.extend([ca, cb]);
    p.assets.extend([a, b]);
    p
}

/// The frame of the sample transition's window at `phase` (0..1): its first frame to its last.
pub fn transition_sample_at(phase: f64) -> Time {
    let fr = FrameRate::FPS_30;
    let span = 1.0 - fr.frame_duration().as_secs_f64();
    fr.snap(Time::from_millis(1500) + Time::from_secs_f64(phase.clamp(0.0, 1.0) * span))
}

/// The samples as inputs: every media layer shows the dusk — the day where
/// it is the second sample's asset `day` — also inside a transition.
fn sample_inputs(layers: &[Layer], dusk: &Arc<CpuFrame>, day: Option<AssetId>) -> Vec<LayerInput> {
    layers
        .iter()
        .map(|l| match &l.content {
            LayerContent::Media { media, .. } if Some(media.media) == day => LayerInput::Cpu(SAMPLE_DAY.clone()),
            LayerContent::Media { .. } => LayerInput::Cpu(dusk.clone()),
            LayerContent::Transition(t) => LayerInput::Transition { from: sample_inputs(&t.from, dusk, day), to: sample_inputs(&t.to, dusk, day) },
            _ => LayerInput::None,
        })
        .collect()
}
```

```rust
    }
```

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod live_edit_tests {
    use super::LiveEdit;
    use kadr_core::{AssetId, FrameRate, Time, TimeRange};
    use kadr_project::{Clip, Sequence, Transition, TransitionKind};

    /// A transition slider's live value is rolled back like a clip's, and a
    /// clip drag after it rolls it back first.
    #[test]
    fn a_live_transition_is_rolled_back() {
        let mut seq = Sequence::new("s", FrameRate::FPS_25, 1920, 1080);
        let a = Clip::new(AssetId::new(), "a", TimeRange::new(Time::ZERO, Time::from_secs(4)), Time::ZERO);
        let b = Clip::new(AssetId::new(), "b", TimeRange::new(Time::ZERO, Time::from_secs(4)), Time::from_secs(4));
        let tr = Transition::legacy(TransitionKind::Wipe, a.id, b.id, Time::from_secs(1));
        let (aid, id) = (a.id, tr.id);
        seq.tracks[0].clips.extend([a, b]);
        seq.transitions.push(tr);
        let mut live = LiveEdit::default();
        live.arm_transition(&mut seq, id);
        seq.transitions[0].duration = Time::from_secs(3);
        live.arm_transition(&mut seq, id);
        seq.transitions[0].duration = Time::from_secs(2);
        assert!(live.roll_back(&mut seq));
        assert_eq!(seq.transitions[0].duration, Time::from_secs(1), "the state before the drag");
        assert!(!live.roll_back(&mut seq), "disarmed");
        live.arm_transition(&mut seq, id);
        seq.transitions[0].duration = Time::from_secs(2);
        live.arm(&mut seq, aid);
        assert_eq!(seq.transitions[0].duration, Time::from_secs(1), "another drag rolls it back first");
    }
}
```

```rust
/// How a choice shows (D7, refinement 13): a recipe's variants as a list
/// (4); options that all have an icon — directions, zoom in / out — as icon
/// segments (1); up to three named ones as text segments (5); more as a list.
pub fn choice_kind(def: Option<&RecipeDef>, key: &str, options: &[&str]) -> i32 {
    const ICONS: [&str; 6] = ["in", "out", "left", "right", "up", "down"];
    if def.is_some_and(|d| d.variants == Some(key)) {
        4
    } else if options.iter().all(|o| ICONS.contains(o)) {
        1
    } else if options.len() <= 3 {
        5
    } else {
        4
    }
}
```

`apps/editor/src/transitions_ui.rs`: imports `use crate::{FxParamRow, TransitionRow};`, `use kadr_core::FrameRate;`, `use kadr_effects::{ParamDef, ParamKind, Params};`, `use kadr_project::ParamValue;`, `use slint::{ModelRc, SharedString, VecModel};`, `use std::collections::BTreeMap;`; add:

```rust
/// The inspector's block of transition `tr` (spec §6.2, D8): its name,
/// «Длительность» in seconds — two frames up to the shorter of its clips,
/// the length shown as drawn — and its recipe's parameters; nothing to set
/// on one this build cannot run. `old`: the parameter model on screen, kept.
pub fn transition_row(seq: &Sequence, tr: &Transition, old: Option<ModelRc<FxParamRow>>) -> TransitionRow {
    let def = kadr_effects::transition_definition(tr);
    let min = seq.frame_rate.frame_to_time(2);
    let limit = seq.transition_limit(tr).unwrap_or(tr.duration).max(min);
    let params: Vec<FxParamRow> = def.map_or_else(Vec::new, |d| d.params.iter().map(|p| cut_param_row(tr, d, p)).collect());
    TransitionRow {
        id: tr.id.to_string().into(),
        name: transition_name(tr).into(),
        known: def.is_some(),
        resettable: reset_command(seq, tr).is_some(),
        duration: tr.duration.min(limit).as_secs_f64() as f32,
        min_duration: min.as_secs_f64() as f32,
        max_duration: limit.as_secs_f64() as f32,
        limit: tf("tr.limit", &[("t", &fmt_secs(limit))]).into(),
        params: crate::effects_ui::reuse_param_rows(old, params),
    }
}

/// One parameter row of a transition (prop `p:<key>`): a number — a 0..1
/// one shown in % — or a choice by [`crate::effects_ui::choice_kind`];
/// a missing or invalid value shows the default, as the recipe reads it.
pub fn cut_param_row(tr: &Transition, def: &RecipeDef, p: &ParamDef) -> FxParamRow {
    let mut row = FxParamRow { key: p.key.into(), prop: format!("p:{}", p.key).into(), label: t(&p.label_key()).into(), ..Default::default() };
    match p.kind {
        ParamKind::Number { min, max, default } => {
            row.kind = 0;
            row.value = tr.params.get(p.key).and_then(ParamValue::as_number).filter(|v| v.is_finite()).unwrap_or(default).clamp(min, max) as f32;
            (row.minimum, row.maximum, row.default_value) = (min as f32, max as f32, default as f32);
            row.percent = min == 0.0 && max == 1.0;
        }
        ParamKind::Choice { options, default } => {
            row.kind = crate::effects_ui::choice_kind(Some(def), p.key, options);
            let current = match tr.params.get(p.key) {
                Some(ParamValue::Choice(c)) => options.iter().copied().find(|o| *o == c.as_str()).unwrap_or(default),
                _ => default,
            };
            row.current = options.iter().position(|o| *o == current).map_or(-1, |i| i as i32);
            row.options = ModelRc::new(VecModel::from(options.iter().map(|o| SharedString::from(*o)).collect::<Vec<_>>()));
            row.tips = ModelRc::new(VecModel::from(options.iter().map(|o| SharedString::from(t(&def.option_name_key(p.key, o)))).collect::<Vec<_>>()));
        }
        ParamKind::Point | ParamKind::Moment => {}
    }
    row
}

/// The length a slider value gives: whole frames, at least two.
pub fn duration_of(secs: f64, rate: FrameRate) -> Time {
    let s = if secs.is_finite() { secs.max(0.0) } else { 0.0 };
    rate.snap(Time::from_secs_f64(s)).max(rate.frame_to_time(2))
}

/// A slider's live value on `tr` (`LiveEdit` rolls it back): the length or
/// the number, as its commit would set it; a choice has no slider.
pub fn set_live(tr: &mut Transition, prop: &str, v: f64, rate: FrameRate) {
    if prop == "duration" {
        tr.duration = duration_of(v, rate);
        return;
    }
    let number = prop.strip_prefix("p:").and_then(|key| Some((key, kadr_effects::transition_definition(tr)?.param(key)?.kind)));
    if let Some((key, ParamKind::Number { min, max, .. })) = number {
        tr.params.insert(key.to_string(), ParamValue::Number(v.clamp(min, max)));
    }
}

/// A slider's release (`duration` or `p:<key>` at `v`): one
/// `UpdateTransition` «Изменение перехода: Шторка», `None` when it changes
/// nothing — also the length shown (clamped to the clips) committed as is.
pub fn commit_command(seq: &Sequence, tr: &Transition, prop: &str, v: f64) -> Option<(String, EditCommand)> {
    let def = kadr_effects::transition_definition(tr)?;
    let cmd = if prop == "duration" {
        let d = duration_of(v, seq.frame_rate);
        let shown = tr.duration.min(seq.transition_limit(tr).unwrap_or(tr.duration));
        if d == tr.duration || d == duration_of(shown.as_secs_f64(), seq.frame_rate) {
            return None;
        }
        EditCommand::UpdateTransition { id: tr.id, params: BTreeMap::new(), duration: Some(d) }
    } else {
        let key = prop.strip_prefix("p:")?;
        let ParamKind::Number { min, max, .. } = def.param(key)?.kind else { return None };
        let value = ParamValue::Number(v.clamp(min, max));
        if tr.params.get(key) == Some(&value) {
            return None;
        }
        EditCommand::UpdateTransition { id: tr.id, params: BTreeMap::from([(key.to_string(), value)]), duration: None }
    };
    Some((tf("cmd.update_transition_named", &[("name", &transition_name(tr))]), cmd))
}

/// «Сбросить»: the recipe's defaults and a new transition's length; `None`
/// when it is so already, judged by what the recipe reads (R14).
pub fn reset_command(seq: &Sequence, tr: &Transition) -> Option<EditCommand> {
    let def = kadr_effects::transition_definition(tr)?;
    let defaults = kadr_effects::transition_params(def);
    let duration = kadr_effects::default_duration(seq.frame_rate);
    let same = Params::resolve(def.params, &tr.params, |_| None) == Params::resolve(def.params, &defaults, |_| None);
    if same && tr.duration == duration {
        return None;
    }
    Some(EditCommand::UpdateTransition { id: tr.id, params: defaults, duration: Some(duration) })
}

/// A block action (`reset`, `remove`, `choice:<key>:<index>`): the label
/// and the command of one undo entry; `None` when nothing would change. A
/// transition from a newer Kadr is only removed.
pub fn action_command(seq: &Sequence, tr: &Transition, action: &str) -> Option<(String, EditCommand)> {
    if action == "remove" {
        return Some((t("cmd.remove_transition"), EditCommand::RemoveTransition(tr.id)));
    }
    let def = kadr_effects::transition_definition(tr)?;
    let name = transition_name(tr);
    if action == "reset" {
        return Some((tf("cmd.reset_transition_named", &[("name", &name)]), reset_command(seq, tr)?));
    }
    let (key, index) = action.strip_prefix("choice:")?.rsplit_once(':')?;
    let ParamKind::Choice { options, default } = def.param(key)?.kind else { return None };
    let pick = *options.get(index.parse::<usize>().ok()?)?;
    let current = match tr.params.get(key) {
        Some(ParamValue::Choice(c)) => options.iter().copied().find(|o| *o == c.as_str()).unwrap_or(default),
        _ => default,
    };
    if pick == current {
        return None;
    }
    let params = BTreeMap::from([(key.to_string(), ParamValue::Choice(pick.to_string()))]);
    Some((tf("cmd.update_transition_named", &[("name", &name)]), EditCommand::UpdateTransition { id: tr.id, params, duration: None }))
}
```

and in `impl App`:

```rust
    /// A transition block's action (spec §6.2): one undo entry, none when nothing would change.
    pub fn tr_action(&mut self, action: &str) {
        let Some(id) = self.tl.transition else { return };
        let seq = self.project.sequence();
        let Some((label, cmd)) = seq.transition(id).and_then(|tr| action_command(seq, tr, action)) else { return };
        self.execute(EditCommand::Batch { label, commands: vec![cmd] });
    }

    /// A transition slider moves: the value goes live into the transition
    /// (the preview and the pill follow); history untouched.
    pub fn tr_live(&mut self, prop: &str, v: f32) {
        let Some(id) = self.tl.transition else { return };
        let seq = self.project.sequence();
        let locked = seq.transition(id).is_none_or(|tr| seq.locate_clip(tr.to).is_some_and(|(ti, _)| seq.tracks[ti].locked));
        if locked {
            return;
        }
        let rate = seq.frame_rate;
        self.live_edit.arm_transition(self.project.sequence_mut(), id);
        if let Some(tr) = self.project.sequence_mut().transitions.iter_mut().find(|t| t.id == id) {
            set_live(tr, prop, v as f64, rate);
        }
        self.preview.invalidate();
        self.refresh_inspector();
        self.refresh_timeline();
        self.request_frame();
    }

    /// The release: the live value rolled back, one `UpdateTransition`
    /// recorded — none when nothing changed (the block shows the
    /// transition as it is, like `inspector_commit`).
    pub fn tr_commit(&mut self, prop: &str, v: f32) {
        self.live_edit.roll_back(self.project.sequence_mut());
        let seq = self.project.sequence();
        let cmd = self.tl.transition.and_then(|id| seq.transition(id)).and_then(|tr| commit_command(seq, tr, prop, v as f64));
        let done = cmd.is_some_and(|(label, cmd)| self.execute(EditCommand::Batch { label, commands: vec![cmd] }));
        if !done {
            self.show_rolled_back();
        }
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `LiveEdit` and the inspector refresh** — `apps/editor/src/inspector.rs`: replace `pub struct LiveEdit(Option<(ClipId, Clip)>);` and its impl with

```rust
#[derive(Default)]
pub struct LiveEdit {
    clip: Option<(ClipId, Clip)>,
    /// A transition slider's drag: the transition as recorded.
    transition: Option<kadr_project::Transition>,
}

impl LiveEdit {
    /// Before a live change of `id`: remembers its recorded state once per
    /// drag. An abandoned drag of something else is rolled back first.
    pub fn arm(&mut self, seq: &mut Sequence, id: ClipId) {
        if self.transition.is_some() || self.clip.as_ref().is_some_and(|(sid, _)| *sid != id) {
            self.roll_back(seq);
        }
        if self.clip.is_none() {
            self.clip = seq.clip(id).map(|c| (id, c.clone()));
        }
    }

    /// [`LiveEdit::arm`] for a transition slider (Длительность, Мягкость).
    pub fn arm_transition(&mut self, seq: &mut Sequence, id: kadr_core::TransitionId) {
        if self.clip.is_some() || self.transition.as_ref().is_some_and(|t| t.id != id) {
            self.roll_back(seq);
        }
        if self.transition.is_none() {
            self.transition = seq.transition(id).cloned();
        }
    }

    /// The recorded state of `id` while a live change of it is armed — a
    /// drag that recomputes its preview from the start (timeline diamonds).
    pub fn recorded(&self, id: ClipId) -> Option<&Clip> {
        self.clip.as_ref().filter(|(sid, _)| *sid == id).map(|(_, c)| c)
    }

    /// Restores the recorded state and disarms; `true` when something was restored.
    pub fn roll_back(&mut self, seq: &mut Sequence) -> bool {
        let mut restored = false;
        if let Some((id, orig)) = self.clip.take()
            && let Some((ti, ci)) = seq.locate_clip(id)
        {
            seq.tracks[ti].clips[ci] = orig;
            restored = true;
        }
        if let Some(orig) = self.transition.take()
            && let Some(slot) = seq.transitions.iter_mut().find(|t| t.id == orig.id)
        {
            *slot = orig;
            restored = true;
        }
        restored
    }

    /// Drops the drag without touching the project (another one was loaded).
    pub fn forget(&mut self) {
        self.clip = None;
        self.transition = None;
    }
}
```

and at the end of the file:

```slint
// The selected transition (effects spec §6.2, D8).
export struct TransitionRow {
    id: string,
    name: string,
    // false: a recipe or version from a newer Kadr — kept, never run: only removed.
    known: bool,
    // «Сбросить» would change something.
    resettable: bool,
    // Seconds: the length as drawn, its range (two frames … the shorter neighbour) and that limit's text.
    duration: float,
    min-duration: float,
    max-duration: float,
    limit: string,
    params: [FxParamRow],
}

// A transition's block: a header — its name, ⋯ with Сбросить / Удалить —
// «Длительность» and its recipe's parameters. Not animatable (D11).
export component TransitionBlock inherits VerticalLayout {
    in property <TransitionRow> tr;
    callback action(string /* reset | remove | choice:<key>:<i> */);
    callback live(string /* duration | p:<key> */, float);
    callback commit(string, float);
    spacing: 2px;
    menu := ContextMenuArea {
        Menu {
            MenuItem { title: Tr.t("fx.menu.reset"); enabled: root.tr.known && root.tr.resettable; activated => { root.action("reset"); } }
            MenuItem { title: Tr.t("fx.menu.remove"); activated => { root.action("remove"); } }
        }
        // As `FxBlock`'s header: the layout gives the menu area its size.
        VerticalLayout {
            Rectangle {
                border-radius: Theme.radius;
                background: Theme.bg2;
                HorizontalLayout {
                    height: 30px;
                    padding-left: 8px;
                    padding-right: 3px;
                    spacing: 6px;
                    Text { text: root.tr.name; font-size: Theme.font; font-weight: 600; color: root.tr.known ? Theme.text : Theme.text-faint; vertical-alignment: center; overflow: elide; horizontal-stretch: 1; }
                    VerticalLayout {
                        alignment: center;
                        more := IconButton {
                            icon: Icons.more;
                            filled: true;
                            size: 24px;
                            icon-size: 16px;
                            tooltip: Tr.t("tr.menu.tip");
                            tooltip-right: true;
                            clicked => { menu.show({ x: more.absolute-position.x - menu.absolute-position.x, y: more.absolute-position.y - menu.absolute-position.y + more.height }); }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    if !root.tr.known: Text { text: Tr.t("fx.unavailable"); font-size: Theme.font-xs; color: Theme.text-faint; wrap: word-wrap; }
    if root.tr.known: PropSlider {
        label: Tr.t("tr.duration");
        value: root.tr.duration;
        minimum: root.tr.min-duration;
        maximum: root.tr.max-duration;
        default-value: 0.5;
        decimals: 2;
        unit: " " + Tr.t("unit.sec_symbol");
        changed(v) => { root.live("duration", v); }
        committed(v) => { root.commit("duration", v); }
    }
    if root.tr.known: HorizontalLayout {
        padding-left: 92px;
        Text { text: root.tr.limit; font-size: Theme.font-xs; color: Theme.text-faint; wrap: word-wrap; }
    }
    for p in root.tr.params: VerticalLayout {
        if p.kind == 0: PropSlider {
            label: p.label; value: p.value; minimum: p.minimum; maximum: p.maximum; default-value: p.default-value;
            display-scale: p.percent ? 100 : 1; decimals: p.percent ? 0 : 2; unit: p.percent ? "%" : "";
            changed(v) => { root.live(p.prop, v); }
            committed(v) => { root.commit(p.prop, v); }
        }
        if p.kind == 1: HorizontalLayout {
            height: 30px;
            vertical-stretch: 0;
            spacing: 8px;
            Text { width: 84px; text: p.label; font-size: Theme.font-sm; color: Theme.text-dim; vertical-alignment: center; overflow: elide; }
            VerticalLayout { alignment: center; ChoiceSegments { options: p.options; tips: p.tips; current: p.current; selected(i) => { root.action("choice:" + p.key + ":" + i); } } }
            Rectangle { horizontal-stretch: 1; }
        }
        if p.kind == 4: HorizontalLayout {
            height: 30px;
            vertical-stretch: 0;
            spacing: 8px;
            Text { width: 84px; text: p.label; font-size: Theme.font-sm; color: Theme.text-dim; vertical-alignment: center; overflow: elide; }
            VerticalLayout { alignment: center; horizontal-stretch: 1; ChoiceList { names: p.tips; current: p.current; selected(i) => { root.action("choice:" + p.key + ":" + i); } } }
        }
        if p.kind == 5: HorizontalLayout {
            height: 30px;
            vertical-stretch: 0;
            spacing: 8px;
            Text { width: 84px; text: p.label; font-size: Theme.font-sm; color: Theme.text-dim; vertical-alignment: center; overflow: elide; }
            VerticalLayout { alignment: center; Segmented { options: p.tips; current: p.current; selected(i) => { if (i != p.current) { root.action("choice:" + p.key + ":" + i); } } } }
            Rectangle { horizontal-stretch: 1; }
        }
    }
}
```

```rust
            let overlays = def.compile_clip(&kadr_effects::Params::resolve(def.params, &Default::default(), |_| None), &ctx).overlays;

            add((l, i));

            // Световая волна (a dissolve under its band in Screen), built as the evaluator builds them.
            let def = kadr_effects::recipe(if row == Row::TransitionPush { "smooth_push" } else { "light_sweep" }).expect("an E3 recipe");
            let params = kadr_effects::Params::resolve(def.params, &kadr_effects::transition_params(def), |_| None);
            let build = def.compile_cut(&params, &kadr_effects::TransitionCtx { progress: 0.5, canvas, output: canvas, seed: 7 }).expect("a cut recipe");
            let (from, from_in) = media_layer(1, &a.video, fill);
            let (to, to_in) = media_layer(2, &a.video2, fill);
            let from = kadr_timeline::scene::shift_side(vec![from], build.from_shift, &build.from_effects, canvas);
            let to = kadr_timeline::scene::shift_side(vec![to], build.to_shift, &build.to_effects, canvas);
            let mut stack = kadr_timeline::scene::transition_stack(LayerId(3), build, from, to, canvas).into_iter();
            add((stack.next().expect("the mix"), LayerInput::Transition { from: vec![from_in], to: vec![to_in] }));

        }
```

Tests: in `bench_scenes_have_the_advertised_layers` the table iterates the old rows only (leave it); add

```rust
    /// The E3 rows hold what they are named after: the leak's two Screen
    /// generators above a clip that is still copied; the push's `Over` of
    /// two shifted pictures; the sweep's band above its dissolve.
    #[test]
    fn the_e3_rows_hold_what_they_are_named_after() {
        let a = Assets::new(64, 36, 16);
        let (leak, inputs) = build(Row::OneLayerLeak, &a);
        let kinds: Vec<(&str, BlendMode)> = leak
            .layers
            .iter()
            .map(|l| {
                let kind = match &l.content {
                    LayerContent::Media { .. } => "media",
                    LayerContent::Generator(Generator::Noise(_)) => "noise",
                    LayerContent::Generator(Generator::Gradient(_)) => "gradient",
                    _ => "other",
                };
                (kind, l.blend)
            })
            .collect();
        assert_eq!(kinds, [("media", BlendMode::Normal), ("noise", BlendMode::Screen), ("gradient", BlendMode::Screen)]);
        let mut out = vec![0u8; 64 * 36 * 4];
        let stats = CpuRenderer::new().render(&PreparedFrame { scene: &leak, inputs: &inputs }, &mut RenderTarget::Cpu(CpuTarget::packed(64, 36, &mut out))).unwrap();
        assert_eq!((stats.layers_drawn, stats.fast_paths), (3, 1), "the clip itself is still the plain copy");

        let (push, inputs) = build(Row::TransitionPush, &a);
        assert_eq!((push.layers.len(), inputs.layers.len()), (1, 1));
        let LayerContent::Transition(t) = &push.layers[0].content else { panic!("a transition layer") };
        assert_eq!(t.op, TransitionOp::Over);
        let half = a.size.w as f32 / 2.0;
        assert!(t.from[0].placement.position.x < half && t.to[0].placement.position.x > half, "left: a leaves, b follows from the right");

        let (sweep, inputs) = build(Row::TransitionSweep, &a);
        assert_eq!((sweep.layers.len(), inputs.layers.len()), (2, 2));
        let LayerContent::Transition(t) = &sweep.layers[0].content else { panic!("a transition layer") };
        assert_eq!(t.op, TransitionOp::Dissolve);
        assert!(matches!(sweep.layers[1].content, LayerContent::Generator(Generator::Gradient(_))) && sweep.layers[1].blend == BlendMode::Screen);
        assert!(matches!(inputs.layers[1], LayerInput::None));
    }
```
