- [ ] **Step 2: The scene row** — in `scene_bench.rs` (module doc: "…five rows — …, the colour row: the same with a Look (Винтаж) and a Vignette on every clip, and the transitions row: ten clips on one track with a transition on each cut and a Light Leak on every clip."):

```rust
/// Ten 6 s clips on V1 with a 2 s transition on each of the nine cuts —
/// the five recipes in turn — and a Light Leak on every clip: a frame in a
/// transition mixes two clips with two overlays each.
fn transitions_project() -> Project {
    let mut p = Project::new("bench-transitions");
    let a = asset(MediaKind::Video, 1920, 1080);
    p.assets.push(a.clone());
    let leak = kadr_effects::recipe("light_leak").expect("an E3 recipe");
    let mut ids = vec![];
    for i in 0..10 {
        let at = Time::from_secs(6 * i);
        // The same 6 s of the source each time: no clip continues another.
        let mut c = Clip::new(a.id, "c", TimeRange::new(Time::ZERO, Time::from_secs(6)), at);
        let e = kadr_effects::fresh(leak, &c, at + Time::from_secs(3));
        c.effects.push(e);
        ids.push(c.id);
        p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips.push(c);
    }
    let recipes = ["clean_fade", "fade_through_color", "smooth_push", "light_sweep", "wipe"];
    for (i, pair) in ids.windows(2).enumerate() {
        let def = kadr_effects::recipe(recipes[i % recipes.len()]).expect("an E3 recipe");
        p.sequence_mut().transitions.push(Transition::new(def.id, def.version, kadr_effects::transition_params(def), pair[0], pair[1], Time::from_secs(2)));
    }
    p
}
```

and the test:

```rust
    /// The transitions row mixes at every cut, each side with its leak, and
    /// shows one clip with its leak between the cuts.
    #[test]
    fn transitions_bench_scene_mixes_every_cut_with_the_leaks() {
        let p = transitions_project();
        let seq = p.sequence();
        assert_eq!((seq.tracks[0].clips.len(), seq.transitions.len()), (10, 9));
        let out = OutputSpec::new(SizeU::new(1920, 1080), RenderQuality::PreviewHigh);
        for (i, tr) in seq.transitions.iter().enumerate() {
            let (_, cut) = seq.transition_cut(tr).expect("on its cut");
            let scene = evaluate(&p, seq, cut, &out);
            let LayerContent::Transition(t) = &scene.layers[0].content else { panic!("cut {i}: a transition") };
            assert_eq!((t.from.len(), t.to.len()), (3, 3), "cut {i}: each clip with its noise and glow");
            assert_eq!(scene.layers.len(), if tr.recipe == "light_sweep" { 2 } else { 1 }, "cut {i}: the sweep's band above");
        }
        assert_eq!(evaluate(&p, seq, Time::from_secs(3), &out).layers.len(), 3, "between cuts: the clip, its noise and its glow");
    }
```

Record each item in §6 of the perf record, with `get_frame` frames where visual:
  1. **A project saved before E3 looks the same** (Review Focus 1): build the E2 editor in its own worktree and target dir (`git worktree add ../kadr-e2 e448971`, `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=…/target-e2 cargo build --release -p kadr-editor`), start it headless, place four 3 s pieces of `Tribal_Samba` on V1 (three cuts), `edit add_transition {at_ms, kind}` with `cross_dissolve`, `dip_to_black`, `wipe` on the three cuts, then a fourth transition made stale (add it on a fourth cut, then move that cut's incoming clip 2 s right); `get_frame` at each transition's 25 / 50 / 75 %; `project save_as %TEMP%\kadr-e3\legacy.kadr`; close it and remove the worktree. In the E3 build: `project open` that file → the same `get_frame`s byte-identical; `get_state` lists three transitions — `clean_fade {style: clean}`, `fade_through_color {color: black}`, `wipe {direction: left, softness: 0}` with their `from` / `to` — and not the stale one; the timeline shows three pills «Растворение», «Через цвет», «Шторка»; `save_as` to `legacy-e3.kadr` writes `recipe` / `from` / `to` and no `kind`, `track` or `at` (read the JSON); reopening it gives the same frames; `legacy.kadr` itself is unchanged (its hash before and after).
  2. **Cards**: «Переходы» shows five cards whose thumbnails loop the selected clip and its neighbour through the transition; «Свет» shows Виньетка and Засветка; «Все» shows 18; names in RU and EN; search «сдвиг» / «push» finds Сдвиг.
  3. **Drag onto a cut** (Review Focus 3, `drag_at`): over a cut on a video row the cut highlights within ±8 px; a drop there → one undo entry «Переход: Сдвиг» (EN «Transition: Smooth Push»), the pill named on the cut, the transition selected and its block in the inspector; drops 20 px from a cut on the clip body, on a gap, on A1 and below the tracks apply nothing and show the reason; a locked V1 refuses; a card dropped on a cut that has a transition replaces it keeping its length.
  4. **Click a card**: a clip selected and the playhead near its end → the transition on that clip's out cut; a clip with no neighbour meeting it → the hint «Нет склейки рядом с плейхедом на дорожке выделенного клипа»; with a transition selected the click replaces it; the same card again → nothing in the history. The context-menu item «Наплыв на ближайшей склейке» puts a 0,5 s Растворение and its toast says so.
  5. **Timeline**: a click on a pill selects it (the clip selection clears, and the reverse); Delete (or the lane menu's «Удалить» when the key cannot be sent) → one undo entry «Удаление перехода»; Esc deselects; the hover tooltip «Шторка · 0,5 с»; at a narrow zoom the pill shows its cross; fade handles above and keyframe diamonds below a pill still take the press; trim edges beside it still trim.
  6. **Inspector**: «ПЕРЕХОД» with the name, «Длительность» from two frames to the shorter clip with «Не длиннее …»; a drag of it is one undo entry «Изменение перехода: Сдвиг» and the pill follows live; directions as icons, Цвет / Стиль as text segments, Мягкость in %; «Сбросить» goes back to 0,5 s and the defaults (disabled when already so); «Удалить». A hand-edited project with `"recipe": "iris"` opens with «Недоступен» on the pill and in the block (only «Удалить»), cuts hard, and saves the entry unchanged.
  7. **Clip commands** (Review Focus 2): split `from` in its middle — the pill stays on the cut (now the right half's), the frames around the cut unchanged; split `to` — stays; trim `to`'s start right leaving a gap — the pill goes; undo — it returns; ripple-delete `from` — gone, undo — back; move `to` away and back — gone after the first move, not recreated by the second; delete a range across the cut — gone; after each, `get_state` shows no transition between clips that do not meet.
  8. **Light Leak** (Review Focus 4): on `Tribal_Samba` at «Обычно» — a warm glow from the corner with soft blotches, the picture readable through it; Розовый / Холодный and variants 1 / 2 / 3 differ visibly; Сила 0 shows none; on the vertical `Brazil.mp4` in a 16:9 sequence the leak stays on the picture and the side bars stay black; on a clip scaled to 0.4 in a corner (PiP) only the PiP glows; a fade-out takes the leak with it; «До» (`\`) shows no leak; on the alpha logo the glow covers its whole rectangle (refinement 16) — show the owner and record the verdict.
  9. **Transitions on real material**: each recipe on a cut between `Tribal_Samba` and `Neymar(3-10).mp4` at 25 / 50 / 75 % (`get_frame`), shown to the owner: Плёнка's lift subtle, Через цвет black and white, Сдвиг edge to edge without a line at the seam (crop the frame at the seam) in a 1080p and in a vertical sequence, Световая волна's three colours and four directions, Шторка at Мягкость 0 and 100 %. The owner approves or asks for changes; tuning happens in the constants of `leak.rs` / `transition.rs` only, then re-bless (`KADR_UPDATE_GOLDEN=1 cargo test -p kadr-render --test golden light_leaks`, `KADR_UPDATE_GOLDEN=1 cargo test -p kadr-project-scenes --test transitions_pixels e3_transitions`, look at every PNG), update the tests that pin a moved constant, rerun `cargo test -p kadr-effects -p kadr-render -p kadr-timeline -p kadr-project-scenes`, commit «effects E3: Light Leak and transitions tuned with the owner».
  10. **MCP / AI**: `list_effects` shows the five transitions with target `cut`; `edit add_transition {at_ms, recipe: "light_sweep", params: {direction: "up"}, duration_ms: 800}` applies; `{kind: "wipe"}` gives `wipe` left, hard; an `at_ms` 1 s from any cut is rejected with the reason; `set_transition {id, duration_ms: 20}` is rejected (under two frames); `set_transition {id, params: {color: "cold"}}` and `remove_transition {id}` work and each is one undo entry; `select {transition}` selects it and `get_state` shows `selected_transition`; the assistant's «поставь световую волну на склейку на 6 секунде» proposes `add_transition light_sweep`, applying it is one undo entry.
  11. **Small and large window**: at 1024 × 640 and maximized the cards, pills, the inspector block and its segments are unclipped, in RU and EN.
  12. **Performance** (`get_perf`, reset before each): playback of a 1080p sequence of `Tribal_Samba` pieces with a Сдвиг, a Световая волна and a Light Leak on every piece against the same without — frame time p90 and dropped frames; the transition cards' thumbnails render while paused and never during playback. These numbers go into the verdict of Step 4.
  13. **Export**: a 6 s export across a Сдвиг and a Световая волна; their middle frames (extracted with ffmpeg) look like `get_frame` at those times.
